中文摘要翻译: 随着各种预训练视觉与语言模型的出现，计算机视觉正从窄域识别转向开放域识别。构建更强大且通用的关键点检测模型以支持多样化任务，在该领域变得愈发重要。为此，我们首先提出了一个大规模统一关键点数据集MegaKPT。该数据集由来自29个现有数据集的超过130万个不同物体实例组成，并拥有高质量的统一标注及关键点文本描述。基于MegaKPT，我们开发了GKDT——一个简单、灵活且强大的基于DINOv3的Transformer模型，用于通用关键点检测。我们的GKDT支持视觉提示、文本提示或两者兼用。为增强模型训练，我们还提出了一套实用的策略，如多模态提示训练和动态重要性采样。通过在22个包含已见或未见物体的测试集上进行测试，我们的单一GKDT模型在检测广泛类别的关键点方面展现出强大的性能和泛化能力，多数类别的PCK@0.1精度超过90%，为现实问题提供了很高的实际应用价值。该数据集、模型和代码将在 https://github.com/AlanLuSun/General-Keypoint-Detection 发布。

</details>


### [91] [Decoupled Guidance: Disentangling Subject and Context Pathways in Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00766)
*Seongmin Kim,Kyucheol Shin,Heesun Jung,Jinseo Kim,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦引导（DeGu）框架，通过独立路由主体身份与场景上下文到两个引导流，并引入空间混合机制动态融合，解决文本到图像个性化中因条件纠缠导致的保真度-可编辑性权衡问题，适用于多种现有方法及扩散Transformer骨干网络。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像个性化方法将主体身份（保真度）与场景上下文（可编辑性）通过相同的条件路径编码，导致二者竞争注意力图资源，产生条件纠缠现象，进而引发保真度-可编辑性权衡。

Method: 提出解耦引导（DeGu）框架：1）将主体身份与场景上下文分别路由至两个独立的引导流；2）引入空间混合机制动态融合两个引导流，确保每个引导流在其语义相关区域运行而不互相干扰；3）作为即插即用框架，无需修改基础骨干模型即可应用于现有个性化方法。

Result: DeGu可一致地提升多种现有个性化方法的整体性能，支持推理时控制保真度-可编辑性平衡，并适用于包括流匹配扩散Transformer（DiTs）在内的多种方法与骨干网络。

Conclusion: 通过解耦条件路径并动态融合，DeGu有效解决了条件纠缠问题，打破了保真度与可编辑性之间的固有权衡，为个性化生成提供了更灵活且通用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image personalization aims to generate a user-provided subject in novel scenes described by text. However, most existing methods encode subject identity (fidelity) and context (editability) through the same conditioning pathway, forcing the two to compete for attention-map resources. We refer to this phenomenon as conditioning entanglement and show that it induces a fidelity-editability trade-off. We further provide causal evidence by replacing the target subject token with a generic subject token, which produces shifts in attention allocation and corresponding changes in context adherence. To this end, we propose Decoupled Guidance (DeGu), a plug-and-play framework that routes subject identity and scene context through two independent guidance streams. We further introduce a spatial mixing mechanism that dynamically fuses these streams, ensuring each operates within its semantically relevant region without interference. Furthermore, DeGu can be readily applied to existing personalization methods without modifying the underlying backbone models, consistently improving the overall personalization performance while enabling inference-time control over the fidelity-editability balance, across diverse methods and backbones, including flow-matching Diffusion Transformers (DiTs).

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像个性化旨在根据文本描述，在 novel 场景中生成用户提供的目标主体。然而，现有大多数方法通过相同的条件路径编码主体身份（保真度）与场景上下文（可编辑性），迫使二者竞争注意力图资源。我们将此现象称为条件纠缠，并证明它导致了保真度-可编辑性权衡。我们进一步通过将目标主体词替换为通用主体词提供因果证据，发现该替换会引起注意力分配的偏移以及上下文遵循度相应的变化。为此，我们提出解耦引导（DeGu），一种即插即用框架，将主体身份与场景上下文通过两个独立的引导流路由。我们还引入空间混合机制动态融合这两个流，确保每个流在其语义相关区域内运行而不受干扰。此外，DeGu可方便地应用于现有个性化方法而无需修改底层骨干模型，一致地提升整体个性化性能，同时支持在推理时控制保真度-可编辑性平衡，适用于多种方法与骨干网络，包括流匹配扩散Transformer（DiTs）。

</details>


### [92] [Soft Mixture-of-Recursions: Going Deeper with Recursive Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00774)
*Sang In Lee,Jihun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Soft Mixture-of-Recursions（SoftMoR）方法，通过学习递归步间的token级混合权重，有效利用递归Transformer的中间表示，在极小参数开销下实现更深更强的视觉Transformer。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有递归Transformer主要致力于参数共享以构建紧凑模型，但简单地增加递归深度并不能可靠地提升性能，因为现有方法未能充分利用递归计算过程中产生的所有中间表示。

Method: 提出Soft Mixture-of-Recursions (SoftMoR)，学习每个token的混合权重，以可学习的灵活方式软性组合所有递归步的输出，使中间表示得到充分利用；并将其实例化为Soft Recursive Vision Transformer (SR-ViT)。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，SR-ViT-S将递归深度从1增加到4时，top-1准确率从79.83%提升至82.48%，仅增加1.7M参数；性能超越参数量大得多的DeiT-B，而参数量仅为其约27%。

Conclusion: SoftMoR通过递归提供了一条参数高效的路径，能够构建更深更强且参数利用率高的视觉Transformer。

Original Abstract: Recent recursive Transformer studies have primarily reused shared parameters across computation steps to construct compact, parameter-efficient models. In this work, we leverage recursion to build effectively deeper Transformers with stronger representational capacity. However, in Vision Transformers, simply increasing recursion depth does not reliably improve performance, as existing recursive approaches do not fully utilize the intermediate representations produced throughout recursive computation. We propose Soft Mixture-of-Recursions (SoftMoR) and its Vision Transformer instantiation, Soft Recursive Vision Transformer (SR-ViT). SoftMoR learns token-wise mixture weights to softly combine outputs from all recursion steps, allowing intermediate representations to be utilized in a learnable and flexible way. Across diverse vision tasks, SR-ViT consistently improves as recursion depth increases with minimal parameter overhead. On ImageNet-1K, increasing recursion depth from 1 to 4 improves SR-ViT-S top-1 accuracy from 79.83% to 82.48% with only 1.7M additional parameters, outperforming the substantially larger DeiT-B while using approximately 27% of its parameters. These results demonstrate that SoftMoR provides a parameter-efficient path to deeper and stronger Vision Transformers through recursion.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的递归Transformer研究主要跨计算步骤重用共享参数，以构建紧凑、参数高效的模型。在这项工作中，我们利用递归来构建有效更深且具有更强表示能力的Transformer。然而，在视觉Transformer中，简单地增加递归深度并不能可靠地提升性能，因为现有的递归方法未能充分利用递归计算过程中产生的所有中间表示。我们提出了Soft Mixture-of-Recursions (SoftMoR)及其视觉Transformer实例化——Soft Recursive Vision Transformer (SR-ViT)。SoftMoR学习token级别的混合权重，以软性组合所有递归步的输出，从而以可学习且灵活的方式利用中间表示。在多种视觉任务中，SR-ViT随着递归深度增加而持续提升性能，且参数开销极小。在ImageNet-1K上，将递归深度从1增加到4使得SR-ViT-S的top-1准确率从79.83%提升至82.48%，仅增加1.7M参数，同时以约27%的参数数量超越了参数量大得多的DeiT-B。这些结果表明，SoftMoR通过递归提供了一条参数高效的路径，用于构建更深更强且参数利用率高的视觉Transformer。

</details>


### [93] [SpiralFovea: Input-Adaptive Foveated Tokenization as a Third Lever of Resource-Adaptive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00780)
*Kyan Mahajan,Mohammad Saqlain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpiralFovea提出了一种无需参数、输入自适应的分词器，根据视觉熵动态调整token的身份、位置、尺度和数量，在输入阶段用≤78个多尺度螺旋环patch替代标准196-patch ViT网格，在细粒度分类任务上提升1.7-2.1个百分点的准确率，同时减少60%的输入token和84%的自注意力FLOPs。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基础模型的自适应推理技术大多聚焦于改变模型内部行为（如提前退出、MoE路由、KV缓存压缩等），但输入层仍然使用固定网格的分词方式，忽略了图像内容本身对token化的需求，作者认为这是一个被忽视的杠杆。

Method: 提出SpiralFovea，一种参数无关的输入自适应分词器。根据局部视觉熵检测内容驱动的热点锚点，围绕锚点构建多尺度螺旋环，生成不超过78个patch（替换标准196-patch ViT网格），且所有选择在查询任何主干参数之前完成。

Result: 在四个细粒度基准上，准确率提升1.7-2.1个百分点，输入token减少60%，每层自注意力FLOPs减少84%，吞吐量提升18-29%。在CUB-200-2011上的消融实验表明：增益大小与主干网络的全图位置先验强度呈负相关，自监督基础模型是输入自适应token化最有价值的场景。

Conclusion: 输入自适应的分词策略是一种有效的性能提升手段，尤其在自监督基础模型上价值最大；增益与模型的全图位置先验强度呈反比关系。

Original Abstract: Most adaptive-inference techniques for foundation models change what the model does - early exit, MoE routing, KV-cache compression, dynamic attention sparsity. The input that hits the backbone, however, remains a fixed-grid tokenisation indifferent to image content. We argue that this is a missed lever. We present SpiralFovea, a parameter-free, input-adaptive tokeniser in which token identity, location, scale, and count are all functions of local visual entropy and selection completes before any backbone parameter is queried. Around content-driven hotspot anchors, multi-scale spiral rings produce <= 78 patches that replace the standard 196-patch ViT grid at the input stage. Across four canonical fine-grained benchmarks, SpiralFovea yields +1.7-2.1 pp accuracy with a 60% reduction in input tokens, an 84% reduction in self-attention FLOPs at every transformer layer, and 18-29% throughput gains over the matched static tokenisation baseline. A controlled ablation on CUB-200-2011 Genus across four backbones reveals a clean diagnostic: the gain magnitude tracks inversely with the strength of the backbone's whole-image positional prior, isolating self-supervised foundation models as the regime where input-adaptive tokenisation is most valuable.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数基础模型的自适应推理技术改变的是模型内部行为——提前退出、MoE路由、KV缓存压缩、动态注意力稀疏化。然而，输入到主干网络的数据仍然是固定网格的分词方式，与图像内容无关。我们认为这是一个被忽视的杠杆。本文提出SpiralFovea，一种无需参数、输入自适应的分词器，其中token的身份、位置、尺度和数量都是局部视觉熵的函数，且所有选择在查询任何主干参数之前完成。围绕内容驱动的热点锚点，多尺度螺旋环生成不超过78个patch，在输入阶段替换标准的196-patch ViT网格。在四个经典细粒度基准上，SpiralFovea相比匹配的静态分词基线，准确率提升1.7-2.1个百分点，输入token减少60%，每个transformer层的自注意力FLOPs减少84%，吞吐量提升18-29%。在CUB-200-2011 Genus上跨越四个主干网络的控制消融实验揭示了一个清晰的诊断结论：增益大小与主干网络的全图位置先验强度呈负相关，从而将自监督基础模型确定为输入自适应分词最具价值的应用场景。

</details>


### [94] [LeVLJEPA: End-to-End Vision-Language Pretraining Without Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00784)
*Lukas Kuhn,Giuseppe Serra,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeVLJEPA 是一种完全非对比式的端到端视觉-语言预训练方法，通过跨模态预测和停止梯度目标进行学习，无需负样本、温度参数、动量编码器或师生调度，能够在大规模下稳定训练。实验表明，该方法生成的编码器在密集语义特征方面优于对比式基线，尤其在下游任务中作为冻结的视觉-语言骨干网络表现最强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练仍以对比式目标为主导，而纯视觉自监督学习已广泛采用非对比式方法。同时，视觉-语言编码器的角色已从零样本分类器转变为视觉-语言模型和密集预测系统的冻结视觉骨干，后者需要消耗完整的图像块令牌网格而非单个池化嵌入。因此，作者希望探索非对比式方法在视觉-语言预训练中的潜力。

Method: 提出 LeVLJEPA，一种完全非对比式的端到端视觉-语言预训练方法。其核心是通过跨模态预测学习，使用停止梯度目标（stop-gradient targets）和每模态分布正则化（per-modality distributional regularization），无需负样本、温度参数、动量编码器或师生调度，且能在大规模下稳定训练。

Result: 1) 作为冻结的视觉-语言模型骨干，LeVLJEPA 在 GQA、VQAv2 和 POPE 三个基准上，在两种不同的语言模型下均优于所有评估的编码器。2) 在语义分割任务上优于对比式基线。3) 在全局读出头（如线性探测）上性能与对比式方法持平。4) 非对比式预训练能有效产生密集的语义视觉特征。

Conclusion: 非对比式预训练是产生密集语义视觉特征的有效手段，LeVLJEPA 证明了在没有负样本、动量编码器等复杂机制的情况下，通过跨模态预测和分布正则化即可实现优于对比式方法的密集特征表示。

Original Abstract: Vision-language pretraining remains dominated by contrastive objectives, whereas vision-only self-supervised learning has largely adopted non-contrastive methods. At the same time, the role of vision-language encoders has shifted: they are increasingly deployed not as zero-shot classifiers but as the frozen visual backbone of vision-language models and dense prediction systems, which consume the full grid of patch tokens rather than a single pooled embedding. We introduce LeVLJEPA, the first fully non-contrastive end-to-end vision-language pretraining method. LeVLJEPA learns through cross-modal prediction with stop-gradient targets and per-modality distributional regularization, without negatives, temperature, momentum encoder, or teacher-student schedule, and trains stably at large scale. We find that the resulting encoder provides markedly stronger dense semantic features for downstream use: as a frozen vision-language-model backbone, LeVLJEPA is the strongest of the evaluated encoders across GQA, VQAv2, and POPE under two distinct language models, and outperforms contrastive baselines on semantic segmentation, while remaining on par on global readouts such as linear probing. These results establish non-contrastive pretraining as an effective means of producing dense semantic vision features.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言预训练长期以来一直由对比式目标主导，而纯视觉自监督学习则已广泛采用了非对比式方法。与此同时，视觉-语言编码器的角色也发生了转变：它们越来越多地被部署为视觉-语言模型和密集预测系统中的冻结视觉骨干，而非零样本分类器——这些系统消耗的是完整的图像块令牌网格，而非单一的池化嵌入。我们提出了 LeVLJEPA，这是第一种完全非对比式的端到端视觉-语言预训练方法。LeVLJEPA 通过带停止梯度目标的跨模态预测和每模态分布正则化进行学习，无需负样本、温度参数、动量编码器或师生调度，并且能够在大规模下稳定训练。我们发现，由此产生的编码器在下游使用中提供了明显更强的密集语义特征：作为冻结的视觉-语言模型骨干，LeVLJEPA 在 GQA、VQAv2 和 POPE 三个基准上，在两种不同的语言模型下，均是所有评估编码器中最强的；在语义分割任务上优于对比式基线，同时在全局读出头（如线性探测）上保持相当的性能。这些结果表明，非对比式预训练是产生密集语义视觉特征的有效手段。

</details>


### [95] [ClinRAG-GRAPH: Clinical-prior Retrieval-Augmented Graph Model with Domain Adversarial Learning for Breast pCR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00798)
*Yaofei Duan,Yuhao Huang,Tianyu Zhang,Yuan Gao,Luyi Han,Xin Wang,Xinyu Xie,Xinglong Liang,Chunyao Lu,Muzhen He,Patrick Pang,Yue Sun,Ning Mao,Tao Tan,Ritse Mann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClinRAG-GRAPH是一个面向乳腺癌新辅助化疗(NAC)前病理完全缓解(pCR)预测的多中心多模态框架，通过构建患者内临床先验图、双分支域对抗学习以及LLM驱动的子图检索增强生成模块，在三个中心的内部和外部测试集上分别达到0.815、0.774和0.712的AUC值，实现了鲁棒且可解释的pCR预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有乳腺癌新辅助化疗(NAC)前的病理完全缓解(pCR)预测面临三个关键挑战：跨模态建模不充分、多中心影像异质性严重、以及缺乏基于证据的可解释性，亟需一种能够融合多模态数据、跨中心鲁棒且具备临床可解释性的预测方法。

Method: 提出ClinRAG-GRAPH框架：(1) 构建患者内临床先验图(clinical-prior graph)，并采用先验引导的关系感知图卷积网络(Prior-guided Relation-aware GCN)进行结构化多模态表示学习；(2) 引入双分支域对抗学习策略，抑制与DCE-MRI协议相关的偏置同时保留pCR相关特征，提升跨中心鲁棒性；(3) 集成大语言模型(LLM)驱动的子图RAG模块，检索临床相似的历史病例并整合检索证据进行pCR推理，增强可解释性。

Result: 在大规模多中心NAC乳腺癌队列（2个公共数据集+3个内部中心）上进行验证，内部测试集AUC达0.815，两个外部测试集AUC分别为0.774和0.712，证明了跨中心的鲁棒预治疗pCR预测性能。

Conclusion: ClinRAG-GRAPH通过临床先验图构建、域对抗学习与LLM驱动的检索增强生成，有效解决了多中心多模态乳腺癌pCR预测中的跨模态融合、域偏移和可解释性难题，在多个中心上取得了鲁棒的预测性能。

Original Abstract: Neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NAC) response prediction is clinically important for treatment stratification in breast cancer. However, robust pre-treatment pathological complete response (pCR) prediction remains challenging due to insufficient cross-modal modeling, multicenter imaging heterogeneity, and weak evidence-grounded interpretability. We propose ClinRAG-GRAPH, a Clinically informed Retrieval-Augmented Generation Graph framework, for pre-treatment pCR prediction from DCE-MRI, structured clinical variables, and biopsy-derived pathological biomarkers. ClinRAG-GRAPH constructs an intra-patient clinical-prior graph and applies a prior-guided relation-aware graph convolutional network for structured multimodal representation learning. To improve cross-center robustness, we introduce a dual-branch domain-adversarial learning strategy to suppress protocol-related MRI bias while preserving pCR-relevant features. To enhance interpretability, we further incorporate large language model (LLM)-driven subgraph RAG module that retrieves clinically analogous historical cases and integrates retrieved evidence for pCR inference. We assemble a large-scale multicenter NAC breast cancer cohort for extensive validation, drawing from two public sources and three in-house centers.Results show that ClinRAG-GRAPH achieves AUCs of 0.815 on the internal test set and 0.774/0.712 on two external test sets, demonstrating robust pre-treatment pCR prediction across centers. The code is available at the anonymized https://github.com/miccai26-1181/ClinRAG-GRAPH.

中文摘要翻译: 新辅助化疗(NAC)反应预测对于乳腺癌的治疗分层具有重要的临床意义。然而，由于跨模态建模不充分、多中心影像异质性严重以及缺乏基于证据的可解释性，稳健的预治疗病理完全缓解(pCR)预测仍面临挑战。本文提出ClinRAG-GRAPH——一个临床信息增强的检索增强生成图框架，用于基于DCE-MRI、结构化临床变量和活检病理生物标志物进行预治疗pCR预测。ClinRAG-GRAPH构建了患者内临床先验图，并采用先验引导的关系感知图卷积网络进行结构化多模态表示学习。为提升跨中心鲁棒性，我们引入了双分支域对抗学习策略，以抑制与协议相关的MRI偏置同时保留pCR相关特征。为增强可解释性，我们进一步集成了大语言模型(LLM)驱动的子图RAG模块，该模块检索临床相似的历史病例并整合检索证据进行pCR推理。我们构建了大规模多中心NAC乳腺癌队列进行广泛验证，数据来源包括两个公共数据集和三个内部中心。结果表明，ClinRAG-GRAPH在内部测试集上达到0.815的AUC，在两个外部测试集上分别达到0.774和0.712的AUC，展示了跨中心的稳健预治疗pCR预测性能。代码已在匿名化的https://github.com/miccai26-1181/ClinRAG-GRAPH公开。

</details>


### [96] [Spotted: Location-informed Reidentification of Hyenas and Leopards in Camera Trap Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00804)
*Halil Sina Kelebek,Julia Hindel,Kobus Hoffman,Lauren Hoffman,Andrew Loveridge,Bob Mandinyenya,Kudakwashe Ncube,Justin Seymour-Smith,Andrea Sibanda,Abhinav Valada,Matthew Wijers,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spotted，一种结合时空先验与人工反馈的动物重识别框架，通过融合视觉相似性与基于相机位置的可行性得分，显著减少专家审核工作量，并在三种斑鬣狗和豹子数据集上取得优于基线的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动物重识别方法过度依赖视觉特征，忽略了野外研究中可获得的辅助信息（如时间戳和相机位置），且性能不足以实现全自动化，通常需要专家人工审核候选匹配结果。

Method: (i) 基于两次检测间所需最低移动速度计算与图像模型无关的可行性得分；(ii) 利用该得分作为伪监督信号，在冻结的视觉基础模型上训练轻量级头部网络；(iii) 融合改进后的视觉相似性与时空可行性得到鲁棒的成对匹配得分；并引入主动对采样策略优先处理高不确定性预测以加速标注。

Result: 在三个数据集上，top-5识别准确率分别比最优基线提升9pp、2pp和9pp；人工反馈策略在保持同等正匹配数量的情况下，减少了多达69pp的查询比较次数。

Conclusion: 融合时空先验信息可有效减少动物重识别中所需的人工审核量，同时提升识别准确率，尤其适用于低质量、不平衡的相机陷阱调查数据。

Original Abstract: Animal re-identification (ReID) in camera-trap surveys remains challenging due to low image quality, strong variation in illumination and viewpoint, and highly imbalanced numbers of observations per individual. As a result, current ReID performance is often insufficient for fully automated use, and practical workflows typically depend on expert review of algorithmically proposed candidate matches. Moreover, most existing approaches focus almost exclusively on visual cues and overlook auxiliary information routinely available in field studies, such as image timestamps and camera-trap locations. We introduce Spotted, a location-informed, human-in-the-loop animal ReID framework that integrates visual similarity with spatio-temporal feasibility priors derived from camera locations, thereby reducing the amount of required expert review. Our method (i) computes an image-model-agnostic feasibility score based on the minimum travel speed required for two detections to correspond to the same individual, (ii) uses these feasibility cues as pseudo-supervision to train a lightweight head on top of a frozen visual foundation model, and (iii) fuses adapted visual similarity with spatio-temporal feasibility to obtain a robust pairwise matching score. We additionally integrate an active pair sampling strategy to accelerate annotation by initially prioritizing uncertain predictions. We evaluate Spotted on three challenging camera-trap ReID datasets comprised of spotted hyenas and leopards, which we release as part of this work. Our model improves average top-5 identification accuracy by 9pp, 2pp and 9pp over the best baseline on our LeopardID102, SpottedHyenaID109 and SpottedHyenaID415 datasets, respectively. Further, we show that our human-in-the-loop strategy reduces the number of queried comparisons by up to 69pp while achieving equivalent positive matches.

中文摘要翻译: 动物重识别在相机陷阱调查中仍然具有挑战性，主要原因包括图像质量低、光照和视角变化大、以及每个个体的观测数量极度不平衡。因此，当前重识别性能往往不足以支持全自动化使用，实际工作流程通常依赖专家对算法提出的候选匹配结果进行审核。此外，现有方法大多只关注视觉线索，忽略了野外研究中可常规获得的辅助信息，如图像时间戳和相机陷阱位置。我们提出Spotted，一种融入位置信息的人工在环动物重识别框架，它将视觉相似性与基于相机位置推导的时空可行性先验相结合，从而减少所需的专家审核量。我们的方法：(i) 基于两次检测之间同一所需的最短移动速度，计算与图像模型无关的可行性得分；(ii) 利用这些可行性线索作为伪监督信号，在冻结的视觉基础模型之上训练一个轻量级头部网络；(iii) 将改进后的视觉相似性与时空可行性融合，得到鲁棒的成对匹配得分。我们还集成了主动对采样策略，通过优先处理高不确定性的预测来加速标注过程。我们在三个具有挑战性的相机陷阱重识别数据集上评估了Spotted，这些数据集包含斑鬣狗和豹子，作为本工作的一部分已公开发布。我们的模型在LeopardID102、SpottedHyenaID109和SpottedHyenaID415数据集上，平均top-5识别准确率分别比最优基线提升9个百分点、2个百分点和9个百分点。此外，我们的人工在环策略在达到同等正匹配数量的同时，减少了多达69个百分点的查询比较次数。

</details>


### [97] [Towards High-Resolution Visual Perception via Hierarchical Entity Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00816)
*Ziyu Ma,Shidong Yang,Yuxiang Ji,Yiming Hu,Tongwen Huang,Yong Wang,Jianfei Cai,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HEE，一种无需训练且与模型无关的框架，通过动态查询引导的实体探索将静态图像理解转化为层次化感知过程，在HR图像理解任务上超越现有训练免费方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理高分辨率图像时，细粒度细节容易丢失；训练依赖方法或固定启发式分割区域的方式难以泛化到复杂HR场景。

Method: HEE框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 双评分机制评估区域是否含足够证据；(2) 对最有希望区域进行目标检测提取细粒度实体，聚类为连贯子区域并组织成多层次语义层次结构；(3) 置信度引导的回溯机制，当深层区域无法给出可信答案时回溯替代路径。

Result: 在Visual Probe和HR-Bench两个复杂HR基准上，HEE在准确性和效率上均优于ZoomEye和RAP等训练免费方法，并在Qwen2.5-VL、LLaVA-OneVision等多个MLLM上表现一致，同时在MME-RealWorld基准上展现了泛化能力。

Conclusion: HEE是一种有效的训练免费、模型无关的HR图像感知框架，通过动态层次化实体探索和置信度引导回溯机制，显著提升了MLLM在复杂高分辨率场景下的理解能力。

Original Abstract: High-resolution (HR) image perception remains a key challenge in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as fine-grained details are often lost when the image is processed as a whole. Existing methods either require training to teach models where to look or heuristically divide the image into fixed regions, both of which struggle to generalize in complex HR scenes. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Entity Exploration (HEE), a training-free and model-agnostic framework that transforms static image understanding into dynamic, query-guided entity exploration. HEE first evaluates each region using a dual scoring mechanism to determine whether it already contains sufficient evidence to answer the question. If not, it applies object detection within the most promising region to extract fine-grained entities, clusters them into coherent subregions, and organizes them into a multi-level semantic hierarchy for deeper exploration. When deeper regions still fail to yield confident answers, a confidence-guided backtracking mechanism revisits alternative paths to ensure adaptive perception. Extensive results show that HEE outperforms training-free methods like ZoomEye and RAP in both accuracy and efficiency on two complex HR benchmarks (Visual Probe and HR-Bench), across different MLLMs such as Qwen2.5-VL and LLaVA-OneVision. Moreover, HEE demonstrates generalization on the MME-RealWorld benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率（HR）图像感知仍然是多模态大语言模型（MLLM）面临的关键挑战，因为当图像作为整体处理时，细粒度细节往往会丢失。现有方法要么需要训练来教导模型关注何处，要么启发式地将图像划分为固定区域，这两种方法都难以在复杂HR场景中泛化。本文提出层次化实体探索（HEE），一种无需训练且与模型无关的框架，将静态图像理解转化为动态的、查询引导的实体探索。HEE首先使用双评分机制评估每个区域，判断其是否已包含足够证据来回答问题。若否，则在最有希望的区域上应用目标检测以提取细粒度实体，将其聚类为连贯的子区域，并组织成多层次语义层次结构以进行更深入的探索。当深层区域仍无法产生可信答案时，置信度引导的回溯机制会重新审视替代路径，确保自适应感知。大量结果表明，HEE在Visual Probe和HR-Bench两个复杂HR基准测试上，在准确性和效率方面均优于ZoomEye和RAP等训练免费方法，并适用于Qwen2.5-VL和LLaVA-OneVision等多种MLLM。此外，HEE在MME-RealWorld基准测试上也展现了良好的泛化能力。

</details>


### [98] [Training-Free Debiasing of Diffusion Models via CLIP-Guided Denoising Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00817)
*Dain Kim,Jinseo Kim,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的文本嵌入引导方法（TES），通过两阶段优化条件文本嵌入来缓解文本到图像扩散模型中的人口统计偏差，在保持图像质量的同时提升公平性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型存在人口统计偏差问题，中性提示词会生成刻板印象；而现有方法要么需要昂贵的重新训练，要么在推理时干预导致图像质量和语义对齐下降。

Method: TES框架采用两阶段策略：早期进行全局对齐，随后在去噪过程中基于CLIP反馈进行迭代细化，直接优化条件文本嵌入，无需修改模型参数。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion上的实验表明，TES在公平性上优于现有无训练基线方法，同时保持了有竞争力的图像质量。

Conclusion: 推理时文本嵌入优化是一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，可用于扩散模型中的公平性感知生成。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models achieve impressive visual quality, yet demographic bias remains a challenge, as neutral prompts consistently produce stereotypical representations across gender and race. Existing approaches remain limited by costly retraining or by inference-time interventions that often degrade image quality and semantic alignment. We propose Text Embedding Steering (TES), a training-free framework that mitigates demographic bias by directly optimizing conditional text embeddings during the diffusion process. We show that a two-stage strategy - early-stage global alignment followed by iterative denoising-time refinement with CLIP-based feedback - enables stable and controllable attribute steering without modifying model parameters. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion demonstrate that TES outperforms existing training-free baselines in fairness while maintaining competitive image quality. These results highlight that inference-time text embedding optimization is a practical and scalable solution for fairness-aware generation in diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像扩散模型在视觉质量上表现出色，但人口统计偏差仍然是一个挑战，因为中性提示词在性别和种族上持续生成刻板印象。现有方法要么受限于昂贵的重新训练，要么在推理时干预导致图像质量和语义对齐下降。我们提出文本嵌入引导（TES），一种无需训练的框架，通过在扩散过程中直接优化条件文本嵌入来缓解人口统计偏差。我们证明了两阶段策略——早期全局对齐，然后基于CLIP反馈进行去噪时迭代细化——能够在无需修改模型参数的情况下实现稳定且可控的属性引导。在Stable Diffusion上的大量实验表明，TES在公平性上优于现有无训练基线方法，同时保持了有竞争力的图像质量。这些结果突显了推理时文本嵌入优化是扩散模型中公平感知生成的一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

</details>


### [99] [Mirror-Fusion Attention for Reflection-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00850)
*Ruixin Li,Jin Liu,Yuling Shi,Stefano Lodi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MFASSL框架，通过在标准自监督学习中注入软反射先验，在不重新设计骨干网络的情况下，利用镜像融合注意力模块和反射一致性损失提升双近似对称数据（医学图像、人脸）的下游性能，仅增加约2.7%参数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准自监督学习方法强制鼓励数据增强后的不变性，严格的翻转不变性会抑制近似对称数据（如医学图像、人脸）中有信息量的左右对应关系，导致信息丢失。

Method: 构建估计对称轴对齐的镜像配对视图；提出轻量级镜像融合注意力（MFA）模块实现镜像区域间自适应token级交互同时保留非对称线索；在基础SSL目标上耦合反射一致性损失和中间层token对齐损失。

Result: 在CheXpert、BraTS、CelebA-HQ、WFLW数据集上，MFASSL在MoCo-v3、DINO、MAE基线（ViT-B/16设置）上提升了下游性能、校准度和反射鲁棒性，且比最近的等变SSL方法获得更强、更一致的增益，仅增加约2.7%参数。

Conclusion: 轻量级的几何感知先验可以有效补充基于不变性的自监督学习。

Original Abstract: Most self-supervised learning (SSL) methods encourage invariance across augmentations, but strict flip invariance can suppress informative left--right correspondences in approximately bilateral data such as medical images and human faces. We propose Mirror-Fusion-Augmented Self-Supervised Learning (MFASSL), a Vision Transformer framework that injects a soft reflection prior into standard SSL without redesigning the backbone. MFASSL constructs mirror-paired views aligned to an estimated symmetry axis and introduces a lightweight Mirror-Fusion Attention (MFA) module for adaptive token-level interaction between mirrored regions while preserving asymmetric cues. The base SSL objective is further coupled with reflection-consistency and mid-layer token-alignment losses. Across CheXpert, BraTS, CelebA-HQ, and WFLW, MFASSL improves downstream performance, calibration, and reflection robustness over MoCo-v3, DINO, and MAE baselines under matched ViT-B/16 settings. It also achieves stronger and more consistent gains than recent equivariant SSL approaches with only approximately 2.7\% additional parameters. These results show that lightweight geometry-aware priors can effectively complement invariance-based SSL.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数自监督学习方法鼓励数据增强后的不变性，但在医学图像和人脸等近似双侧对称的数据中，严格的翻转不变性会抑制有信息量的左右对应关系。本文提出了镜像融合增强自监督学习（MFASSL），一个视觉Transformer框架，它无需重新设计骨干网络即可将软反射先验注入标准自监督学习中。MFASSL构建了与估计对称轴对齐的镜像配对视图，并引入了轻量级的镜像融合注意力（MFA）模块，用于镜像区域之间自适应的token级交互，同时保留非对称线索。基础自监督目标进一步与反射一致性损失和中间层token对齐损失相结合。在CheXpert、BraTS、CelebA-HQ和WFLW数据集上，与MoCo-v3、DINO和MAE基线在匹配的ViT-B/16设置下相比，MFASSL在下游性能、校准度和反射鲁棒性方面均有提升。它还在仅增加约2.7%额外参数的情况下，比最近的等变自监督方法取得了更强且更一致的增益。这些结果表明，轻量级的几何感知先验可以有效补充基于不变性的自监督学习。

</details>


### [100] [Stitched Embeddings: A Unified Latent Space for 3D Garments and 2D Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00829)
*Andrea Sanchietti,Riccardo Marin,Bharat Lal Bhatnagar,Yuanlu Xu,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Stitched Embeddings，首个无需物理模拟即可统一3D服装重建与2D缝纫模式推断的双向潜空间框架，利用预训练3D基础模型和BoxMesh中间表示实现高效、可微的服装建模。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 服装因其拓扑多样性比参数化人体更难建模，传统方法依赖2D缝纫模式与物理模拟之间的桥接，计算开销大且效率低，同时高质量服装建模数据稀缺。

Method: 利用预训练3D基础模型的几何先验克服数据稀缺问题；提出BoxMesh作为关键中间表示，将2D面板对齐到3D配置而无需物理模拟器；构建双向潜空间统一3D重建与2D模式推断；整个流程可微分。

Result: 在模式重建任务上达到最先进精度，同时显著提升了效率；支持从网格恢复缝纫模式以及从2D模式进行3D编辑等新应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经3D视觉与物理服装制造流水线之间提供了可扩展的桥梁，证明了无需物理模拟即可实现服装重建与模式推断的统一。

Original Abstract: While garments are essential for realistic digital humans, their topological variety makes them much harder to model than parametric bodies. Traditional tailoring relies on 2D sewing patterns, yet bridging these patterns to 3D geometry currently requires physical simulations. We present Stitched Embeddings, the first simulation-free framework to unify 3D garment reconstruction and sewing pattern inference within a single bidirectional latent space. By leveraging the geometric priors of a pretrained 3D foundation model, our approach overcomes the data scarcity typically associated with high-quality garment modeling. We propose to use the BoxMesh as a critical intermediate representation to align 2D panels into 3D configurations without the computational overhead of a simulator. This architecture achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in pattern reconstruction while significantly improving efficiency. Furthermore, our differentiable pipeline enables novel applications, including pattern recovery from meshes and 3D editing from 2D patterns. Finally, this work provides a scalable link between neural 3D vision and the physical garment manufacturing pipeline. Project Page: https://andreus00.github.io/stitchedembeddings

中文摘要翻译: 尽管服装对于逼真的数字人体至关重要，但其拓扑多样性使其比参数化人体更难建模。传统裁剪依赖于2D缝纫模式，但将这些模式与3D几何桥接目前需要物理模拟。我们提出Stitched Embeddings，这是首个无模拟框架，在单一双向潜空间中统一了3D服装重建与缝纫模式推断。通过利用预训练3D基础模型的几何先验，我们的方法克服了高质量服装建模中常见的数据稀缺问题。我们提出使用BoxMesh作为关键的中间表示，将2D面板对齐到3D配置，且无需模拟器的计算开销。该架构在模式重建上达到了最先进的精度，同时显著提升了效率。此外，我们的可微流水线支持新颖应用，包括从网格恢复缝纫模式以及从2D模式进行3D编辑。最后，本工作为神经3D视觉与物理服装制造流水线之间提供了可扩展的桥梁。

</details>


### [101] [MoVA: Learning Asymmetric Dual Projections for Modular Long Video-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00858)
*Peiyuan Zhu,Shaoan Xie,Zijian Li,Yifan Shen,Namrata Deka,Harsh Shrivastava,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoVA框架，通过双非对称投影（文本侧自适应选择帧感知子空间，视频侧解缠文本相关视觉概念）解决视频-文本对齐中的时序错位和语义不对称问题，在多项任务中优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对比预训练模型（如CLIP）在视频-文本对齐中继承了两个根本性缺陷：时序错位（文本只关联特定时间窗口）和语义不对称（帧级细节与字幕概念之间稀疏、双向且非等价的相关性），导致表示纠缠，无论短字幕（歧义）还是长字幕（静态与动态纠缠）都无法解决。

Method: 提出MoVA（Modular Long Video-Text Alignment）框架：1）建立允许跨时间维度和不同粒度灵活对齐的理论条件；2）学习双非对称投影——文本侧投影自适应选择字幕的帧感知子空间，视频侧投影解缠文本相关的视觉概念。

Result: 在多项视频-文本对齐任务中，MoVA优于现有方法，验证了其在保持全局跨模态语义的同时解缠演化中的帧特定概念，并能自然扩展至长字幕和长视频。

Conclusion: MoVA通过理论指导的双非对称投影机制有效解决了视频-文本对齐中的时序错位和语义不对称问题，为长视频-文本灵活对齐提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Contrastive pre-training has propelled video-text alignment, yet models often inherit the critical limitations of their image-text predecessors like CLIP, resulting in entangled representations. These challenges are severely exacerbated by two fundamental properties in the video domain: Temporal Misalignment, where textual descriptions often correlate only to specific, constrained temporal windows, leaving other frames text-irrelevant; and Semantic Asymmetry, which dictates a sparse, bidirectional, and non-equivalent relevance between frame-level visual details and caption-level concepts. This failure persists whether captions are short and temporally disjoint, creating ambiguity, or long and detailed, fostering entanglement between static objects and their temporal evolution. In this paper, we establish theoretical conditions that enable flexible alignment between video and text representations across the temporal dimension and at varying levels of granularity. Building on these theoretical insights, we introduce MoVA, Modular Long Video-Text Alignment, which learns dual asymmetric projections: a text-side projection that adaptively selects frame-aware subspaces of the caption, and a video-side projection that disentangles text-relevant visual concepts. Our framework ensures that the model can preserve global cross-modal semantics while disentangling evolving, frame-specific concepts and scale naturally to long captions and videos. Empirical evaluations show that MoVA outperforms existing methods in multiple video-text alignment tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 对比预训练推动了视频-文本对齐的发展，但模型通常继承了其图像-文本前身（如CLIP）的关键局限性，导致表示纠缠。这些问题在视频领域中被两个基本属性严重加剧：时序错位（文本描述通常仅关联特定的、受限的时间窗口，使其他帧与文本无关）和语义不对称（帧级视觉细节与字幕级概念之间存在稀疏、双向且非等价的相关性）。无论字幕是简短且时间不连续的（产生歧义），还是冗长且详细的（导致静态对象与其时间演化之间的纠缠），这种失败都持续存在。在本文中，我们建立了允许视频和文本表示跨时间维度及不同粒度进行灵活对齐的理论条件。基于这些理论洞见，我们提出了MoVA（模块化长视频-文本对齐），该方法学习双非对称投影：一个文本侧投影，自适应地选择字幕的帧感知子空间；一个视频侧投影，解缠文本相关的视觉概念。我们的框架确保模型能够保持全局跨模态语义，同时解缠演化的、帧特定的概念，并自然地扩展到长字幕和长视频。实证评估表明，MoVA在多个视频-文本对齐任务中优于现有方法，证明了我们方法的有效性。

</details>


### [102] [Pano2World: End-to-End 3D Generation via Unified Multi-View Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00832)
*Zhenjia Li,Jinrang Jia,Yifeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pano2World提出了一种从单张室内全景图直接生成可自由探索的3D高斯场景的方法，通过全景扩散模型和视图感知注意力路由实现多视图一致性，并引入潜在特征适配器避免信息损失，在多位置全景新视角合成任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单全景图转3D场景的方法存在两大问题：一是逐视角补全导致误差累积和繁琐的多步流程；二是基于视频生成模型的方法受限于连续轨迹约束，难以同时从多个方向覆盖场景。因此需要一种能直接、高效地从单张全景图生成持久、可自由导航的3D表示的方法。

Method: 首先从输入全景图重建粗粒度3D高斯代理，并在自适应采样的邻近位姿上渲染得到几何对齐的引导全景图；然后通过全景扩散模型和视图感知注意力路由（View-Aware Attention Routing）联合去噪所有目标视图，每个目标视图同时接收引导全景图的几何约束和源全景图的全局语义约束；最后提出Latent Feature Adapter（潜在特征适配器），直接蒸馏联合去噪过程中形成的多视图隐特征到场景潜在表示中，再解码为最终的3D高斯场景。

Result: 在多位置全景新视角合成基准上，Pano2World显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Pano2World通过端到端的全景到3D高斯场景生成框架，有效解决了误差累积和视角覆盖受限的问题，为单全景图自由视角导航提供了一种高效且质量更高的解决方案。

Original Abstract: A single panorama captures the full visual sphere from one camera center, yet confines users to looking around in place without enabling true scene exploration. Converting a single panorama into a persistent, renderable 3D representation for free-viewpoint navigation has attracted growing interest; existing methods either adopt iterative per-view completion that propagates inpainting results to update the underlying geometry, leading to progressive error accumulation and cumbersome multi-step pipelines, or leverage the temporal consistency priors of video generation models, yet the continuous-trajectory constraint intrinsic to such models limits their flexibility in covering scenes from multiple directions simultaneously. We present Pano2World, which takes a single indoor panorama as input and directly outputs a persistent, explorable 3D Gaussian scene. Given the source panorama, Pano2World first reconstructs a coarse 3D Gaussian proxy and renders it at adaptively sampled nearby poses to obtain geometrically aligned guidance panoramas; a panoramic diffusion model then jointly denoises all target views via View-Aware Attention Routing, where each target view simultaneously receives geometric constraints from its corresponding guidance panorama and global semantic guidance from the source panorama, naturally enforcing cross-view consistency. To avoid the information loss incurred by decoding the multi-view hidden features formed during joint denoising back to the pixel domain via VAE, we introduce Latent Feature Adapter, a geometry-aware bridge module that directly distills these hidden features into a scene latent, subsequently decoded into the final 3D Gaussian scene. Experiments demonstrate that Pano2World significantly outperforms existing methods on the multi-position panoramic novel-view synthesis benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 单张全景图虽能从一个相机中心捕获完整的视觉球体，但将用户限制在原地环顾四周，无法实现真正的场景探索。将单张全景图转换为持久的、可渲染的3D表示以支持自由视角导航，已引起越来越多的关注。现有方法要么采用迭代逐视角补全，将修复结果传播以更新底层几何结构，导致渐进式误差累积和繁琐的多步流程；要么利用视频生成模型的时间一致性先验，但此类模型固有的连续轨迹约束限制了其同时从多个方向覆盖场景的灵活性。我们提出Pano2World，该方法以单张室内全景图为输入，直接输出一个持久的、可探索的3D高斯场景。给定源全景图，Pano2World首先重建一个粗粒度3D高斯代理，并在自适应采样的邻近位姿上渲染该代理以获取几何对齐的引导全景图；随后，一个全景扩散模型通过视图感知注意力路由（View-Aware Attention Routing）联合去噪所有目标视图，其中每个目标视图同时接收来自对应引导全景图的几何约束和来自源全景图的全局语义约束，自然地强制跨视图一致性。为避免通过VAE将联合去噪过程中形成的多视图隐特征解码回像素域所造成的信息损失，我们引入了潜在特征适配器（Latent Feature Adapter），这是一个几何感知的桥接模块，它直接将这些隐特征蒸馏到场景潜在表示中，随后解码为最终的3D高斯场景。实验表明，Pano2World在多位置全景新视角合成基准上显著优于现有方法。

</details>


### [103] [Foundation Models vs. Radiomics for Lung Computed Tomography: A Benchmark of Feature Extractors, Classification Heads, and Segmentation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01001)
*Nils Neukirch,Martin Maurer,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种特征提取器、七种分类头和三种分割策略在CT肺癌表型分析中的表现，发现最优设计取决于具体任务：分割对体积和分期分类最重要，分类器选择对生存、组织学和年龄预测最关键。推荐Curia+肿瘤分割+CatBoost作为安全默认方案，无肿瘤勾画时可用Curia-2+肺分割+逻辑回归替代。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究在比较放射组学与基础模型时，未能独立分析特征提取器、分类头和分割策略各自的贡献，也缺乏跨队列鲁棒性测试。

Method: 在五项任务（肿瘤体积与分期分类、2年生存预测、组织学分类、年龄预测）上，系统比较五种特征提取器（Curia、Curia-2、DINOv3、Radiomics2D、Radiomics3D）、七种分类头（TabPFN、TabICL、XGBoost、CatBoost、随机森林、逻辑回归、Ridge）和三种分割策略。使用LUNG1训练、内部测试集和外部LUNG2队列评估，以最差跨队列性能为主要指标。

Result: 分割策略主导体积和分期分类的性能，分类器选择主导生存、组织学和年龄预测。放射组学在肿瘤体积、分期和生存预测上有竞争力（部分归因于标签衍生效应）；Curia变体在生存预测上达到可比峰值；DINOv3在各任务略逊。补丁与切片聚合方式影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 推荐Curia+肿瘤分割+CatBoost作为跨任务安全默认方案，但任务特异性选择始终优于任何通用默认方案。无肿瘤勾画时，Curia-2+肺分割+逻辑回归是竞争性替代。所有流程采用两阶段设计，适用于小队列场景以避免过拟合。

Original Abstract: Radiomics is the established approach for CT-based lung cancer phenotyping, yet comparisons with foundation models rarely isolate contributions of feature extractor, classification head, and segmentation choice, or test cross-cohort robustness. We benchmark five feature extractors (Curia, Curia-2, DINOv3, Radiomics2D, Radiomics3D), seven classification heads (TabPFN, TabICL, XGBoost, CatBoost, Random Forest, logistic regression, Ridge), and three segmentation regimes on five tasks: tumor volume and stage classification, 2-year survival prediction, histology classification, and age prediction. Models are trained on LUNG1 (n=338) and evaluated on an internal test set (n=84) and the external LUNG2 cohort (n=211), with worst-case cross-cohort performance as the primary metric. The dominant design factor is task-dependent: segmentation drives volume and stage classification, while classifier choice drives survival, histology, and age prediction. Radiomics is competitive for tumor volume, tumor stage and survival (partly due to label-derivation effects for the former); Curia variants reach comparable peak scores for survival; DINOv3 falls slightly short across tasks. Patch and slice aggregation have negligible impact. We recommend Curia with tumor segmentation and a CatBoost head as a safe default, achieving the best mean rank across the three primary clinical tasks, though task-specific selection consistently outperforms any cross-task default. When tumor delineations are unavailable, Curia-2 with lung segmentation and logistic regression offers a competitive alternative. All pipelines use a two-stage design suited to small cohort sizes where end-to-end fine-tuning would risk overfitting.

中文摘要翻译: 放射组学是基于CT的肺癌表型分析的既定方法，然而与基础模型的比较很少独立评估特征提取器、分类头和分割选择各自的贡献，也缺乏跨队列鲁棒性测试。我们在五项任务上系统比较了五种特征提取器（Curia、Curia-2、DINOv3、Radiomics2D、Radiomics3D）、七种分类头（TabPFN、TabICL、XGBoost、CatBoost、随机森林、逻辑回归、Ridge）和三种分割策略：肿瘤体积与分期分类、2年生存预测、组织学分类和年龄预测。模型在LUNG1（n=338）上训练，在内部测试集（n=84）和外部LUNG2队列（n=211）上评估，以最差跨队列性能作为主要指标。主导设计因素依赖于任务：分割驱动体积和分期分类的性能，而分类器选择驱动生存、组织学和年龄预测。放射组学在肿瘤体积、肿瘤分期和生存预测上具有竞争力（前两者部分归因于标签衍生效应）；Curia变体在生存预测上达到可比峰值；DINOv3在所有任务上略逊一筹。补丁和切片聚合方式影响可忽略。我们推荐Curia配合肿瘤分割和CatBoost分类头作为安全默认方案，在三个主要临床任务上实现了最佳平均排名，尽管任务特异性选择始终优于任何跨任务默认方案。当肿瘤勾画不可用时，Curia-2配合肺分割和逻辑回归提供了有竞争力的替代方案。所有流程均采用两阶段设计，适用于端到端微调存在过拟合风险的小队列场景。

</details>


### [104] [Rethinking Multi-Label Image Classification With Deep Learning: Taxonomy, Challenge, and Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00839)
*Xuelin Zhu,Xiu-Shen Wei,Jiawei Ge,Shuai Xu,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了基于深度学习的多标签图像分类（MLIC）方法，将现有方法系统性地归纳为六大类（区域导向、标签导向、架构导向、表示导向、学习导向和数据导向），并深入分析了MLIC的学习机制、关键挑战及未来研究方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多标签图像分类是计算机视觉中的基础任务，广泛应用于自动驾驶、疾病诊断、推荐系统和移动服务机器人等领域。尽管基于CNN、RNN和Transformer的深度学习方法已显著推动该领域发展，但缺乏一个全面、系统的综述来梳理和分类这些方法，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文通过文献综述的方式，首先回顾了MLIC的背景知识（问题定义、数据集、骨干网络和评价指标），然后构建了一个可解释的分类法，将深度学习方法分为六类：区域导向方法、标签导向方法、架构导向方法、表示导向方法、学习导向方法和数据导向方法。最后深入剖析了MLIC中的学习博弈及其对其他视觉领域的启示。

Result: 提出了一个系统性的MLIC方法分类体系（六类方法），总结了MLIC中的关键挑战（如长尾分布、标签相关性建模等），并指出了未来有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文为研究社区提供了一个全面且系统的MLIC视角，有助于推动该领域及计算机视觉其他相关方向的后续探索与创新。

Original Abstract: Multi-label image classification (MLIC), a fundamental task in computer vision, focuses on identifying multiple objects or concepts within an image, underpinning numerous read-world applications, such as autonomous driving, disease diagnosis, recommendation system, and mobile service robot. Over the past decade, deep learning paradigms based on convolutional neural networks, recurrent neural networks, and Transformers have significantly advanced this field, owing to their powerful capability in visual representation and relationship modeling. These advances have markedly improved the robustness, scalability, and generalization ability of MLIC models across diverse datasets and application domains. In this survey, we provide a comprehensive review of the deep learning-based literature on MLIC. Concretely, we first revisit the background, including problem definition, datasets, backbones and evaluation metrics. Next, we develop a plausible taxonomy for the deep learning-based MLIC approaches, organizing them into six groups: region-oriented methods, label-oriented methods, architecture-oriented methods, representation-oriented methods, learning-oriented methods, and data-oriented methods. Finally, we provide an insightful exposition of the underlying learning game in MLIC and its implications for other vision domains, and we empirically summarize the key challenges and research directions in MLIC while outlining promising avenues for future development. We believe this survey offers the research community a holistic and systematic perspective on MLIC, thereby facilitating subsequent exploration and innovation in this field and beyond.

中文摘要翻译: 多标签图像分类（MLIC）是计算机视觉中的一项基础任务，旨在识别图像中的多个物体或概念，支撑着众多实际应用，如自动驾驶、疾病诊断、推荐系统和移动服务机器人。过去十年中，基于卷积神经网络、循环神经网络和Transformer的深度学习范式凭借其在视觉表示和关系建模方面的强大能力，显著推动了该领域的发展。这些进展在多样化数据集和应用领域中显著提升了MLIC模型的鲁棒性、可扩展性和泛化能力。在本综述中，我们全面回顾了基于深度学习的MLIC文献。具体而言，我们首先回顾了背景知识，包括问题定义、数据集、骨干网络和评价指标。接着，我们构建了一个可解释的深度学习MLIC方法分类体系，将其组织为六大类：区域导向方法、标签导向方法、架构导向方法、表示导向方法、学习导向方法和数据导向方法。最后，我们深入阐述了MLIC中潜在的学习博弈及其对其他视觉领域的启示，并经验性地总结了MLIC的关键挑战和研究方向，同时概述了未来发展的有前景的路径。我们相信，本综述为研究社区提供了一个整体且系统的MLIC视角，从而推动了该领域及更广泛领域的后续探索与创新。

</details>


### [105] [TrajLoc: Trajectory-Attention Localization for Multi-Object Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00861)
*Omer Sela,Inbar Huberman-Spiegelglas,Michael Rotman,Sagie Benaim,Avi Ben-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajLoc方法，通过将每个物体token的交叉注意力权重替换为高斯热图，在注意力层内实现每个物体的独立空间约束，从而在图像到视频生成中精确控制多个物体的运动轨迹，显著提升视觉保真度和轨迹遵循度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将多个物体的轨迹纠缠在共享的密集条件信号中，导致在拥挤场景下难以保持物体级别的对应关系。随着物体数量增加和路径交叉遮挡，问题愈发严峻，亟需一种能独立约束每个物体空间位置的方法。

Method: TrajLoc在注意力层内为每个物体token的交叉注意力权重替换为以目标位置为中心的高斯热图，实现严格的逐物体空间约束。每个物体token接口承载轨迹和深度信息（通过学习嵌入），并通过编码首帧外观来保持物体身份。

Result: 在六个数据集（最多20个同时控制的物体）和两个架构不同的骨干网络（CogVideoX 5B和WaN 2.1 14B）上，相比最强基线，PSNR平均提升+4.3 dB，轨迹终点误差降低51%。

Conclusion: TrajLoc通过解耦每个物体的空间约束，在图像到视频生成中实现了对多个物体运动轨迹的精确控制，同时保持了物体身份和视觉质量，验证了该范式在复杂场景下的有效性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Controlling the motion of multiple objects in image-to-video (I2V) generation requires preserving object identities while enforcing adherence to distinct target trajectories. This becomes particularly challenging as the number of objects increases and their paths intersect or occlude one another. Existing approaches entangle multiple trajectories within a shared, dense conditioning signal, making object-level correspondence difficult to preserve in crowded scenes. We depart from this paradigm and enforce a strict, per object spatial constraint that isolates instances independently. Our method, TrajLoc, achieves this directly within the attention layers by substituting the cross-attention weights of each object token with a Gaussian heatmap centered on its target location at every frame. The same per object token interface carries trajectory and depth through a learned embedding and preserves identity by encoding first frame appearance in place of an object token. Evaluations across six datasets, featuring up to 20 simultaneously controlled objects and out of distribution real world scenes, demonstrate that our method consistently improves both visual fidelity and trajectory adherence. Applied to two architecturally distinct backbones (CogVideoX 5B and WaN 2.1 14B), our approach achieves average gains of +4.3 dB PSNR and a 51% reduction in trajectory end point error compared to the strongest baselines. Project page: https://sela-omer.github.io/traj-loc/

中文摘要翻译: 在图像到视频（I2V）生成中控制多个物体的运动，要求在保持物体身份的同时严格遵循不同的目标轨迹。随着物体数量增加及其路径交叉或相互遮挡，这一问题变得尤为棘手。现有方法将多个轨迹纠缠在共享的密集条件信号中，使得在拥挤场景下难以保持物体级别的对应关系。我们突破这一范式，强制执行严格的逐物体空间约束，将每个实例独立开来。我们的方法TrajLoc直接在注意力层内实现这一点：将每个物体token的交叉注意力权重替换为以其每帧目标位置为中心的高斯热图。相同的逐物体token接口通过学习的嵌入向量携带轨迹和深度信息，并通过编码首帧外观替代物体token来保持身份。在六个数据集上的评估表明，我们的方法在视觉保真度和轨迹遵循度上均实现了一致提升，这些数据集包含多达20个同时控制的物体以及分布外的真实世界场景。将本方法应用于两个架构不同的骨干网络（CogVideoX 5B和WaN 2.1 14B），相比最强基线，PSNR平均提升+4.3 dB，轨迹终点误差降低51%。项目页面：https://sela-omer.github.io/traj-loc/

</details>


### [106] [EFlow: Learning Evidence Flow for Long-Video Reasoning with Adaptive Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00867)
*Wenhao Zhang,Kuanwei Lin,Xuyi Yang,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EFlow，一种基于Qwen3-VL的证据优先视频推理框架，通过分离时间定位与逻辑推理、引入置信度感知反思机制，解决了长视频推理中"过早语义承诺"导致证据偏差的问题。在五个基准上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工具增强视频框架将时间定位与答案推理交织在同一轨迹中，导致早期语义假设偏向证据定位，形成"过早语义承诺"的失败模式——有偏差的定位获取不完整证据，不完整证据又强化错误推理。亟需一种证据优先的推理框架来解耦这两个过程。

Method: EFlow基于Qwen3-VL，核心包含：(1) 显式分离时间定位（CoT for Temporal Grounding）与逻辑推理（CoT for Reasoning）两个阶段，先检索证据再进行答案推理；(2) 置信度感知反思机制，在检索证据可能不足时重新评估整个视频；(3) 构建专用轨迹数据集，通过监督微调、强化学习和强化微调联合训练。

Result: 在五个视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，EFlow能够持续提升长视频推理性能。

Conclusion: EFlow通过证据优先的范式有效缓解了长视频推理中的过早语义承诺问题，为长视频理解任务提供了更可靠的推理框架。

Original Abstract: Long-video reasoning is fundamentally constrained by how models acquire and utilize visual evidence. Existing tool-augmented video frameworks often interleave temporal grounding and answer reasoning within a single trajectory, causing early semantic hypotheses to bias evidence localization. We term this failure mode premature semantic commitment, where biased grounding retrieves incomplete evidence and incomplete evidence further reinforces incorrect reasoning. To address this issue, we propose EFlow, an evidence-first video reasoning framework built upon Qwen3-VL. EFlow explicitly separates temporal grounding and logical reasoning through CoT for Temporal Grounding and CoT for Reasoning, enabling the model to retrieve relevant evidence before answer inference. In addition, EFlow introduces a confidence-aware reflection mechanism that re-evaluates the full video when retrieved evidence is potentially insufficient. We further construct dedicated trajectory datasets and train EFlow through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that EFlow consistently improves long-video reasoning performance.

中文摘要翻译: 长视频推理从根本上受限于模型获取和利用视觉证据的方式。现有的工具增强视频框架通常将时间定位和答案推理交织在单一轨迹中，导致早期语义假设偏向证据定位。我们将这种故障模式称为"过早语义承诺"——有偏差的定位获取不完整证据，不完整证据又进一步强化错误推理。为解决这一问题，我们提出EFlow，一种基于Qwen3-VL的证据优先视频推理框架。EFlow通过"时间定位思维链"和"推理思维链"显式分离时间定位与逻辑推理，使模型在答案推理之前先检索相关证据。此外，EFlow引入了一种置信度感知反思机制，当检索到的证据可能不足时重新评估整个视频。我们进一步构建了专用轨迹数据集，并通过监督微调、强化学习和强化微调来训练EFlow。在五个视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，EFlow持续改进了长视频推理性能。

</details>


### [107] [OmniView-Space: Reinforcing Spatial Reasoning via Multi-Perspective Spatial Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00881)
*Xudong Li,Mengdan Zhang,Peixian Chen,Jiaxi Tan,Zihao Huang,Jingyuan Zheng,Yan Zhang,Xiawu Zheng,Xing Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在空间智能上的不足，提出OmniView-Space框架，通过多视角空间映射、工具引导的自我中心推理和认知地图蒸馏，在多轮空间推理中保持空间一致性，实现SOTA性能并减少对外部几何流水线的依赖。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在空间推理任务中，尤其是在需要进行多步推理并动态地将证据重新锚定到不同参考系（如以相机、物体或方向为中心）时表现不佳，现有方法依赖文本推理或3D重建难以维持空间一致性。

Method: 提出OmniView-Space框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视角空间映射（MPSM），将重建的几何结构重新锚定到查询对齐的视觉认知地图和文本空间图；(2) 工具引导的自我中心推理，通过交错策略训练模型主动选择查询所需的自我锚点并请求对应的MPSM证据；(3) 认知地图蒸馏，利用MPSM生成的轨迹和自身框架奖励训练模型使用自生成的认知地图进行推理。

Result: 在单图像和多图像空间推理基准上取得SOTA性能，蒸馏后的模型在保持性能的同时减少了对外部几何流水线的依赖。

Conclusion: OmniView-Space通过多模态自我中心证据维持空间一致性的方案在空间推理任务上是有效的，且认知地图蒸馏能够在不牺牲性能的前提下降低对外部几何流水线的依赖。

Original Abstract: Spatial intelligence remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires coherent spatial scene representations beyond basic object recognition. Existing methods typically build such representations through textual reasoning or 3D reconstruction. However, they often falter during multi-step reasoning, particularly when required to dynamically re-anchor evidence to the specific camera-, object-, or direction-centric reference frames demanded by complex queries. To address this, we propose OmniView-Space, a framework designed to maintain spatial consistency through multimodal egocentric evidence. Our approach consists of three core components: (1) Multi-Perspective Spatial Mapping (MPSM), which re-anchors reconstructed geometry into a query-aligned visual cognitive map and a textual spatial graph; (2) Tool-Guided Egocentric Reasoning, an interleaved policy trained to actively select the ego anchor required by the query and request the corresponding MPSM evidence; and (3) Cognitive-Map Distillation, which uses MPSM-generated trajectories and ego-frame rewards to train the model to reason with self-generated cognitive maps. Experiments on single- and multi-image spatial reasoning benchmarks show that OmniView-Space achieves state-of-the-art performance. Furthermore, the distilled model maintains this performance while reducing reliance on external geometry pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 空间智能对于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）来说仍然是一个持续的挑战，因为它需要超越基本物体识别的连贯空间场景表示。现有方法通常通过文本推理或3D重建来构建此类表示。然而，它们在多步推理过程中常常表现不佳，特别是在需要将证据动态地重新锚定到复杂查询所要求的特定以相机、物体或方向为中心的参考系时。为此，我们提出了OmniView-Space，一个旨在通过多模态自我中心证据维持空间一致性的框架。我们的方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视角空间映射（MPSM），它将重建的几何结构重新锚定到与查询对齐的视觉认知地图和文本空间图；(2) 工具引导的自我中心推理，一种交错的策略，训练模型主动选择查询所需的自我锚点并请求相应的MPSM证据；(3) 认知地图蒸馏，利用MPSM生成的轨迹和自身框架奖励来训练模型使用自生成的认知地图进行推理。在单图像和多图像空间推理基准上的实验表明，OmniView-Space实现了最先进的性能。此外，蒸馏后的模型在保持这一性能的同时减少了对外部几何流水线的依赖。

</details>


### [108] [Improving Sparse-View 3DGS Generalization via Flat Minima Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00885)
*Kangmin Seo,Sangeek Hyun,MinKyu Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在稀疏视角监督下容易过拟合的问题，提出了一种基于平坦最小值（Flat Minima）优化的轻量训练框架。该方法通过对高斯参数施加各向异性感知的扰动进行正则化，并引入周期性重初始化策略来稳定优化过程，在不改变模型架构的前提下，有效提升了稀疏视角下的新视角合成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3DGS在稀疏输入视角下容易过拟合观测图像，对未见过视角的泛化能力差。现有方法缺乏有效正则化手段来缓解这一过拟合问题。

Method: （1）将平坦最小值优化原理适配到3DGS中，通过考虑高斯各向异性和训练进度的可控高斯参数扰动来正则化优化过程；（2）引入周期性重初始化策略，在短时间窗口内将非位置参数暂时恢复到初始状态，以稳定平坦最小值优化过程。

Result: 在LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的实验表明，该方法在稀疏视角监督下取得了更优的定量指标和感知质量，生成的重建结果更清晰、更稳定，对新颖视角的泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 本文提出的平坦最小值优化框架能够无缝集成到现有3DGS流水线中，无需架构改动即可有效缓解稀疏视角过拟合问题，提升新视角合成的质量和泛化性。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in neural rendering have established 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) as a highly efficient representation for novel view synthesis, enabling fast training and real-time rendering with strong fidelity. However, when supervision is limited to sparse input views, 3DGS tends to overfit to the observed images and generalize poorly to unseen viewpoints. We address this challenge from the perspective of flat minima (FM) optimization, which seeks solutions that remain stable under small parameter perturbations. Viewing Gaussian parameters as trainable weights, we adapt FM principles to the geometric and dynamic nature of 3DGS with a lightweight training framework. Our method regularizes optimization with controlled Gaussian perturbations that account for each Gaussian's anisotropy and the training progress, preserving fine details while improving robustness to sparse-view overfitting. To further stabilize this flat minima optimization process, we introduce periodic reinitialization, which temporarily returns non-positional parameters to their initial states for a short window. Together, these techniques integrate seamlessly into existing 3DGS pipelines without architectural changes. Experiments on LLFF and Mip-NeRF360 datasets demonstrate improved quantitative metrics and perceptual quality under sparse-view supervision, producing reconstructions that are sharper, more stable, and better generalized to novel viewpoints.

中文摘要翻译: 神经渲染领域的最新进展已使3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）成为一种用于新视角合成的高效表示方法，可实现快速训练和实时渲染并保持高保真度。然而，当监督信息仅限于稀疏的输入视角时，3DGS容易过拟合到观测图像，对未见过视角的泛化能力较差。本文从平坦最小值（Flat Minima, FM）优化的角度来应对这一挑战，该优化方法旨在寻找在参数微小扰动下保持稳定的解。将高斯参数视为可训练权重，我们针对3DGS的几何和动态特性适配了FM原理，构建了一个轻量级训练框架。我们的方法通过可控的高斯扰动来正则化优化过程，这些扰动考虑了每个高斯的各向异性特性及训练进度，在保留细节的同时提高了对稀疏视角过拟合的鲁棒性。为进一步稳定这一平坦最小值优化过程，我们引入了周期性重初始化策略，在短时间窗口内将非位置参数暂时恢复到其初始状态。综上所述，这些技术无需改变模型架构即可无缝集成到现有的3DGS流水线中。在LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的实验表明，在稀疏视角监督下，该方法在定量指标和感知质量上均有改善，生成的重建结果更清晰、更稳定，对新颖视角的泛化能力更强。

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Pixel Overlap: A Framework for Decomposing Segmentation Evaluation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00886)
*Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将二值目标分割评估指标分解为五个模块化设计阶段（预测表示、目标提取、目标匹配、分数计算、指标报告）的框架，系统分析了现有指标的设计选择与局限性，并讨论了面向任务的自定义评估协议的设计空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有二值目标分割评估指标往往被当作孤立的公式使用，其隐含的设计假设和局限性不透明，缺乏一个统一的框架来系统比较和理解不同指标之间的差异及其适用场景。

Method: 提出一个五阶段分解框架，将每个评估指标拆解为：预测表示、目标提取、目标匹配、分数计算和指标报告五个模块化设计阶段，利用该框架对代表性指标进行分析，展示新指标如何解决旧协议中的特定限制。

Result: 通过该框架清晰展示了各指标的设计假设和局限性，揭示了新指标（如针对伪装、透明、玻璃、镜像等场景的评估协议）如何针对性地改进早期指标中的特定缺陷，并为任务感知的自定义评估协议提供了设计空间参考。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架使评估指标的设计选择透明化，有助于研究者根据具体任务需求设计和选择更合适的评估协议，推动了评估方法论的系统化发展。

Original Abstract: Evaluation metrics are central to binary target segmentation because they determine how progress is measured, compared, and interpreted. In this paper, target denotes the task-defined positive region to be segmented rather than a generic foreground object. It may be salient, camouflaged, transparent, glass-like, mirror-like, shadow-like, lesion-like, or defined by other application-specific semantics. We treat existing metrics as compositions of modular design choices rather than isolated formulas. The proposed framework decomposes each metric into five stages covering prediction representation, target extraction, target matching, score computation, and metric reporting. We use this framework to analyze representative metrics and show how newer metrics address specific limits in earlier protocols. The stage choices keep each metric's assumptions visible. We then discuss the design space opened by the framework and its implications for task-aware evaluation protocols. Reference code is available at https://github.com/lartpang/PySODMetrics.

中文摘要翻译: 评估指标在二值目标分割中至关重要，因为它们决定了如何衡量、比较和解释研究进展。本文中，"目标"指的是任务所定义的需要分割的正区域，而非通用的前景对象。目标可以是显著、伪装、透明、玻璃状、镜面状、阴影状、病灶状，或由其他应用特定语义所定义。本文将现有指标视为模块化设计选择的组合，而非孤立的公式。所提出的框架将每个指标分解为五个阶段：预测表示、目标提取、目标匹配、分数计算和指标报告。我们利用该框架分析代表性指标，并展示较新的指标如何解决早期协议中的特定局限。各阶段的选择使得每个指标的设计假设清晰可见。最后，我们讨论了该框架所开启的设计空间及其对面向任务的评估协议的启示。参考代码可在 https://github.com/lartpang/PySODMetrics 获取。

</details>


### [110] [Geometry-Aware Cross-Height Channel Knowledge Map Prediction for UAV-Assisted Communications With Uncertainty-Guided 3D Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00887)
*Zhihan Zeng,Amir Hussain,Yue Xiu,Phee Lep Yeoh,Lu Chen,Zhongpei Zhang,Guan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的条件预测框架，融合城市场景先验、稀疏多高度观测和目标高度描述符，实现面向无人机通信的跨高度信道知识地图（CKM）预测，并引入不确定性头和代价感知在线感知策略，在基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低空无人机在不同高度层获取信道知识时，通常仅能获得稀疏的观测数据，难以直接推断未观测高度上的密集信道信息。现有方法在跨高度CKM预测上性能不足，尤其在未见场景的零样本和小样本设定下表现有限，因此需要一种能够有效融合几何先验与稀疏观测的预测框架。

Method: 提出了一种几何感知的条件预测框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 融合城市场景先验（几何信息）、稀疏多高度观测和目标高度描述符；(2) 采用FPN-Transformer架构重建未观测目标高度的密集CKM；(3) 引入不确定性头来表征预测置信度，并设计代价感知的在线无人机感知策略，以在运动和安全性约束下支持主动感知。

Result: 在分层空中CKM基准上，FPN-Transformer在未见场景零样本和补丁随机协议下分别将RMSE降低至5.347dB和1.111dB，优于最强基线3D-RadioDiff（6.937dB和1.221dB）。10-shot两高度支持的小样本适应进一步将RMSE从5.347dB降至3.518dB。不确定性引导的代价感知感知策略在感知预算40时将主动重建误差从初始6.94dB降至4.79dB，优于仅不确定性感知（5.08dB）和随机采样（5.84dB）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的几何感知条件预测框架和FPN-Transformer能够有效利用场景先验和稀疏观测实现高精度跨高度CKM预测；不确定性引导的代价感知策略在无人机在线感知中具有显著优势，能够提升重建质量并降低感知成本。

Original Abstract: Low-altitude Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) often need to infer channel knowledge across a range of heights from only sparse observations collected at a few altitude layers. To address this challenge, this paper studies height-conditioned cross-height channel knowledge map (CKM) prediction for UAV-assisted communications in geometry-rich urban environments. We develop a geometry-aware conditional prediction framework that combines urban scene priors, sparse multi-altitude observations, and target-height descriptors to reconstruct dense CKMs at unobserved target heights. An uncertainty head is further introduced to characterize prediction confidence and to support cost-aware online UAV sensing under motion and safety constraints. Experiments on a layered aerial CKM benchmark show that the proposed Feature Pyramid Network (FPN)-Transformer achieves the best overall performance under both unseen-scene zero-shot and legacy patch-random protocols, reducing the Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) to 5.347dB and 1.111dB, respectively, compared with 6.937dB and 1.221dB for the strongest baseline 3D-RadioDiff. Moreover, after applying our unseen-scene few-shot adaptation, the RMSE further decreases from 5.347dB in zero-shot prediction to 3.518dB with 10-shot two-height support, while the uncertainty-guided cost-aware sensing policy improves active reconstruction from 6.94dB at initialization to 4.79dB at sensing budget 40, outperforming uncertainty-only sensing at 5.08dB and random aerial sampling at 5.84dB.

中文摘要翻译: 低空无人机（UAV）通常需要从仅在少数高度层收集的稀疏观测中推断跨高度范围的信道知识。为应对这一挑战，本文研究了面向几何丰富的城市环境中无人机辅助通信的、基于高度条件的跨高度信道知识地图（CKM）预测。我们开发了一种几何感知的条件预测框架，融合城市场景先验、稀疏多高度观测以及目标高度描述符，以重建未观测目标高度上的密集CKM。进一步引入不确定性头来表征预测置信度，并支持在运动和安全约束下进行代价感知的无人机在线感知。在分层空中CKM基准上的实验表明，所提出的特征金字塔网络（FPN）-Transformer在未见场景零样本和传统补丁随机两种协议下均取得了最佳整体性能，将均方根误差（RMSE）分别降低至5.347dB和1.111dB，而最强基线3D-RadioDiff分别为6.937dB和1.221dB。此外，在应用我们提出的未见场景小样本自适应后，RMSE从零样本预测的5.347dB进一步降低至10-shot两高度支持下的3.518dB；同时，不确定性引导的代价感知策略在感知预算为40时将主动重建误差从初始的6.94dB降至4.79dB，优于仅基于不确定性的感知（5.08dB）和随机空中采样（5.84dB）。

</details>


### [111] [DeWorldSG: Depth-Aware 3D Semantic Scene Graph Generation via World-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00889)
*Seok-Young Kim,Abdelrahman Elskhawy,Taewook Ha,Dooyoung Kim,Eunjae Shin,Benjamin Busam,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeWorldSG提出了一种从RGB-D序列生成时空鲁棒的3D语义场景图的新框架，通过实例级几何3D高斯分布表示对象，并利用时空证据聚合与世界模型先验来增强关系预测，在3DSSG和ReplicaSSG数据集上取得SoTA性能，三元组召回率提升77.4%，谓词召回率提升23.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法因3D对象表示不稳定和逐帧推理导致的关系缺失，难以构建可靠的3D场景图。

Method: 通过深度引导滤波估计实例级几何3D高斯分布，将每个对象表示为概率化3D节点而非单一投影点；跨对象对聚合时空证据，并利用世界模型(V-JEPA 2)的上下文先验精炼关系。

Result: 在3DSSG和ReplicaSSG数据集上，对象与谓词预测均达到SoTA性能，三元组召回率提升77.4%，谓词召回率提升23.2%，生成时空一致的场景结构。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了3D场景图构建中的表示不稳定和关系稀疏问题，适用于机器人操作和增强现实应用。

Original Abstract: We present DeWorldSG, a novel framework that generates spatio-temporally robust 3D Semantic Scene Graphs from RGB-D sequences. Existing methods often struggle to construct reliable 3D scene graphs due to unstable 3D object representations and missing relations caused by frame-wise inference. DeWorldSG addresses these issues by estimating instance-level geometric 3D Gaussian distributions through depth-guided filtering and representing each object as a probabilistic 3D node rather than a single projected point. To mitigate relational sparsity from frame-wise inference, our framework further aggregates spatiotemporal evidence across object pairs and refines relations using contextual priors derived from a world model (V-JEPA 2). Experiments on the 3DSSG and ReplicaSSG datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance in both object and predicate prediction, while producing temporally consistent scene structures. In particular, our method improves triplet recall by 77.4% and predicate recall by 23.2% over prior SoTA approaches, making it suitable for robotic manipulation and AR applications. Our code and models are open-sourced.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了DeWorldSG，一种从RGB-D序列生成时空鲁棒的3D语义场景图的新型框架。现有方法常因不稳定的3D对象表示和逐帧推理导致的关系缺失，难以构建可靠的3D场景图。DeWorldSG通过深度引导滤波估计实例级几何3D高斯分布，将每个对象表示为概率化3D节点而非单一投影点，从而解决了上述问题。为缓解逐帧推理带来的关系稀疏性，我们的框架进一步跨对象对聚合时空证据，并利用来自世界模型（V-JEPA 2）的上下文先验精炼关系。在3DSSG和ReplicaSSG数据集上的实验表明，该方法在对象和谓词预测上均达到当前最优性能，同时生成时空一致的场景结构。特别地，我们的方法相比先前最优方法将三元组召回率提升了77.4%，谓词召回率提升了23.2%，使其适用于机器人操作和增强现实应用。我们的代码和模型已开源。

</details>


### [112] [MG-RWKV: Multi-Grained Context-Aware RWKV for Temporal Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00902)
*Jingchen Ni,Cangjin Yu,Dan Jiang,Quan Zhang,Keyu Lv,Shannan Yan,Linyue Pan,Ke Zhang,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MG-RWKV，一种基于RWKV的多粒度时序伪造定位框架，以O(T)复杂度实现高效的全序列处理，并在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时序伪造定位方法受限于CNN的局部感受野、Transformer的二次复杂度，而线性模型难以平衡全局上下文压缩与局部突变感知。本文旨在解决这一效率与精度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出MG-RWKV框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) 双向RWKV架构，在无二次开销下捕获双向时序上下文；(2) 多粒度混合专家（MG-MoE），在显式时序感受野上进行动态路由，自适应选择粒度；(3) 跨粒度一致性（CGC），通过层级尺度配对和空间边界加权对齐相邻特征金字塔层，减少误报。

Result: 在Lav-DF、TVIL和Psynd数据集上的大量实验表明，MG-RWKV以低计算成本取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MG-RWKV通过多粒度框架有效平衡了全局上下文建模与局部伪造感知，实现了高效且可解释的时序伪造定位。

Original Abstract: Driven by Artificial Intelligence-Generated Content (AIGC), the authenticity of audio-visual content is facing severe challenges. Temporal Forgery Localization (TFL) aims to precisely identify manipulated segments within untrimmed sequences. However, existing methods are limited by CNNs' local receptive fields or Transformers' quadratic complexity, while emerging linear models often struggle to balance global authentic context compression with local abrupt forgery perception. To address this, we propose MG-RWKV, a multi-granularity framework that leverages the data-dependent state evolution of RWKV to achieve efficient full-sequence processing with O(T) complexity. Our framework features three core innovations: (1) a Bidirectional RWKV architecture that captures bidirectional temporal contexts without quadratic overhead; (2) a Multi-Granularity Mixture of Experts (MG-MoE) that performs dynamic routing over explicit temporal receptive fields, adaptively selecting granularities based on forgery duration to significantly enhance decision interpretability; and (3) Cross-Granularity Consistency (CGC), which aligns adjacent feature pyramid levels through hierarchical scale-wise pairing and spatial boundary-aware weighting, effectively reducing false positives in authentic regions. Extensive experiments on Lav-DF, TVIL, and Psynd datasets demonstrate that MG-RWKV achieves state-of-the-art performance with low computational cost.

中文摘要翻译: 在人工智能生成内容（AIGC）的驱动下，音视频内容的真实性正面临严峻挑战。时序伪造定位（TFL）旨在精确识别未修剪序列中被篡改的片段。然而，现有方法受限于CNN的局部感受野或Transformer的二次复杂度，而新兴的线性模型往往难以平衡全局真实上下文的压缩与局部突变伪造的感知。为解决这一问题，我们提出了MG-RWKV，一个多粒度框架，利用RWKV的数据依赖状态演化实现O(T)复杂度的高效全序列处理。我们的框架包含三项核心创新：(1) 双向RWKV架构，在无二次开销下捕获双向时序上下文；(2) 多粒度混合专家（MG-MoE），在显式时序感受野上进行动态路由，基于伪造持续时间自适应选择粒度，显著增强决策可解释性；(3) 跨粒度一致性（CGC），通过层级尺度配对和空间边界感知加权对齐相邻特征金字塔层级，有效降低真实区域中的误报。在Lav-DF、TVIL和Psynd数据集上的大量实验表明，MG-RWKV以低计算成本实现了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [113] [Condensing Large-Scale Datasets Directly with Minimal Information Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00916)
*Xinyi Shang,Peng Sun,Bei Shi,Zixuan Wang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIM框架，通过直接对齐原始与合成数据分布来替代双压缩范式，解决了数据集蒸馏中信息损失和跨架构泛化差的问题，在ImageNet-1K IPC=10上达到48.7% Top-1准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于解耦信息提取管线的数据集蒸馏方法存在双压缩过程（数据→模型→图像），导致严重信息损失和分布偏移，进而使RELABEL策略失效（预训练模型成为不可靠标注器），同时存在计算开销大、跨架构泛化差的问题。

Method: 提出CIM（度量驱动框架），摒弃双压缩范式，通过直接对齐原始数据集与合成数据集之间的数据分布，显式量化并最小化信息差距，确保高保真信息凝聚，从而天然满足有效重标注的前提条件。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，IPC=10时仅用单张RTX-4090 GPU训练80分钟，ResNet-18上Top-1准确率达48.7%，比NRR-DD和DELT分别高出2.6%和2.9%，达到新的最优性能。

Conclusion: CIM通过直接分布对齐替代双压缩范式，从根本上解决了信息损失问题，实现了更高效、更泛化的数据集蒸馏，在性能上大幅超越现有方法。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in scaling dataset distillation rely heavily on decoupled information extraction pipelines, comprising SQUEEZE, RECOVER, and RELABEL stages. Despite their scalability to large-scale datasets, these methods suffer from prohibitive computational overhead and poor cross-architecture generalization. In this paper, we reveal the root cause of these bottlenecks: the implicit dual-compression process, from data to model and back to images, inherently induces severe information loss. Crucially, we empirically and theoretically demonstrate that this loss creates a distribution shift that fundamentally compromises the widely adopted RELABEL strategy, transforming the pre-trained model into an unreliable labeler that yields sub-optimal labels. To overcome these critical flaws, we propose CIM, a novel, metric-driven framework that abandons the flawed dual-compression paradigm. Instead, CIM explicitly quantifies and minimizes the information gap between the original and synthetic datasets. By directly aligning the data distributions, our approach ensures high-fidelity information condensation and inherently satisfies the prerequisites for effective relabeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CIM establishes a new state-of-the-art. Notably, it distills ImageNet-1K at an IPC=10 in merely 80 minutes on a single RTX-4090 GPU, achieving an unprecedented 48.7% Top-1 accuracy on ResNet-18 and significantly outperforming previous SOTA approaches, such as NRR-DD and DELT, by 2.6% and 2.9%, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/LINs-lab/CIM.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，数据集蒸馏（dataset distillation）领域的进展高度依赖于解耦信息提取管线（decoupled information extraction pipelines），该管线包含SQUEEZE、RECOVER和RELABEL三个阶段。尽管这些方法可扩展到大规模数据集，但其计算开销巨大且跨架构泛化能力差。本文揭示了这些瓶颈的根本原因：从数据到模型再回图像的隐式双压缩过程（dual-compression process）本质上会导致严重的信息损失。关键的是，我们通过实验和理论证明，这种信息损失会造成分布偏移，从根本上破坏了广泛采用的RELABEL策略，使预训练模型成为产生次优标签的不可靠标注器。为克服这些关键缺陷，我们提出CIM，一种新颖的度量驱动框架，摒弃了有缺陷的双压缩范式。相反，CIM显式量化并最小化原始数据集与合成数据集之间的信息差距。通过直接对齐数据分布，我们的方法确保了高保真信息凝聚，并天然满足有效重标注的前提条件。大量实验表明，CIM取得了新的最优性能。值得注意的是，它能在单张RTX-4090 GPU上仅用80分钟蒸馏ImageNet-1K（IPC=10），在ResNet-18上达到前所未有的48.7% Top-1准确率，显著优于此前的最优方法NRR-DD和DELT，分别高出2.6%和2.9%。我们的代码已开源：https://github.com/LINs-lab/CIM。

</details>


### [114] [GMO-E$^2$DIT: Grounded Multi-Operation Editing for E-Commerce Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00920)
*Zipeng Guo,Xiaoan Liu,Lichen Ma,Cheng Wang,Yu He,Xiaolong Fu,Jingling Fu,Xinyuan Shan,Shaojie Guo,Luohang Liu,Junshi Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GMO-E²DIT，一种将VLM与掩码条件图像编辑器结合的智能编辑框架，通过解耦认知推理与生成渲染、迭代执行子任务的方式，实现多轮、局部、可审计的电商图像编辑，在指令准确性和编辑保真度上超越现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单次图像编辑器将意图解析、空间定位与图像合成耦合在单一阶段，在商业电商场景中经常出现部分执行失败的问题，无法满足多局部、可审计的实际需求。需要一种能够解耦认知推理与生成渲染、支持迭代纠错的编辑框架。

Method: 提出GMO-E²DIT智能编辑框架：1) 使用VLM智能体构建区域锚定的编辑议程，将认知推理与生成渲染解耦；2) 通过操作感知掩码和参考图像执行子程序；3) 引入反射驱动循环，检查中间结果并决定下一步状态，实现安全保留部分进展、重试未完成操作和错误恢复。

Result: 在EComEditBench基准上，GMO-E²DIT在指令准确性和编辑保真度方面优于现有基线，与强封闭源模型相比达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: GMO-E²DIT通过智能体化的迭代编辑框架有效解决了多轮局部编辑中的部分执行失败问题，为商业电商场景提供了可靠、可审计的编辑方案。

Original Abstract: Real-world e-commerce image editing often requires multiple, localized, and auditable operations rather than global restyling. This compositional nature poses a dual challenge: models must precisely apply all requested edits to the correct regions while preserving unmodified content, even under ambiguous instructions. Existing one-shot editors conflate intent resolution, spatial grounding, and synthesis into a single step, frequently resulting in partial execution failures, which is unacceptable for commercial scenarios. To address this, we introduce GMO-E$^2$DIT, an agentic editing framework that couples a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with a mask-conditioned image editor to tackle structured multi-turn task completion. Given an underspecified instruction, the VLM agent constructs a region-grounded edit agenda, effectively decoupling cognitive reasoning from generative rendering. The framework then executes sub-programs via operation-aware masks and references, utilizing a reflection-driven loop to inspect intermediate results and determine the subsequent state. This iterative mechanism reliably preserves safe partial progress, retries unfinished operations, and recovers from errors. Furthermore, we develop a unified data pipeline providing aligned supervision for planning, execution, and reflection, alongside EComEditBench, a comprehensive benchmark for instruction-driven evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMO-E$^2$DIT achieves competitive performance compared to strong closed-source models, yielding superior instruction accuracy and edit fidelity over existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的电商图像编辑通常需要多重、局部且可审计的操作，而非全局风格迁移。这种组合性质带来了双重挑战：模型必须精准地将所有请求的编辑应用到正确区域，同时在模糊指令下保留未修改的内容。现有的单次编辑器将意图解析、空间定位与图像合成融合在单一阶段，经常导致部分执行失败，这在商业场景中是难以接受的。为解决此问题，我们提出了GMO-E²DIT，一种智能体编辑框架，它将视觉语言模型（VLM）与掩码条件图像编辑器相结合，以处理结构化多轮任务完成。给定一条欠明确的指令，VLM智能体构建一个区域锚定的编辑议程，有效解耦认知推理与生成渲染。该框架随后通过操作感知掩码和参考执行子程序，利用反射驱动循环检查中间结果并决定下一步状态。这种迭代机制能够可靠地保留安全的部分进展、重试未完成的操作并从错误中恢复。此外，我们开发了一套统一的数据管线，为规划、执行和反射提供对齐的监督信号，并构建了EComEditBench这一全面的指令驱动评估基准。大量实验表明，GMO-E²DIT在与强封闭源模型相比时取得了竞争性性能，在指令准确性和编辑保真度上优于现有基线。

</details>


### [115] [Post-Training Pruning for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00927)
*Chengzhi Hu,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Mengjuan Chen,Zhikai Li,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiT-Pruning，一种针对扩散变换器（DiTs）的定制化剪枝方法。通过设计基于能量的显著性度量（平衡权重与激活贡献）和聚类感知的剪枝粒度，解决了传统剪枝方法因DiTs独特架构和参数分布导致性能严重下降的问题。实验表明，在50%稀疏度下，该方法在FLUX.1-dev上CLIP分数仅损失0.001，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散变换器（DiTs）在图像生成中性能优异但计算开销巨大。后训练剪枝是一种有前景的解决方案，但由于DiTs独特的架构设计、参数分布（权重幅值远大于LLM）以及现有剪枝方法（针对LLM开发）通过一系列近似推导度量指标放大了权重的相对贡献，导致传统剪枝方法不适用且性能严重下降。此外，现有剪枝粒度忽视了模型结构的差异。

Method: 提出DiT-Pruning方法，包含两个核心创新：(1) 设计了一种基于能量视角的新型显著性度量，平衡权重和激活的贡献，更有效识别重要元素；(2) 观察到二维权重空间中的聚类模式，据此采用聚类感知的剪枝粒度，实现有效的稀疏分配。

Result: 在多种DiTs架构上的大量评估表明，该方法能一致地保持图像质量，尤其是在高稀疏度下。在MJHQ数据集上以512x512分辨率测试FLUX.1-dev模型，50%稀疏度下CLIP分数仅损失0.001，大幅优于近期剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DiT-Pruning通过定制化的显著性标准和剪枝粒度，有效解决了传统剪枝方法在扩散变换器上不适用的问题，实现了在保持图像质量前提下的高效模型压缩，尤其在高稀疏度场景下表现出色。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have demonstrated impressive performance in image generation but suffer from substantial computational overhead and resource consumption. Post-training pruning offers a promising solution; however, due to DiTs' unique architectural design and parameter distribution, traditional pruning methods are inapplicable, leading to significant performance degradation. Specifically, prior methods developed for LLMs, which derive metrics through a series of approximations, amplify the relative contribution of weights in the saliency metric. In addition, weights in DiTs exhibit significantly larger magnitudes than those in LLMs. Moreover, existing pruning granularity overlooks variations in model structures. In this paper, we propose DiT-Pruning, which improves pruning performance by introducing customized saliency criteria and pruning granularity. We design a novel metric that balances the contributions of weights and activations from an energy-based perspective, enabling more effective identification of important elements. Furthermore, we observe distinct clustering patterns in the two-dimensional weight space. Accordingly, we adopt a clustering-aware pruning granularity, enabling effective sparse allocation. Extensive evaluations on various DiTs show that our method consistently preserves image quality, especially under high sparsity. For FLUX.1-dev at 512x512 resolution on MJHQ, DiT-Pruning achieves only a 0.001 loss in CLIP score at 50% sparsity, dramatically outperforming recent pruning methods.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散变换器（DiTs）在图像生成中展现了令人印象深刻的性能，但面临着巨大的计算开销和资源消耗。后训练剪枝提供了一种有前景的解决方案；然而，由于DiTs独特的架构设计和参数分布，传统剪枝方法不适用，导致显著的性能下降。具体而言，先前为LLMs开发的方法通过一系列近似推导度量指标，放大了显著性度量中权重的相对贡献。此外，DiTs中的权重表现出比LLMs中显著更大的幅值。而且，现有剪枝粒度忽视了模型结构的差异。本文提出DiT-Pruning，通过引入定制化的显著性标准和剪枝粒度来提升剪枝性能。我们设计了一种新颖的度量指标，从基于能量的视角平衡权重和激活的贡献，从而更有效地识别重要元素。此外，我们观察到二维权重空间中存在不同的聚类模式。据此，我们采用聚类感知的剪枝粒度，实现有效的稀疏分配。在多种DiTs上的大量评估表明，我们的方法能一致地保持图像质量，尤其是在高稀疏度下。对于在MJHQ上以512x512分辨率运行的FLUX.1-dev，DiT-Pruning在50%稀疏度下仅达到0.001的CLIP分数损失，大幅优于最近的剪枝方法。

</details>


### [116] [Dataset Biases and Shortcut Learning in Motion-Based AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00948)
*Joren Michels,Lode Jorissen,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种最先进的基于运动的AI生成视频检测器，发现其性能严重依赖于数据集的运动偏差（AI视频帧间运动较少），一旦消除该偏差，所有检测器性能降至随机水平；而基于频率的检测器展现出更强的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近年来AI生成视频的视觉质量大幅提升，使得人眼难以区分真实与合成视频。现有基于运动的检测器声称性能优异，但本文怀疑其高表现可能源于数据集中的系统性偏差而非真正的泛化能力，因此旨在揭示这些漏洞并推动更鲁棒的检测方法发展。

Method: 1) 评估四种SOTA基于运动的AI生成视频检测器；2) 识别其预处理和采样偏差，并量化这些偏差对性能的贡献；3) 在消除运动偏差的数据集上重新评估；4) 通过数据集重平衡和简单空间数据增强测试模型性能变化；5) 与基于频率的检测器进行对比实验。

Result: 1) 基于运动的检测器高度依赖于评估数据集中的运动模式偏差（AI视频帧间运动较少）；2) 在无此偏差的数据集上，所有基于运动的检测器性能降至接近随机水平；3) 数据集重平衡和简单空间增强导致所有基于运动的检测器性能严重下降；4) 基于频率的检测器在所有评估数据集上保持强性能。

Conclusion: 基于运动的AI生成视频检测器存在严重的泛化缺陷，其高性能主要来自数据集中的运动偏差而非真实检测能力；基于频率的检测方法可能是更具泛化性的替代方案。呼吁建立更代表性、无偏差的数据集和更鲁棒的评估协议。

Original Abstract: The visual quality of AI-generated videos has improved drastically in recent years, making it increasingly difficult for humans to distinguish between real and synthetic media. In this work, we evaluate the robustness and applicability of four state-of-the-art motion-based AI-generated video detectors. We identify significant preprocessing and sampling biases in these methods and demonstrate that they account for a substantial portion of their reported performance. Furthermore, we find that these detectors are highly sensitive to motion patterns specific to their evaluation datasets, where AI-generated videos generally exhibit less inter-frame movement than real videos. We show that for all detectors, performance collapses to near-random levels when evaluated on a dataset that does not contain this motion bias. Additionally, through dataset rebalancing and the application of simple spatial augmentations, we observe severe performance degradation across all evaluated models. In contrast, we find that an existing frequency-based detector maintains strong performance across all evaluated datasets, suggesting that frequency-based approaches may offer a more generalizable path forward for AI-generated video detection. We hope that our work raises awareness towards these vulnerabilities and encourages the development of more representative, unbiased datasets and more robust evaluation protocols.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，AI生成视频的视觉质量得到了极大提升，使得人类越来越难以区分真实媒体与合成媒体。在这项工作中，我们评估了四种最先进的基于运动的AI生成视频检测器的鲁棒性和适用性。我们识别出这些方法中存在显著的预处理和采样偏差，并证明这些偏差对其报告性能的很大一部分负责。此外，我们发现这些检测器对其评估数据集特有的运动模式高度敏感，其中AI生成视频通常比真实视频表现出更少的帧间运动。我们表明，当在不存在这种运动偏差的数据集上进行评估时，所有检测器的性能都降至接近随机水平。此外，通过数据集重平衡和简单空间数据增强的应用，我们观察到所有被评估模型的性能都出现严重退化。相比之下，我们发现一种现有的基于频率的检测器在所有评估数据集上保持了强劲的性能，这表明基于频率的方法可能为AI生成视频检测提供一条更具泛化性的前进道路。我们希望我们的工作能够提高对这些漏洞的认识，并鼓励开发更具代表性、无偏差的数据集以及更鲁棒的评估协议。

</details>


### [117] [Learning Cardiac Motion Priors for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00955)
*Andrew Bell,George Webber,Andrew P King,Steffen E Petersen,Muhummad Sohaib Nazir,Alistair Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种学习心脏运动隐式神经表示先验的策略（群体先验、共识先验、自解码器和元学习），发现所有学习先验均优于随机初始化，其中元学习在早期性能和50次迭代的适应轨迹上表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 隐式神经表示(INR)适合心脏运动估计，但将其拟合到每个图像序列耗时且对优化轨迹敏感；学习先验可引导优化并加速适应，但心脏运动INR的学习先验方法尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 比较了四种学习心脏运动先验的策略：(1) 通过联合优化学习的群体先验；(2) 通过权重平均获得的共识先验；(3) 自解码器；(4) 元学习。使用UK Biobank的短轴标记心脏磁共振图像进行评估。

Result: 所有学习先验相比随机初始化均显著提升了早期适应性能；共识先验简单有效，自解码器在早期适应中能更快恢复大变形；元学习在早期表现强劲，并在50次迭代中保持最佳适应轨迹。

Conclusion: 学习先验能有效提升心脏运动INR的适应性能，其中元学习策略综合表现最优。

Original Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) are well suited to cardiac motion estimation, providing continuous, compact representations of motion fields. However, fitting an INR to each image sequence is time-consuming and sensitive to the optimisation trajectory. Learned priors can help guide optimisation towards plausible motion fields and enable faster adaptation, but learning priors for cardiac motion INRs remains under-explored. In this work, we compare four strategies for learning cardiac motion priors, including a population prior learned by joint optimisation, a consensus prior obtained by weight averaging, auto-decoders, and meta-learning. Using short-axis tagged cardiac magnetic resonance images from the UK Biobank, we evaluate their impact on tracking accuracy, motion behaviour, and adaptation trajectory.
  All learned priors substantially improved early adaptation performance compared with random initialisation. While the simple consensus prior was effective, auto-decoders recovered large deformations faster during early adaptation. Meta-learning achieved strong early performance and maintained the best adaptation trajectory over 50 iterations.

中文摘要翻译: 隐式神经表示(INR)非常适合心脏运动估计，能够提供运动场的连续、紧凑表示。然而，将INR拟合到每个图像序列既耗时又对优化轨迹敏感。学习先验有助于引导优化朝着合理的运动场方向进行，并实现更快的适应，但针对心脏运动INR的学习先验方法仍未被充分探索。本文比较了四种学习心脏运动先验的策略，包括通过联合优化学习的群体先验、通过权重平均获得的共识先验、自解码器以及元学习。利用UK Biobank的短轴标记心脏磁共振图像，我们评估了这些策略在跟踪精度、运动行为和适应轨迹方面的影响。与随机初始化相比，所有学习先验均显著提升了早期适应性能。虽然简单的共识先验效果良好，但自解码器在早期适应过程中能更快地恢复大变形。元学习在早期表现强劲，并在50次迭代中保持了最佳的适应轨迹。

</details>


### [118] [GaussianEmoTalker: Real-Time Emotional Talking Head Synthesis with Audio-Driven and Blendshape-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00959)
*Haijie Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yixuan Dong,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GaussianEmoTalker，一种基于3D高斯泼溅的实时情感说话人脸生成框架，通过中性到情感的残差变形方式实现可控强度的情感表达。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话人脸合成在唇同步和视觉质量上已有显著进展，但生成具有可控强度的情感表情头像仍具挑战，尤其是在实时约束下。

Method: 首先构建身份特定的中性说话空间（GaussianBlendshapes），提供高保真高斯属性和音素同步的中性运动；然后通过结合网格位移线索、音频特征、情感类别和强度编码，预测情感条件化的残差变形；最后引入空间-音频-情感注意力模块融合异构信号，估计高斯属性偏移以实现表达性和时间稳定的渲染。

Result: 实验表明，与近期情感说话头方法相比，GaussianEmoTalker在视频质量、唇同步精度、可控情感表达和实时渲染方面均具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于中性到情感残差变形范式的实时情感说话人脸合成框架，有效实现了高保真度、唇同步、可控情感表达和实时性能。

Original Abstract: Audio-driven talking head synthesis has achieved impressive progress in lip synchronization and visual quality, yet generating expressive emotional avatars with controllable intensity remains challenging, especially under real-time constraints. In this paper, we present GaussianEmoTalker, an audio-driven framework for real-time emotional talking head synthesis based on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Instead of directly predicting the final emotional avatar from speech, we formulate emotional animation as a neutral-to-emotional residual deformation problem. GaussianEmoTalker first constructs an identity-specific neutral talking space with GaussianBlendshapes, which provides high-fidelity Gaussian attributes and phoneme-synchronized neutral motion. It then predicts an emotion-conditioned residual deformation by combining mesh displacement cues, audio features, emotion categories, and intensity encodings. To fuse these heterogeneous signals, we introduce a spatial-audio-emotion attention module that estimates the offsets of Gaussian attributes for expressive and temporally stable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GaussianEmoTalker achieves competitive video quality, accurate lip synchronization, controllable emotional expression, and real-time rendering compared with recent emotional talking head methods. Our project page is available at https://njust-yang.github.io/GaussianEmoTalker.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 音频驱动的说话人脸合成在唇部同步和视觉质量方面已经取得了显著进展，但在实时约束下生成具有可控强度的表情化情感虚拟形象仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们提出了GaussianEmoTalker，一种基于3D高斯泼溅的音频驱动实时情感说话人脸合成框架。与直接从语音预测最终情感虚拟形象不同，我们将情感动画建模为一个从中性到情感的残差变形问题。GaussianEmoTalker首先构建一个身份特定的中性说话空间（GaussianBlendshapes），该空间提供高保真度的高斯属性以及音素同步的中性运动。然后，它通过结合网格位移线索、音频特征、情感类别和强度编码，预测一个情感条件化的残差变形。为了融合这些异构信号，我们引入了一个空间-音频-情感注意力模块，用于估计高斯属性的偏移，从而实现富有表现力且时间稳定的渲染。大量实验表明，与近期情感说话头方法相比，GaussianEmoTalker在竞争性的视频质量、精确的唇部同步、可控的情感表达以及实时渲染方面均表现出色。我们的项目页面可在 https://njust-yang.github.io/GaussianEmoTalker.github.io/ 访问。

</details>


### [119] [Slope-Guided Mamba and Angular-Refined Transformer for Light Field Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00965)
*Li Jin,Jian Huang,Junde Lu,Shuai Wang,Hao Sheng,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SMART混合网络，结合Slope-Guided Mamba和Angular-Refined Transformer，通过角调制空间模块和流形对齐轨迹模块解决光场超分辨率中空间-角度相关性建模和扫描-几何失配问题，在五个基准上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有光场超分辨率方法存在两个主要局限：一是空间和角度维度解耦建模，限制了早期跨维度交互导致几何不一致；二是连续序列建模的刚性扫描机制与极线几何冲突，限制了几何感知特征聚合。

Method: 提出SMART混合网络，包含两个核心模块：(1) 角调制空间模块(Angular-Modulated Spatial Module)，引入角度先验弥合解耦鸿沟，增强空间-角度相关性建模；(2) 流形对齐轨迹模块(Manifold-Aligned Trajectory Module)，实现沿极线结构的几何一致序列建模，缓解扫描-几何失配问题。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上取得最先进性能，PSNR相比前人方法提升0.42 dB，且伪影显著减少。

Conclusion: 通过混合Mamba和Transformer架构，结合角调制空间模块和流形对齐轨迹模块，有效解决了光场超分辨率中的空间-角度解耦和扫描-几何失配两大挑战。

Original Abstract: Light Field Super-Resolution (LFSR) necessitates accurate modeling of spatial-angular correlations while preserving intrinsic 4D ray coherence. However, maintaining such high-dimensional consistency remains challenging, primarily due to two inherent limitations in prevailing modeling paradigms. First, spatial and angular dimensions are often modeled in a decoupled manner, restricting early cross-dimensional interaction and leading to geometric inconsistencies. Moreover, although continuous sequence modeling paradigms show promise in representing epipolar structures, their rigid scanning mechanisms fundamentally conflict with epipolar geometry, limiting geometry-aware feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose a hybrid light field super-resolution network, termed SMART, which integrates a Slope-Guided Mamba and an Angular-Refined Transformer to effectively overcome these limitations. Specifically, we introduce an angular-modulated spatial module to bridge the decoupling gap, incorporating angular priors to strengthen spatial-angular correlation modeling. To mitigate the scan-geometry mismatch, we propose a manifold-aligned trajectory module that enables geometry-consistent sequence modeling along epipolar structures. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that SMART achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing previous methods by 0.42 dB (PSNR) with significantly reduced artifacts.
