To address this, we propose projected quantum kernels and classical kernel approximation techniques that reduce feature dimensionality while preserving key quantum properties. Using these approximate kernels, we develop misspecified GP bandit algorithms and derive regret bounds that characterize the trade-off between approximation error and information gain. The regret bounds provide principled guidance for selecting the optimal model complexity. Empirically, our methods outperform full quantum kernels in sample efficiency, while substantially reducing computational overhead, enabling scalable GP optimization for quantum-native applications.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了基于量子核的高斯过程（GP） bandit优化问题，假设平均奖励函数位于量子核所诱导的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中。该设定受到NISQ时代任务的启发，例如量子控制、态制备和变分量子算法。虽然量子核可以通过领域特定的归纳偏置提供“量子优势”，但直接使用完整的高维量子核会显著增加模型复杂度和信息增益，导致更高的累积遗憾和较差的可学习性。为解决该问题，我们提出了投影量子核和经典核近似技术，在降低特征维度的同时保留关键的量子属性。利用这些近似核，我们开发了误设定（misspecified）的GP bandit算法，并推导了刻画近似误差与信息增益之间权衡的遗憾界。这些遗憾界为选择最优模型复杂度提供了原则性指导。实验结果表明，我们的方法在样本效率上优于完整量子核，同时大幅降低了计算开销，从而为量子原生应用实现了可扩展的GP优化。

</details>


### [212] [When Context Compensates for Sparse Event History: AlphaEarth for Spatio-Temporal Point-Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01082)
*Yahya Aalaila,Mouad Elhamdi,Gerrit Großmann,Daniel Jenson,Elizaveta Semenova,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了在局部事件历史稀疏时，外生空间上下文（AlphaEarth嵌入）能否提升时空点过程模型的空间迁移性能。实验表明，上下文信息在短历史长度下可带来2-6倍的预测提升，长历史下仍有10-20%的改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时空点过程模型在事件历史稀疏时难以泛化到新区域，需要探究外生空间上下文能否补偿历史数据不足带来的性能下降。

Method: 以固定的对数高斯Cox过程为骨干，构建两个对比模型：仅基于事件历史的模型，以及在其基础上增加AlphaEarth嵌入作为线性空间上下文的增强模型。在8个留出区域上进行急救医疗服务（EMS）预测评估，跨不同历史长度w进行对比。

Result: AlphaEarth嵌入在所有历史长度下均提升了区域外预测性能，其中在历史稀疏（1-2周）时改进最为显著（约2-6倍），在历史较长（20-104周）时仍有约10-20%的提升。

Conclusion: 空间上下文信息能够在事件历史有限的条件下显著稳定时空点过程模型的空间迁移预测性能。

Original Abstract: Spatio-temporal point-process models must often generalise across space when local event histories are sparse. We study whether exogenous spatial context can compensate in such regimes. Using a fixed log-Gaussian Cox process backbone, we compare an event-only model with the same model augmented by AlphaEarth embeddings as linear spatial context. We evaluate spatial transfer on emergency medical services (EMS) forecasting across eight held-out regions, fixed forecast anchors, and a sweep over history length $w$, using only AlphaEarth (AE) embeddings available strictly before each anchor. AE improves out-of-region predictive performance across all history regimes, with the largest gains under scarce histories: approximately $2$--$6\times$ multiplicative improvements at $1-2$ weeks, tapering to roughly $10$--$20\%$ at $w=20$--$104$ weeks. These results show that contextual information can substantially stabilise spatially transferred point-process forecasts when event history is limited.

中文摘要翻译: 时空点过程模型在局部事件历史稀疏时通常需要跨空间进行泛化。我们研究了外生空间上下文能否在此类场景中提供补偿。以固定的对数高斯Cox过程为骨干，本文将仅基于事件历史的模型与在相同模型基础上添加AlphaEarth嵌入作为线性空间上下文的增强模型进行对比。我们在急救医疗服务（EMS）预测任务上评估空间迁移性能，涉及八个留出区域、固定的预测锚点以及历史长度w的遍历。实验中仅使用严格在预测锚点之前可获取的AlphaEarth（AE）嵌入。结果表明，AE在所有历史长度下均提升了区域外预测性能，其中在历史稀疏时增益最大：在1-2周历史长度下实现了约2-6倍的乘法改进，在w=20-104周时长下衰减至约10-20%。这些结果表明，在事件历史有限的条件下，上下文信息能够显著稳定空间迁移的时空点过程预测。

</details>


### [213] [Staleness-Learning Rate Scaling Laws for Asynchronous RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01083)
*Jingwei Song,Haofeng Xu,Jie Xiao,Chengke Bao,Jingwei Shi,Pengbin Feng,Weixun Wang,Yuhang Han,Chuan Wu,Linfeng Zhang,Bill Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了异步GRPO中陈旧 rollout 带来的偏差与稳定性问题，推导了偏差上界和条件性崩溃时间标度律，并给出了双约束稳定性条件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高吞吐RLHF系统常因解耦rollout生成与策略优化而导致学习器使用过时样本，然而这种陈旧性对算法稳定性的影响尚缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 将行为策略显式引入GRPO代理目标，区分代理梯度映射与分布依赖总体目标的真实全导数；在局部有界性、分布平滑性和行为策略平滑性假设下，理论推导陈旧rollout带来的每步梯度偏差阶数，并推导条件性崩溃时间标度律。

Result: 1) 陈旧rollout引入的每步代理梯度偏差为O(S * η)，其中S为最大rollout滞后，η为学习率；2) 当周期内漂移低于批裁剪半径时，崩溃由累积学习器漂移T * η主导；当陈旧rollout约束激活时，稳定性显式依赖于S * η；3) 导出双约束稳定性条件η << min{R_batch/(S * G_upd), R_crit/(T * G_upd)}。

Conclusion: 该理论解释了在有限视野范围内最大稳定学习率可能对陈旧性呈现弱依赖性的现象，为理解异步RLHF系统的稳定性提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: High-throughput RLHF systems often decouple rollout generation from policy optimization, leading to the use of stale rollouts during learner updates. In this work, we study the effect of such staleness in asynchronous GRPO. We make the behavior policy explicit in the GRPO surrogate objective and distinguish between the surrogate-gradient mapping used by the learner and the true total derivative of a distribution-dependent population objective. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and behavior-policy smoothness, we show that stale rollouts introduce a per-step surrogate-gradient bias of order O(S * eta), where S denotes the maximum rollout lag and eta denotes the learning rate. We further derive a conditional collapse-time scaling law: when within-cycle drift remains below a batch-level clipping radius, collapse is governed primarily by cumulative learner drift T * eta; when the stale-rollout constraint is active, stability instead depends explicitly on S * eta. This yields a two-constraint stability condition eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}, explaining why the maximum stable learning rate may appear weakly dependent on staleness in the horizon-limited regime.

中文摘要翻译: 高吞吐量RLHF系统通常将 rollout 生成与策略优化解耦，导致学习器在更新过程中使用过时的 rollout。在这项工作中，我们研究了异步GRPO中这种陈旧性的影响。我们将行为策略显式地引入GRPO代理目标，并区分学习器所使用的代理梯度映射与分布依赖的总体目标的真实全导数。在局部有界性、分布平滑性和行为策略平滑性的假设下，我们证明陈旧 rollout 会引入每步代理梯度偏差，其阶数为 O(S * η)，其中 S 表示最大 rollout 滞后，η 表示学习率。我们进一步推导出一个条件性崩溃时间标度律：当周期内漂移低于批级别裁剪半径时，崩溃主要由累积学习器漂移 T * η 主导；当陈旧 rollout 约束被激活时，稳定性则显式地依赖于 S * η。这导出了一个双约束稳定性条件 η << min{R_batch/(S * G_upd), R_crit/(T * G_upd)}，解释了为什么在有限视野范围内，最大稳定学习率可能对陈旧性呈现弱依赖性。

</details>


### [214] [CausalMix: Data Mixture as Causal Inference for Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01104)
*Zinan Tang,Yukun Zhang,Shaomian Zheng,Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Dingnan Jin,Jun Zhou,Yujun Wang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalMix 将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题，通过估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）动态推断最优数据混合比例，克服了传统方法在数据分布变化时需要从头重新训练的局限性，实验表明其在一系列下游任务上优于 RegMix 等基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据混合优化方法依赖静态数据分布假设，当数据池发生变化时需要昂贵的从头重新训练，无法从小的设置无缝扩展到更大的数据池和模型规模。

Method: 将数据池的统计特征作为协变量、域混合比例作为处理变量，在 Qwen2.5-0.5B 上进行 512 次运行拟合因果模型以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），然后外推得到 800K 数据池的最优混合比例并用于训练 7B 模型。该框架还成功泛化到 Qwen3-4B-Base 的长思维链数据。

Result: 在多个下游任务上，CausalMix 引导的混合比例一致地提升了模型性能，优于 RegMix 及其他基线方法；并通过 CATE Interpreter 对学习到的混合策略进行了可视化分析。

Conclusion: CausalMix 提供了一个因果且可解释的框架，通过因果建模隔离混杂偏差，动态推断状态依赖的最优数据混合比例，有效解决了数据分布变化时的可扩展性问题。

Original Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) training, data mixing plays a pivotal role in determining model performance. Recent methods optimize mixture weights via proxy models, but they rely on the assumption of static data distributions. As a result, when the underlying data pool shifts, these methods require costly retraining from scratch. This limitation restricts their ability to scale seamlessly from small settings to larger data pools and model sizes. In this paper, we propose CausalMix to address this limitation by casting data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem. We formulate the statistical features of the data pool as covariates and the domain mixture as the treatment. After fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), we extrapolate the optimal mixture for an 800K data pool and apply it to train a 7B model. Furthermore, we successfully generalize the framework to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. By leveraging causal modeling to isolate confounding biases, CausalMix dynamically infers state-dependent optimal data mixtures. Extensive experiments show that the mixture guided by CausalMix consistently improves performance across multiple downstream tasks, outperforming RegMix and other baselines. In addition, we use the CATE Interpreter to provide visual analysis of the learned mixing strategy. Overall, CausalMix offers a causal and interpretable framework for optimizing LLM data mixtures.

中文摘要翻译: 在大语言模型（LLM）训练中，数据混合对模型性能起着关键作用。现有方法通过代理模型优化混合权重，但它们依赖于静态数据分布的假设。因此，当底层数据池发生偏移时，这些方法需要昂贵的从头重新训练。这一局限性限制了它们从小规模设置无缝扩展到更大数据池和模型规模的能力。本文提出 CausalMix 以解决该局限，将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题。我们将数据池的统计特征形式化为协变量，将域混合形式化为处理变量。在 Qwen2.5-0.5B 上拟合因果模型进行 512 次运行以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）后，我们外推得到 800K 数据池的最优混合比例，并将其应用于训练 7B 模型。此外，我们成功将该框架泛化到 Qwen3-4B-Base 上的长思维链数据。通过利用因果建模隔离混杂偏差，CausalMix 动态推断状态依赖的最优数据混合比例。大量实验表明，CausalMix 引导的混合比例在多个下游任务上一致地提升了性能，优于 RegMix 及其他基线方法。同时，我们使用 CATE Interpreter 对学习到的混合策略提供可视化分析。总体而言，CausalMix 为优化 LLM 数据混合提供了一个因果且可解释的框架。

</details>


### [215] [SynLaD: Latent Diffusion for Generating Synthesizable Molecules Conditioned on 3D Pharmacophore Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01105)
*Miruna Cretu,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Saeed Saremi,Omar Mahmood,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Vishnu Sresht,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynLaD 是一个统一配体药物设计目标与合成可行性的潜扩散框架，通过将3D结构解码与自回归合成路径解码相结合，在生成可合成且多样化的活性配体类似物方面优于现有基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有小分子生成模型通常优化配体药物设计目标（生成高活性分子）或合成可行性（分子可制备性）中的某一项，而牺牲另一项，导致发现既高分又易合成的分子存在瓶颈。因此需要一种统一框架，同时兼顾这两个目标。

Method: SynLaD 构建了一个编码器-双解码器架构：编码器将分子映射到潜空间；两个解码头分别负责（1）几何头：重构原子类型与3D坐标；（2）自回归合成头：基于反应式序列输出合成路径。在潜空间上，扩散Transformer根据药效团条件生成新的潜变量，进而解码为分子结构和合成路线。

Result: 在生物活性配体的类似物生成任务中，SynLaD 在可合成性和多样性命中物生成方面优于现有基线模型，表明单一模型既能产生形状对齐的分子，又能给出可行的合成方案。

Conclusion: SynLaD 证明了通过潜扩散框架将配体设计目标与合成可及性统一于一个模型中是可行的，能够同时生成与药效团匹配且具有可行合成路线的分子。

Original Abstract: We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 SynLaD，一个用于小分子生成的潜扩散框架，它将基于配体的药物设计目标（设计什么）与合成可行性（如何合成）统一起来。当前的模型通常优化其中一个目标而牺牲另一个目标，导致发现高评分且易合成分子时存在瓶颈。SynLaD 通过学习一个潜空间——该空间可同时解码为3D结构和合成路径——将反应约束生成与药效团条件化的3D设计相结合。编码器将分子映射到潜表示，该表示由两个解码头使用：（i）几何头，重构原子类型和坐标；（ii）自回归合成头，以序列化、基于反应式的符号输出合成路线。一个扩散Transformer在所学潜空间中生成新的潜变量，并以药效团特征为条件。在生物活性配体的类似物生成任务中，SynLaD 在可合成且多样化的命中物生成方面优于现有基线模型，证明了一个单一模型既能产生形状对齐的分子，又能给出可行的合成方案。

</details>


### [216] [Muon as a Residual Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01124)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Muon优化器可被理解为一种隐式残差连接：它通过正交化更新牺牲局部梯度保真度，但提升了对下游层表示的可利用性，从而在局部下降与下游可用性之间取得平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Muon优化器在训练大规模神经网络中表现优异，但其成功机理尚不明确，已有多种不同视角的解释，缺乏统一且直观的理解。

Method: 提出一种机械论解释：将Muon视为训练过程中的隐式残差连接。通过在受控线性优化设定中研究正交化更新带来的局部梯度保真度与表示保留之间的权衡。

Result: Muon学习到的表示对局部目标拟合较慢，但更易于被下游层利用，揭示了局部下降与下游可用性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为Muon提供了概念性解释，并为设计能够平衡局部下降与下游可用性的优化器提供了新的设计视角。

Original Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.

中文摘要翻译: Muon最近已成为训练大型神经网络最有效的优化器之一，但其经验上的成功已从多个不同角度得到解释。在本文中，我们提出了一种简单的机械论解释：Muon可以被理解为训练过程中的一种隐式残差连接。具体而言，正交化更新会牺牲一些即时的梯度保真度，同时改善对下游层表示保留的能力。我们在受控的线性优化设定中研究了这一权衡，其中Muon可以学习到对局部目标拟合较慢但更易于被下游层利用的表示。我们的研究结果为Muon提供了概念性解释，并为设计能够平衡局部下降与下游可用性的优化器提供了设计视角。

</details>


### [217] [ZO-Act: Efficient Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning via One-Shot Activation-Informed Low-Rank Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01125)
*Xun Dong,Yibo Xu,Naigang Wang,Xin Li,Penghang Yin,Zi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZO-Act，一种基于激活信息的零阶优化微调方法，通过将扰动限制在输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间，降低方差并提升大语言模型微调性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法扰动全模型权重或随机构造的低维子空间，导致估计方差大、性能有限，亟需一种更有效的低维扰动空间构造方法。

Method: 针对每个线性层，在初始化时基于输入激活计算一个小的激活基（固定低秩子空间），仅优化轻量级系数矩阵，通过前向损失评估完成优化；支持动量优化器（如Adam）和量化模型微调。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4量化的Llama-3-8B上，在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务中一致优于强零阶微调基线。

Conclusion: 扰动低维系数可同时降低方差相关收敛项和有限差分误差，而LLM激活和梯度的低秩结构有效控制了子空间近似偏差，使ZO-Act成为一种高效且实用的零阶微调方法。

Original Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables fine-tuning large language models when backpropagation is unavailable or memory-prohibitive, but existing methods often perturb full model weights or randomly constructed low-dimensional subspaces, yielding high-variance estimates and limited performance. We propose ZO-Act, an activation-informed ZO fine-tuning method that restricts perturbations to a fixed low-rank subspace derived from input activations. For each linear layer, ZO-Act computes a small activation basis once at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This reduces the effective perturbation dimension, exposes explicit trainable variables compatible with momentum-based optimizers such as Adam, and naturally supports quantized LLM fine-tuning by keeping low-bit weights frozen. We analyze ZO-Act as zeroth-order optimization over a restricted coefficient space and show that perturbing the low-dimensional coefficients reduces both the variance-dependent convergence term and the finite-difference error of the ZO estimator, at the cost of a controlled subspace approximation bias that is mitigated by the low-rank structure of LLM activations and gradients. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B show consistent gains over strong ZO fine-tuning baselines across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 零阶优化能够在反向传播不可用或内存受限时微调大语言模型，但现有方法往往扰动完整模型权重或随机构造的低维子空间，导致估计方差大且性能有限。我们提出ZO-Act，一种基于激活信息的零阶微调方法，将扰动限制在由输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间内。对于每个线性层，ZO-Act在初始化时计算一次小的激活基，仅通过前向损失评估来优化轻量级系数矩阵。这降低了有效扰动维度，暴露了与Adam等动量优化器兼容的显式可训练变量，并通过冻结低比特权重自然地支持量化大语言模型的微调。我们将ZO-Act分析为受限系数空间上的零阶优化，并证明扰动低维系数同时降低了零阶估计器的方差相关收敛项和有限差分误差，其代价是受控的子空间近似偏差——该偏差因大语言模型激活和梯度的低秩结构而得以缓解。在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4量化的Llama-3-8B上的实验表明，在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务中，ZO-Act一致优于强零阶微调基线。

</details>


### [218] [GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01128)
*Meenakshi Krishnan,Pranav Pulijala,Ke Chen,Haizhao Yang,Ramani Duraiswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA是一种几何自适应积分自编码器，通过将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，并利用交叉注意力机制使积分变换层适应局部几何特征，统一解决了任意几何上PDE的正问题（含边界值问题）和逆问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有几何自适应神经算子主要针对输入输出共享同一空间域的正问题设计，难以处理输入输出位于不同域的边界值问题（BVP）和逆问题，缺乏统一的单架构解决方案。

Method: GAIA将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，通过交叉注意力机制将这些令牌注入积分变换层，使核函数能够根据局部几何特征进行自适应调整，以单次前向传播统一处理正问题、BVP和逆问题，无需重训练、迭代优化或图构建。

Result: 在7个2D和3D基准上评估（含4个逆问题/BVP新基准），GAIA在所有逆问题和BVP任务上取得SOTA：翼型流重建中位相对L2误差降低64%，电阻抗成像误差降低27%；在MCB的所有形状类别上优于所有基线；在正问题上与专用求解器竞争力相当，且在点分辨率变化时保持稳定精度。

Conclusion: GAIA提供了一个统一的、无需重训练的几何自适应算子学习框架，能够有效处理任意几何上的正问题、边界值问题和逆问题，且在不同分辨率下具有稳健的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.

中文摘要翻译: 针对任意几何上偏微分方程（PDE）的算子学习为大规模仿真构建了快速的神经替代模型。尽管近期几何自适应神经算子取得了实质性进展，但它们主要针对输入和输出共享同一空间域的正问题设计。这限制了它们在边界值问题（BVP）和逆问题中的适用性，因为这些问题的输入和输出可能位于不同域上。我们提出了几何自适应积分自编码器（GAIA），这是一种算子学习模型，它将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，并通过交叉注意力机制将这些令牌注入积分变换层，使得核函数能够根据局部几何特征进行自适应调整。这产生了一个统一的架构，能够以单次前向传播处理任意域上的正问题（包括BVP）和逆问题，无需重训练、迭代优化或图构建。我们在7个2D和3D基准上评估了GAIA，其中4个是逆问题和BVP的新基准或大幅扩展的基准：电阻抗成像、光学断层成像、变化几何上的3D达西流，以及机械零件基准（MCB）上泊松BVP的修改设置。GAIA在每个逆问题和BVP任务上都取得了新的最先进结果：与次优的摊销方法相比，在翼型流重建中位相对L2误差降低64%，在电阻抗成像中降低27%，并且在MCB的每个形状类别上均优于所有基线。在其他正问题上，GAIA与专用求解器具有竞争力，同时在基于Transformer的基线性能下降的点分辨率变化上保持稳定精度。

</details>


### [219] [Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps for Online Feedback-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01144)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Jens Sjölund,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IMPFM框架，通过交互式多粒子流图实现样本高效的在线反馈驱动搜索，解决未知偏好下的全局探索与奖励对齐问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式奖励对齐方法擅长局部探索，但在偏好未知、仅能通过序列反馈逐步揭示的场景下缺乏全局探索能力，难以发现高收益区域。

Method: IMPFM框架：基于流图的交互式多粒子系统，通过后验样本共享机制纠正个体粒子漂移，结合探索-利用重加权机制保持结构多样性，并引入多粒子交互感知的Feynman-Kac校正器逐步引导系统朝向KL倾斜的目标分布。

Result: 大量实验和消融研究证明IMPFM在多样化的搜索和对齐任务中优于现有基线方法，能有效实现全局探索并防止模式坍缩。

Conclusion: IMPFM通过顺序校正的多粒子动力学和交互机制，有效克服了标准SMC采样的权重退化问题，实现了样本高效、偏好未知条件下的全局探索与奖励对齐。

Original Abstract: While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local exploration within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we propose Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework for sample-efficient online feedback-driven search. IMPFM progressively transports a group of interactive particles toward the target distribution, maintaining the broad coverage essential for heterogeneous preference alignment. IMPFM introduces a principled and efficient posterior sample sharing mechanism across particles powered by flow maps. By correcting individual particle drift with the collective posterior samples of the entire ensemble at each resampling step, the framework maximizes sample utility to enable global exploration while actively mitigating reward over-optimization, typical of standard control frameworks. Paired with a principled exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism involving multi-particle interaction, this sequentially corrected multi-particle dynamics explicitly preserves structural diversity and overcomes the weight degeneracy inherent to standard SMC samplers. Crucially, we prove that the resulting sampling framework yields a multi-particle interaction-aware Feynman-Kac corrector that progressively steers the multi-particle system toward a KL-tilted target distribution, facilitating global exploration and preventing mode collapse. Extensive empirical evaluations and rigorous ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks confirm the efficacy of IMPFM over existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成模型已经实现了无需训练的奖励对齐，但当前方法通常擅长在底层分布的狭窄区域内进行局部探索。当偏好是事先未知的且仅通过序列反馈逐步揭示时——这种场景需要广泛探索以发现高收益区域——这些方法就难以胜任。为解决这一问题，我们提出了序列控制交互式多粒子流图（IMPFM），一个面向样本高效的在线反馈驱动搜索框架。IMPFM渐进地将一组交互粒子向目标分布输送，同时保持异构偏好对齐所需的广泛覆盖性。IMPFM引入了一种由流图驱动的、原则性且高效的后验样本共享机制，在每次重采样步骤中，利用整个粒子集合的集体后验样本来纠正单个粒子的漂移，从而最大化样本效用以实现全局探索，同时积极缓解标准控制框架中常见的奖励过度优化问题。结合一种涉及多粒子交互的原则性探索-利用重加权机制，这种顺序校正的多粒子动力学显式地保持了结构多样性，并克服了标准SMC采样器固有的权重退化问题。关键的是，我们证明了所得到的采样框架产生了一个多粒子交互感知的Feynman-Kac校正器，该校正器逐步将多粒子系统引导向KL倾斜的目标分布，从而促进全局探索并防止模式坍缩。广泛的实证评估和跨多种搜索与对齐任务的严格消融实验证实了IMPFM相对于现有基线方法的有效性。

</details>


### [220] [Efficient Compression of Structured and Unstructured Volumes via Learned 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01164)
*Landon Dyken,Sharmistha Chakrabarti,Nathan Debardeleben,Steve Petruzza,Qi Wu,Will Usher,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯基元的显式体数据压缩模型，通过将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示，结合CUDA加速采样和基于采样误差的致密化策略，在结构化与非结构化体数据上实现了与隐式神经表示（INR）相当的重建质量，并在非结构化数据上全面超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有用于非结构化体数据的隐式神经表示（INR）不编码几何信息，需要额外存储部分网格用于后续采样，限制了压缩率；而新视角合成方法已证明3D高斯的显式集合可准确可视化体数据。本文旨在利用3D高斯基元构建显式体数据压缩模型，以消除网格存储需求并提升压缩性能。

Method: 1) 将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示；2) 通过加权聚合相交高斯的方式重建空间位置处的标量值；3) 开发了针对结构化与非结构化模型采样的CUDA加速流水线；4) 设计鼓励模型准确编码域信息的损失函数；5) 提出基于采样误差的致密化策略。

Result: 在结构化体数据上，所提显式模型达到与INR相当的重建质量，同时训练速度显著提升；在非结构化体数据上，所有指标均明显优于现有INR方法。

Conclusion: 基于3D高斯基元的显式模型天然编码了域几何信息，无需为非结构化体数据存储网格，从而带来更高的压缩机会，在体数据压缩任务上是一种有效且高效的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Recent work has shown that implicit neural representations (INRs) can be trained to effectively compress structured and unstructured volume data, allowing for direct data querying with a reduced memory footprint. However, as existing INRs for unstructured volumes do not encode geometry, they require partial mesh storage for later sampling, limiting achievable compression. At the same time, novel view synthesis methods have shown that explicit collections of 3D Gaussians can be used to accurately visualize volume data. In this work, we introduce an explicit model for volume data compression based on 3D Gaussian primitives. We reinterpret collections of 3D Gaussians as an explicit representation of a scalar field and use a sampling strategy that reconstructs scalar values at spatial locations through weighted aggregation of intersecting Gaussians. We develop optimized CUDA-accelerated pipelines for structured and unstructured model sampling, loss functions that encourage accurate domain encoding by our models, and a novel sampling-error based densification strategy. Our explicit formulation naturally encodes domain geometry, eliminating the need for mesh storage in unstructured volumes and introducing significantly higher compression opportunities. Compared to existing INRs, we demonstrate that our explicit model achieves competitive reconstruction quality with significant training speedups on structured volumes, while markedly outperforming in all metrics on unstructured volumes.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究表明，隐式神经表示（INR）可被训练用于高效压缩结构化和非结构化体数据，在降低内存占用的同时支持直接数据查询。然而，现有的面向非结构化体数据的INR不编码几何信息，因此需要部分网格存储以供后续采样，这限制了可达的压缩率。与此同时，新视角合成方法已经证明，3D高斯的显式集合可用于准确可视化体数据。本文提出一种基于3D高斯基元的显式体数据压缩模型。我们将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示，并采用一种采样策略，通过加权聚合相交高斯来重建空间位置处的标量值。我们开发了针对结构化和非结构化模型采样的优化CUDA加速流水线，设计了鼓励模型准确编码域信息的损失函数，并提出了一种基于采样误差的致密化策略。我们的显式公式天然编码了域几何信息，消除了非结构化体数据中网格存储的需求，并带来了显著更高的压缩机会。与现有的INR相比，我们证明所提出的显式模型在结构化体数据上达到了具有竞争力的重建质量且训练速度显著提升，同时在非结构化体数据的所有指标上均明显优于现有方法。

</details>


### [221] [Decision-Aware Training for Sample-Based Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01171)
*Kornelius Raeth,Nicole Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策感知训练方法，通过将可微决策损失融入能量评分目标，使基于样本的生成模型在训练时直接考虑下游决策成本，在保持完整概率预测的同时提升成本敏感区域的预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于样本的生成模型训练目标（如严格适当评分规则）对决策者的成本结构不敏感，训练信号按数据密度分配，无法意识到预测误差在下游决策中最昂贵的区域，导致高成本区域的预测质量不足。

Method: 在能量评分目标中引入可微的决策损失项，该损失直接惩罚基于模型预测采取行动所产生的成本，形成联合损失函数。该决策损失本身也是一种适当评分规则，具有理论保障。

Result: 在一个人工合成任务和两个真实世界任务上验证了该方法，在成本敏感区域实现了有针对性的改进，同时保留了完整的概率预测能力。

Conclusion: 决策感知训练能够有效提升生成模型在高成本区域的预测质量，且不牺牲整体概率预测的完整性，为高风险决策场景下的概率预测提供了一种有理论依据的训练范式。

Original Abstract: Sample-based generative models are increasingly used for probabilistic forecasting in high-stakes decision settings, yet their training objectives are blind to the decision maker's cost structure. These models are commonly trained with strictly proper scoring rules, such as the energy score, which allocate their training signal in proportion to data density, with no awareness of where forecast errors are most costly for downstream decisions. We therefore propose decision-aware training for sample-based generative models, augmenting the energy score objective with a differentiable decision loss that directly penalises the cost incurred by acting on the model's forecast. This combined loss is theoretically grounded, as the decision loss is itself a proper scoring rule. We validate our method on one synthetic and two real-world tasks, showing targeted improvements in cost-sensitive regions while retaining full probabilistic forecasts.

中文摘要翻译: 基于样本的生成模型越来越多地用于高风险决策场景中的概率预测，但它们的训练目标对决策者的成本结构并不敏感。这些模型通常使用严格适当的评分规则（如能量评分）进行训练，其训练信号按数据密度比例分配，无法意识到预测误差在下游决策中最昂贵的区域。为此，我们提出面向决策感知的基于样本的生成模型训练方法，在能量评分目标中引入一个可微的决策损失，该损失直接惩罚基于模型预测采取行动所导致的成本。这一联合损失具有理论依据，因为决策损失本身也是一种适当的评分规则。我们在一个人工合成任务和两个真实世界任务上验证了该方法，在成本敏感区域实现了有针对性的改进，同时保留了完整的概率预测能力。

</details>


### [222] [QuasiMoTTo: Quasi-Monte Carlo Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01179)
*Michael Y. Li,Anthony Zhan,Kanishk Gandhi,Noah D. Goodman,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuasiMoTTo，一种通过准蒙特卡洛方法生成相关但边缘分布正确的样本，替代独立同分布采样，在推理和强化学习中显著提升样本效率的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩展推理计算时，并行独立采样会产生大量冗余解，浪费计算资源。这种浪费看似不可避免，但本文探索了在保持并行性的前提下生成相关但精确样本的设计空间，以提高样本效率。

Method: 将自回归采样重新参数化为逆CDF采样，用准蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法生成更均匀分布的底层均匀随机数，从而产生覆盖输出空间更广、冗余更少的相关样本。每个样本的边缘分布保持与原始语言模型一致，可直接用于策略梯度训练。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以25-47%更少的样本达到与i.i.d.相同的pass@k准确率，且常饱和任意边缘保持采样器的pass@k理论上界。在策略梯度强化学习（GRPO）中，以50%更少训练步数匹配i.i.d.性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入相关但边缘分布正确的采样方法，可以打破独立采样在扩展推理计算中的效率瓶颈，显著提升计算到性能的转化效率，在推理和强化学习中均有实际收益。

Original Abstract: Scaling inference compute, by generating many parallel attempts per problem, is a costly but reliable lever for improving language model capabilities. By default these attempts are generated independently, wasting inference compute on redundant solutions. This waste seems unavoidable. After all, independence is what makes parallel sampling trivial to scale. However, this tradeoff is not fundamental: there is a rich design space of samplers that generate correlated but exact samples entirely in parallel. We explore this design space as an avenue for improving sample efficiency in scaling inference compute and reinforcement learning (RL). Concretely, we introduce QuasiMoTTo, which uses correlated samples as a drop-in replacement for i.i.d. samples. To generate these samples, QuasiMoTTo uses a reparameterization of autoregressive sampling as inverse-CDF sampling and draws the underlying uniforms with quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC); because QMC spreads the uniforms out more evenly than i.i.d., the resulting samples cover the output space with far less redundancy. Even though the batch is correlated, each sample is marginally distributed according to the language model, so we can use the batch for policy-gradient training. Our empirical analysis focuses on understanding how efficiently QuasiMoTTo can turn compute into performance. To evaluate correlated samplers, whose dependence breaks standard pass@k estimators, we first develop an unbiased bootstrap estimator. Across four reasoning benchmarks, QuasiMoTTo matches i.i.d. pass@k accuracy with 25-47% fewer samples. Strikingly, QuasiMoTTo often saturates an upper bound on pass@k that holds for any marginal-preserving sampler. We also apply QuasiMoTTo to policy-gradient RL (GRPO) where it matches i.i.d. performance with 50% fewer training steps. These gains come from higher coverage, which yields a stronger learning signal per batch.

中文摘要翻译: 通过为每个问题生成大量并行尝试来扩展推理计算，是提升语言模型能力的一种昂贵但可靠的手段。默认情况下，这些尝试是独立生成的，浪费了推理计算在冗余解上。这种浪费似乎不可避免，毕竟独立性正是让并行采样易于扩展的原因。然而，这一权衡并非本质性的：存在一个丰富的采样器设计空间，可以完全并行地生成相关但精确的样本。我们探索了这一设计空间，将其作为提升推理计算和强化学习（RL）扩展中样本效率的途径。具体而言，我们提出了QuasiMoTTo，它将相关样本作为独立同分布（i.i.d.）样本的即插即用替代品。为生成这些样本，QuasiMoTTo将自回归采样重新参数化为逆累积分布函数（逆CDF）采样，并用准蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法抽取底层均匀分布；由于QMC比i.i.d.更均匀地散布均匀分布变量，生成的样本以更少的冗余覆盖输出空间。尽管批次内样本是相关的，但每个样本的边缘分布仍然服从语言模型，因此我们可以将这些批次用于策略梯度训练。我们的实证分析侧重于理解QuasiMoTTo如何高效地将计算转化为性能。为了评估相关采样器（其依赖关系破坏了标准的pass@k估计器），我们首先开发了一个无偏的bootstrap估计器。在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以25-47%更少的样本达到了与i.i.d.相同的pass@k准确率。引人注目的是，QuasiMoTTo常常饱和任意边缘保持采样器的pass@k理论上界。我们还将QuasiMoTTo应用于策略梯度强化学习（GRPO），在以50%更少的训练步骤下匹配了i.i.d.的性能。这些收益来自于更高的覆盖率，这为每批次提供了更强的学习信号。

</details>


### [223] [Right in the Right Way: LM Training with Verifiable Rewards and Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01181)
*Mehul Damani,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗性生成器-判别器框架，将可验证奖励与来自人类示教的学习信号相结合，在保持RLVR准确性的同时，显著改善了风格、多样性等不可验证属性，并有效抑制了奖励作弊行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅优化可客观评分的内容，忽视了风格、结构等不可验证的人类输出特征，导致多样性崩塌、响应不自然和奖励作弊等问题。

Method: 提出对抗性生成器-判别器框架：生成器通过RL训练同时最大化任务准确性和来自判别器的对抗性奖励；判别器与生成器策略联合训练，学习区分人类输出与模型生成输出，作为人类输出分布的代理提供关于难以形式化为标量奖励的生成方面的反馈。

Result: 在bug修复任务中，编辑距离显著低于RLVR基线且性能匹配；在故事生成任务中，胜率显著提升且故事更多样化、更类人；在奖励作弊基准中，几乎消除模型不当行为同时保持高分。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了RL和SFT之间的差距，为联合优化任务的可验证和不可验证属性提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Original Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training LMs on tasks with well-defined success metrics, such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, current RLVR methods optimize only what can be objectively scored, often neglecting subjective, non-verifiable aspects of human-like outputs, such as style and structure. This limitation leads to well-documented failure modes such as diversity collapse, unnatural-sounding responses, and reward hacking. We propose an adversarial generator-discriminator framework that augments verifiable rewards with a learned signal from human demonstrations. A generator model is trained using RL to maximize both task accuracy and an adversarial reward derived from a discriminator. The discriminator, trained alongside the generator policy, learns to distinguish human-written outputs from model-generated ones. The discriminator serves as a learned proxy for the human output distribution, providing feedback on aspects of generation that are difficult to formalize as scalar rewards. Across diverse domains, including bug fixing and open-ended generation, our approach consistently improves non-verifiable properties while preserving the accuracy gains of RLVR. In bug fixing, our method produces solutions with significantly lower edit distance compared to RLVR baselines while matching end performance. In story generation, our method significantly improves win rate while producing stories that are diverse and more human-like. And in a simple reward hacking benchmark, our method nearly eliminates model misbehavior while maintaining high benchmark scores. Together, these results show that our approach bridges RL and SFT, offering a scalable path toward jointly optimizing the verifiable and non-verifiable properties of a task.

中文摘要翻译: 带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为在具有明确成功指标的任务（如代码生成和数学推理）上训练语言模型的有效范式。然而，当前的RLVR方法仅优化可客观评分的内容，往往忽视人类输出中不可验证的主观方面，如风格和结构。这一限制导致了诸如多样性崩塌、响应不自然和奖励作弊等有据可查的失败模式。我们提出了一种对抗性生成器-判别器框架，该框架用来自人类示教的学习信号增强可验证奖励。生成器模型通过RL进行训练，以最大化任务准确性和来自判别器的对抗性奖励。与生成器策略联合训练的判别器学习区分人类编写的输出和模型生成的输出。判别器作为人类输出分布的学习代理，为难以形式化为标量奖励的生成方面提供反馈。在包括bug修复和开放式生成的多个领域中，我们的方法在保持RLVR准确性提升的同时，持续改善了不可验证的属性。在bug修复中，与RLVR基线相比，我们的方法在匹配终端性能的同时生成了编辑距离显著更低的解决方案。在故事生成中，我们的方法显著提高了胜率，同时生成了多样化且更类人的故事。在一个简单的奖励作弊基准测试中，我们的方法在保持高基准分数的同时几乎消除了模型的不当行为。这些结果共同表明，我们的方法弥合了RL和SFT之间的差距，为联合优化任务的可验证和不可验证属性提供了一条可扩展的路径。

</details>


### [224] [Neural Certificate Pricing for Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01185)
*Jingyi Chen,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经证书定价（NCP）框架，利用组合优化中“验证容易、搜索困难”的不对称性，通过无监督学习预测对偶价格并重构原始边际解，在多个CO任务上达到或超越SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 组合优化问题的难解性源于其可认证的离散结构导致指数级搜索空间。虽然验证候选解的可行性是多项式时间的，但枚举所有候选以达到最优性认证仍然困难。现有方法依赖监督学习或枚举违规不等式，效率较低。

Method: 提出神经证书定价（NCP）框架，包含两个关键组件：1）神经网络预测证书级别的对偶价格（dual prices）；2）结构化恢复层（structured recovery layer）基于对偶价格重构原始边际解（primal marginal）。NCP可视为摊销分离（amortized separation）：不逐一枚举违规不等式，而是通过学习残差价格来聚合它们的整体效应。

Result: 在三类组合优化问题上，NCP要么大幅超越最优神经基线方法，要么在仅使用其计算时间的一小部分情况下达到同等性能，并且表现出更强的分布外泛化能力。理论上证明了当证书一致性条件成立时，恢复的边际解全局可行，且预测价格的一阶误差仅带来目标值的二阶损失。

Conclusion: NCP有效利用了CO问题中验证与搜索的不对称性，通过无监督学习对偶价格实现了高效且可泛化的组合优化求解，为神经组合优化提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Combinatorial optimization (CO) problems are difficult because certifiable discrete structure induces exponential search. One needs to search over the set exponentially many candidates to certify optimality, however, the structural feasibility of a path, packing, or cover can be verified in polynomial time once supplied. In this study, we introduce Neural Certificate Pricing (NCP) that exploits this asymmetry under an unsupervised learning framework. A neural network is trained to predict certificate-level dual prices, while a structured recovery layer constructs the induced primal marginal. NCP can be viewed as amortized separation: instead of enumerating violated inequalities, it learns the residual prices through which their aggregate effect enters recovery. When the certificate-consistency condition holds, the recovered marginal is globally feasible, and a local theory shows that first-order errors in the predicted price induce only second-order loss in objective value. Across three classes of CO problems, NCP either outperforms state-of-the-art neural baselines by large margins or matches them at a fraction of the computation time, and shows stronger out-of-distribution generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 组合优化（CO）问题之所以困难，是因为可认证的离散结构导致指数级搜索空间。为了证明最优性，需要在指数级数量的候选解中进行搜索；然而，一旦给定解，路径、包装或覆盖等问题的结构可行性可以在多项式时间内得到验证。在本研究中，我们提出了神经证书定价（Neural Certificate Pricing, NCP）方法，在无监督学习框架下利用这种不对称性。神经网络被训练来预测证书级别的对偶价格，同时一个结构化恢复层构建诱导出的原始边际解。NCP可被视为摊销分离（amortized separation）：它不逐一枚举违规不等式，而是学习残差价格，通过这些残差价格将不等式的聚合效应引入恢复过程。当证书一致性条件成立时，恢复的边际解是全局可行的，局部理论表明，预测价格的一阶误差仅会在目标值上引起二阶损失。在三类组合优化问题上，NCP要么大幅超越最优的神经基线方法，要么在仅使用其一小部分计算时间的情况下与之持平，并展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。

</details>


### [225] [Quantum vs. Classical Machine Learning: A Unified Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01197)
*Chuanming Yu,Jiaming Liu,Zihao Ge,Xiongfei Wu,Lulu Zhu,Pengzhan Zhao,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过七组监督学习和强化学习模型的对比实验，实证评估了量子机器学习（QML）模型与经典基线模型的性能。结果表明，当前QML模型在预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典模型，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面展现出潜力。文章总结了QML在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面的挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目前支持量子机器学习模型相对于经典模型性能优势的证据尚不充分，为填补这一实证研究空白，本文系统对比了QML与经典模型的性能。

Method: 选取七组模型对，涵盖监督学习和强化学习，对QML模型及其经典对应模型进行系统的实证对比研究。

Result: QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典基线模型；但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面表现良好。QML面临硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性三方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 当前QML模型整体性能尚不及经典模型，但噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面有发展潜力；研究为QML的鲁棒性和参数优化提供了基础。

Original Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising computational paradigm for machine learning (ML), with the potential to offer computational advantages over classical approaches. At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

中文摘要翻译: 量子计算已成为机器学习领域中一种前景广阔的计算范式，有潜力相较于经典方法带来计算优势。现阶段，支持量子机器学习模型相较于经典模型在性能和优势方面的证据尚不充分。为填补这一空白，本文对量子机器学习模型及其经典对应模型的性能进行了实证研究。我们比较了涵盖监督学习和强化学习的七组模型对。结果表明，所评估的量子机器学习模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性或训练时间方面尚未超越经典基线模型。尽管如此，量子机器学习在过滤噪声和控制假阳性方面仍是一种有前景的方法。我们的研究结果总结了量子机器学习在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面面临的挑战，为量子机器学习的鲁棒性和参数优化研究奠定了基础。本工作开源地址为 https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML。

</details>


### [226] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2 是一种基于 xLSTM 的递归时间序列基础模型，支持多变量预测与已知未来协变量，以恒定计算成本实现流式处理，并在零样本任务上达到 SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 Transformer 时间序列基础模型在捕获跨变量依赖时存在二次复杂度和全历史重计算开销，无法高效处理真实场景中连续到达的流式观测数据，且难以同时整合未来已知协变量并保持目标变量的严格因果性。

Method: 基于 xLSTM 的递归架构，结合双向时间混合器与不对称分组注意力变量混合器，实现流式场景下每块恒定计算成本；提出合成耦合流水线，从大规模单变量语料库动态组合多变量样本以支持可扩展预训练。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 上达到零样本 SOTA；可稳定扩展到任意上下文长度；每块的推理成本保持恒定；单变量模式 38.4M 激活参数，多变量模式额外 44.1M 参数。

Conclusion: TiRex-2 是首个同时实现递归设计、未来已知协变量集成与严格因果多变量预测的时间序列基础模型，为高效流式时间序列预测提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出 TiRex-2——一个基于递归 xLSTM 的时间序列基础模型，它将单变量模型 TiRex 推广至同时包含过去与未来协变量的多变量预测。真实世界的预测本质上是序贯的：观测连续到达，变量联合演化，且部分协变量是预先已知的。现有基于 Transformer 的时间序列基础模型能够捕获跨变量依赖，但上下文长度上具有二次复杂度，且每当新观测到达时需要重新计算完整历史。TiRex-2 通过以记忆为中心的递归设计克服了这些限制，在流式场景下实现了每块恒定的计算开销。该模型将双向时间混合器与非对称分组注意力变量混合器相结合，能够在保持目标变量严格因果性的同时集成未来已知协变量。据我们所知，这是首个同时实现上述特性组合的时间序列基础模型。为支持可扩展的多变量预训练，我们提出了一种合成耦合流水线，能够从大规模单变量语料库中动态组合多样的多变量样本。实验结果表明，TiRex-2 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 上达到了最先进的零样本性能，在流式处理至任意上下文长度时保持稳定，且每块的推理成本恒定。该模型在单变量模式下使用 38.4M 激活参数，在多变量预测中额外激活 44.1M 参数。

</details>


### [227] [Language-Critique Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01225)
*Chih-Han Yang,Dai-Jie Wu,Yun-Ping Huang,Ping-Chun Hsieh,Kenneth Marino,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于语言批评的模仿学习框架，用自然语言结构监督信号替代传统标量信号，从次优演示中学习鲁棒策略，在连续控制任务中优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统从次优演示中模仿学习依赖置信度、判别器分数、重要性权重等压缩标量信号，这些信号无法明确表达关于任务进度、失败模式或纠正动作的中间推理信息，表达能力有限。

Method: 提出语言批评框架：首先从演示中构建语言标签，明确描述当前进度、识别次优行为并提供细粒度纠正指导；然后引入语言批评损失函数，直接利用这些结构化信号训练策略而不将其降维为标量；并将其实例化为行为克隆（LC-BC）和扩散策略（LC-DP）两种形式；同时给出理论证明表明目标函数上界专家性能差距。

Result: 在导航、操作和游戏等多样化连续控制任务中，所提方法LC-BC和LC-DP在各项指标上持续优于强模仿学习和离线强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 语言可以作为从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略的强大结构化监督信号，语言批评框架有效避免了传统标量信号的信息坍缩问题。

Original Abstract: Prior work on imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations typically relies on compressed supervision signals such as confidence estimates, discriminator scores, or importance weights. These scalar signals are inherently limited, as they cannot explicitly express intermediate reasoning about task progress, failure modes, or corrective actions. We propose a language-critique framework for imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations that instead leverages natural language as a structured supervision signal, avoiding the collapse of expressive feedback into scalars. Our method first constructs language labels from demonstrations that explicitly describe current progress, identify suboptimal behaviors, and provide fine-grained corrective guidance. We then introduce a language-critique loss that directly trains policies using these structured signals without reducing them to scalars, and instantiate it for both behavior cloning and diffusion policies, yielding LC-BC and LC-DP. We further provide a theoretical result showing that the proposed objective upper-bounds the expert performance gap under standard assumptions. Empirically, we evaluate on diverse continuous control tasks spanning navigation, manipulation, and gameplay, where our methods consistently outperform strong imitation learning and offline reinforcement learning baselines. These results demonstrate that language can serve as a powerful and structured form of supervision for learning robust policies from suboptimal data.

中文摘要翻译: 从次优演示中进行模仿学习的先前工作通常依赖于压缩的监督信号，如置信度估计、判别器分数或重要性权重。这些标量信号本质上存在局限性，因为它们无法明确表达关于任务进度、失败模式或纠正动作的中间推理。我们提出了一种基于语言批评的次优演示模仿学习框架，该框架利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号，避免了将表达性反馈坍缩为标量。我们的方法首先从演示中构建语言标签，明确描述当前进度、识别次优行为并提供细粒度的纠正指导。然后，我们引入一种语言批评损失函数，直接使用这些结构化信号训练策略，而无需将其降维为标量，并将其实例化为行为克隆和扩散策略，分别得到LC-BC和LC-DP。我们进一步提供了理论结果，表明在标准假设下，所提目标函数上界了专家性能差距。在实验方面，我们在涵盖导航、操作和游戏的多样化连续控制任务上进行了评估，结果表明我们的方法持续优于强模仿学习和离线强化学习基线。这些结果表明，语言可以作为从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略的一种强大且结构化的监督形式。

</details>


### [228] [Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Hongzhou Lin,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化学习（RL）后训练中Transformer各层的贡献分布，发现RL收益高度集中在少数中间层，仅训练单层即可恢复甚至超越全参数训练的收益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法通常均匀更新所有模型参数，隐含假设各层对RL收益贡献相似。本文旨在挑战这一假设，系统探究RL适应过程在Transformer各层中的分布规律。

Method: 提出"层贡献度"（layer contribution）指标，量化单层训练所恢复的全参数RL收益比例。在7个模型（Qwen3、Qwen2.5系列）、3种RL算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）和多种任务（数学推理、代码生成、智能体决策）上进行了系统性的逐层训练分析。

Result: 1) 训练单个Transformer层即可恢复甚至超越全参数RL训练的大部分收益；2) RL收益高度集中在少数层，甚至单层；3) 高贡献层稳定集中在Transformer中部，靠近输入和输出端的层贡献显著更低；4) 层排序在不同数据集、任务、模型系列和RL算法间保持强相关性。

Conclusion: RL后训练的收益在Transformer层间分布极不均匀，高度集中于中间层，表明现有均匀更新所有参数的RL训练方式存在冗余，为更高效的层选择性RL训练提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已成为大型语言模型（LLM）后训练的核心组成部分，但关于RL适应过程如何在Transformer各层之间分布，目前知之甚少。现有方法通常统一更新所有模型参数，隐式假设每一层对RL后训练所获得的收益贡献相似。在本工作中，我们通过系统的逐层RL训练研究挑战了这一假设。令人惊讶的是，我们发现训练单个Transformer层即可恢复全参数RL训练所获得的大部分收益，在某些情况下甚至能超越它。为量化这一现象，我们引入了"层贡献度"这一指标，用于衡量单独训练某一层所恢复的全参数RL改进的比例。在涵盖两个模型系列（Qwen3、Qwen2.5）、三种RL算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）以及数学推理、代码生成和智能体决策等多个任务领域的七个模型上，我们观察到一种 remarkably 稳定的模式：RL收益高度集中在一小部分Transformer层中，在许多情况下甚至集中在单层。更引人注目的是，相同的结构模式持续出现：高贡献层集中在Transformer堆叠的中部，而靠近输入和输出端的层贡献显著较低。由此产生的层排序在不同数据集、任务、模型系列和RL算法之间保持强相关性。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Pinching Antennas-Assisted Sensing: A Ziv-Zakai Bound (ZZB) Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00234)
*Hao Jiang,Chongjun Ouyang,Yuanwei Liu,Arumugam Nallanathan,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从Ziv-Zakai界（ZZB）角度分析了夹捏天线系统（PASS）的感知性能，解决了其多模态观测模型带来的感知模糊问题，并提出了低复杂度的替代目标函数以优化感知性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PASS固有的多模态观测模型会导致感知模糊性，而传统贝叶斯克拉美-罗界（BCRB）难以在宽信噪比范围内提供紧致的下界，因此需要从ZZB视角进行分析以更好地刻画MSE下界。

Method: 建立了针对上行感知场景的观测模型（单目标、单波导、多夹捏天线），推导了任意先验分布下的ZZB通用表达式（并特化为高斯和均匀分布），分析了低/高SNR渐近ZZB，引入模糊函数研究ZZB与BCRB的关系，提出了无SNR和感知SNR的两种替代目标函数以降低ZZB优化的计算复杂度。

Result: ①相比BCRB，ZZB在宽SNR范围内提供更紧致的感知性能下界；②ZZB的模糊感知能力能有效处理多模态引起的感知歧义，提供可靠的MSE下界；③所提替代目标函数能以较低计算复杂度实现有效的ZZB最小化。

Conclusion: ZZB是分析PASS感知性能的有效工具，能处理多模态模糊性并提供紧致的性能下界，所提替代函数为实际系统优化提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: The sensing capability of the pinching-antenna system (PASS) is analyzed from a Ziv-Zakai bound (ZZB) perspective, motivated by the sensing ambiguity arising from the multimodal observation model inherent to PASS. In comparison to other Bayesian sensing bounds, the ZZB provides a lower bound on the mean-squared error (MSE) across a broad range of signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) and accounts for ambiguity in the likelihood functions. First, an observation model is developed for an uplink sensing scenario where a single sensing target transmits uplink pilots to a single-waveguide PASS receiver equipped with multiple pinching antennas (PAs). Building on this model, general ZZB expressions are derived for arbitrary prior distributions of the target's position, and are then specialized to the Gaussian and uniform cases. Second, the asymptotic ZZBs in low- and high-SNR regimes are characterized, and the relationship between the ZZBs and the conventional Bayesian Cramér-Rao bound (BCRB) is further studied by introducing the concept of an ambiguity function. Furthermore, to reduce the high computational complexity of direct evaluation of the ZZB, SNR-free and SNR-aware surrogate objective functions are proposed to facilitate ZZB-based optimization for enhancing sensing performance. Numerical results demonstrate that: i) Compared with the BCRB, the ZZB provides a tight sensing performance lower bound over a wide range of SNRs, ii) the ambiguity-awareness of the ZZB can address the multimodality-induced ambiguity in sensing, thereby yielding a reliable lower bound on the MSE, and iii) the proposed surrogate objective functions enable effective ZZB minimization with a lower computational complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 从Ziv-Zakai界（ZZB）的角度分析了夹捏天线系统（PASS）的感知能力，其动机源于PASS固有的多模态观测模型所导致的感知模糊性。与其他贝叶斯感知界相比，ZZB在广泛的信噪比（SNR）范围内提供了均方误差（MSE）的下界，并能够刻画似然函数中的模糊性。首先，针对一个上行感知场景建立了观测模型，该场景中单个感知目标向配备多个夹捏天线（PA）的单波导PASS接收机发送上行导频。在此基础上，推导了目标位置任意先验分布下的ZZB通用表达式，并分别特化为高斯分布和均匀分布的情况。其次，刻画了低SNR和高SNR下的渐近ZZB特性，并通过引入模糊函数的概念进一步研究了ZZB与传统贝叶斯克拉美-罗界（BCRB）之间的关系。此外，为降低ZZB直接计算的高复杂度，分别提出了无SNR和感知SNR的替代目标函数，以促进基于ZZB的感知性能优化。数值结果表明：i）与BCRB相比，ZZB在广泛的SNR范围内提供了紧致的感知性能下界；ii）ZZB的模糊感知能力能够处理由多模态引起的感知歧义，从而给出可靠的MSE下界；iii）所提出的替代目标函数能够以较低的计算复杂度实现有效的ZZB最小化。

</details>


### [230] [ADC-Aware End-to-End Optimization of a Dynamic Metasurface Antenna with Strong Mutual Coupling for Monostatic Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00253)
*Philipp del Hougne*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于强互耦动态超表面天线的单站感知流水线中，考虑模数转换器非理想性的端到端优化方法，实验表明ADC感知训练在低分辨率ADC场景下至关重要，且忽略互耦会导致性能降至随机猜测水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究通常假设理想的模数转换器（ADC）并依赖简化的电磁模型，忽略了实际系统中ADC非理想性和天线单元间强互耦对性能的影响。

Method: 基于互耦感知的多端口网络模型（参数通过实验标定获得），对带有96个1比特可编程元件的混沌腔体DMA进行ADC感知的端到端优化，联合优化DMA配置和数字分类器，可选地联合学习ADC决策阈值。

Result: 在1比特ADC和8个DMA配置下，理想ADC训练的系统部署均匀1比特ADC时测试准确率从95.5%降至56.0%；而ADC感知训练（相同均匀1比特ADC）达到87.2%；忽略互耦时性能降至随机猜测水平；学习非均匀ADC阈值相比固定均匀ADC仅有微小提升。

Conclusion: 在低分辨率ADC场景下，ADC感知训练是必要的；互耦建模同样不可或缺；学习非均匀ADC阈值带来的额外增益有限。

Original Abstract: Dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs) enable programmable wave-domain signal processing that can be jointly optimized with downstream digital processing in an end-to-end manner. Existing studies, however, typically assume ideal analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) and often rely on simplified electromagnetic models. Here, we study ADC-aware end-to-end optimization of a monostatic sensing pipeline based on a DMA with strong mutual coupling (MC). We model the wave domain using an MC-aware multiport-network model whose parameters were experimentally estimated for a fabricated chaotic-cavity-backed DMA with 96 one-bit-programmable meta-elements. We perform ADC-aware end-to-end optimization of the DMA configurations and digital classifier, either with awareness of a fixed uniform ADC or, optionally, with jointly learned ADC decision thresholds, and compare against baselines that assume an ideal ADC and/or ignore MC. Our results show that ADC awareness is essential in low-resolution ADC regimes: with one-bit ADCs and eight DMA configurations, deploying an ideal-ADC-trained system with a uniform one-bit ADC reduces the test accuracy from 95.5% to 56.0%, whereas ADC-aware training with the same fixed uniform one-bit ADC achieves 87.2%. We also show that without MC awareness the accuracy drops to the random-guess level. Learning non-uniform ADC thresholds provides at most modest additional gains over fixed uniform ADCs in the considered DMA-based sensing pipeline.

中文摘要翻译: 动态超表面天线（DMA）支持可编程的波域信号处理，可与下游数字处理以端到端方式联合优化。然而，现有研究通常假设理想的模数转换器（ADC），并依赖于简化的电磁模型。本文研究了基于具有强互耦（MC）的DMA的单站感知流水线的ADC感知端到端优化。我们使用MC感知的多端口网络模型对波域进行建模，该模型的参数通过实验对一款制造的混沌腔体背衬DMA（具有96个1比特可编程元元件）进行了标定。我们对DMA配置和数字分类器进行了ADC感知的端到端优化，要么感知固定均匀ADC，要么可选地联合学习ADC决策阈值，并与假设理想ADC和/或忽略MC的基线进行比较。结果表明，在低分辨率ADC场景下，ADC感知至关重要：使用1比特ADC和8个DMA配置，部署采用统一1比特ADC的理想ADC训练系统，测试准确率从95.5%降至56.0%，而采用相同固定统一1比特ADC的ADC感知训练达到了87.2%。我们还表明，如果没有MC感知，准确率会降至随机猜测水平。在所考虑的基于DMA的感知流水线中，学习非均匀ADC阈值相比于固定均匀ADC最多带来适度的额外增益。

</details>


### [231] [Evolving Intelligent Complex Systems via Intellicise Networks: Architecture, Technologies, and Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00316)
*Ping Zhang,Rui Meng,Xiaodong Xu,Song Gao,Zixuan Huang,Yaheng Wang,Yinqiu Liu,Ruichen Zhang,Yiming Liu,Kaiwen Yu,Yaping Sun,Han Meng,Haonan Tong,Huishi Song,Qianqian Yang,Shuoyao Wang,Lexi Xu,Qinghe Du,Geng Sun,Jiawen Kang,Gang Wu,Yiqing Zhou,Haixia Zhang,Zesong Fei,Aimin Hao,Ming Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从灵巧网络（Intellicise Networks）视角系统探索了未来智能复杂系统，提出了基于信息论、系统论、博弈论和控制论的跨域智能通信网络架构，包含跨层组织框架、多功能平面和新型信息流，并综述了从语义提取到具身智能体通信的关键使能技术。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 未来工程基础设施正演变为大规模、开放、异构、无线互连的复杂系统，面临网络资源利用优化、高维信息空间管理和多样化业务需求适配等重大挑战，亟需新的网络范式来应对这些问题。

Method: 提出了基于灵巧网络的跨域智能通信网络架构：① 跨层组织框架（感知→认知→决策的纵向演化）；② 多功能平面（控制、用户、数据、计算、智能、安全平面提供横向灵巧能力）；③ 新型信息流（数据流、知识流、模型流、任务流互联各层和平面，形成闭环）。该架构以信息论、系统论、博弈论和控制论为理论基础。

Result: 综述了关键使能技术的演进路径：从语义提取到意图理解、从异构资源集成到自配置与自优化、从生成式AI到智能体AI、从具身AI到共生AI；并给出了灵巧网络在具身智能体通信中的案例研究及代表性应用。

Conclusion: 灵巧网络以其意图驱动、语义原生和分布式智能的特性，为未来智能复杂系统提供了有前景的范式，所提架构和技术路径可有效支持大规模异构无线互连系统的智能化演进。

Original Abstract: Future engineering infrastructures are evolving into large-scale, open, heterogeneous, and wirelessly interconnected complex systems. These systems present significant challenges in optimizing network resource utilization, managing high-dimensional information spaces, and accommodating diverse business requirements. Intellicise networks, characterized by Intent-driven operation, semantic-native capability, and distributed intelligence, offer a promising paradigm for enabling such intelligent complex systems. We provide a systematic exploration of future intelligent complex systems from the perspective of intellicise networks. Specifically, we propose a cross-domain intelligent communication network architecture based on intellicise networks, grounded in information theory, systems theory, game theory, and cybernetics. The architecture comprises a cross-layer organizational framework, multi-functional planes, and novel information flows. The cross-layer framework defines the vertical evolution from perception and cognition to decision, while the control, user, data, computation, intelligence, and security planes deliver horizontal intellicise capabilities. Moreover, data, knowledge, model, and task flows interconnect the various layers and planes, forming a closed-loop process that derives simplicity from high-level intelligene while concurrently pursuing enhanced. Building on this architecture, we review key enabling technologies, tracing their evolution from semantic extraction to intent understanding, from heterogeneous resource integration to self-configuration and self-optimization, from generative artificial intelligence (AI) to agentic AI, and from embodied AI to symbodied AI. Additionally, we present a case study on intellicise networks for embodied agent communications and discuss representative applications and services for intelligent complex systems.

中文摘要翻译: 未来的工程基础设施正在演变为大规模、开放、异构且无线互连的复杂系统。这些系统在优化网络资源利用、管理高维信息空间以及适应多样化业务需求方面提出了重大挑战。灵巧网络（Intellicise Networks）以意图驱动（Intent-driven）运行、语义原生（semantic-native）能力和分布式智能为特征，为赋能此类智能复杂系统提供了一种有前景的范式。我们从灵巧网络的视角对未来智能复杂系统进行了系统性的探索。具体而言，我们提出了一种基于灵巧网络的跨域智能通信网络架构，该架构以信息论、系统论、博弈论和控制论为理论基础。该架构由跨层组织框架、多功能平面和新型信息流组成。跨层框架定义了从感知、认知到决策的纵向演化，而控制平面、用户平面、数据平面、计算平面、智能平面和安全平面则提供横向的灵巧能力。此外，数据流、知识流、模型流和任务流互联各个层和平面，形成一个闭环过程，该过程从高级智能中提取简洁性，同时追求增强的性能。基于此架构，我们回顾了关键使能技术，追溯了其从语义提取到意图理解、从异构资源集成到自配置与自优化、从生成式人工智能（AI）到智能体AI（agentic AI）、从具身AI（embodied AI）到共生AI（symbodied AI）的演进过程。此外，我们展示了灵巧网络在具身智能体通信中的案例研究，并讨论了面向智能复杂系统的代表性应用和服务。

</details>


### [232] [Semantic-based Internet of Embodied Intelligence: Visions and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00342)
*Yaheng Wang,Rui Meng,Xiaodong Xu,Yiming Liu,Feiliang Song,Linyuan Hu,Huishi Song,Lexi Xu,Tony Q. S. Quek,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于语义的具身智能物联网（SIoEI）范式，通过将语义信息作为统一度量贯穿智能体全生命周期，解决多智能体网络扩展中多模态数据传输开销大及逻辑推理与物理约束解耦的关键瓶颈，并在感知、智能、控制和通信四个维度阐释语义赋能的变革作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式AI和具身智能虽能实现自主智能体与物理世界交互，但将其扩展为多智能体网络（即具身智能物联网IoEI）面临两大瓶颈：海量多模态数据传输开销过大，以及逻辑推理与物理约束之间的解耦问题。亟需一种新的范式来解决这些规模化部署中的关键挑战。

Method: 提出基于语义的具身智能物联网（SIoEI）范式，将语义信息作为统一度量贯穿智能体全生命周期。系统定义了具身智能的四个关键维度（感知、智能、控制、通信），并详细阐述语义赋能如何革命性地改进环境感知、认知与任务规划、动作生成与鲁棒控制、以及通信与组网。

Result: 通过案例研究验证，语义赋能的端到端过程显著提升了信道鲁棒性，并降低了具身智能的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: SIoEI范式有望解决多智能体网络扩展的瓶颈问题，同时指出了该范式中关键的开放研究方向。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and embodied intelligence (EI) enable autonomous agents to interact with the physical world. However, scaling these systems into networks of multiple agents, namely the Internet of EI (IoEI), faces critical bottlenecks. These include the overhead of massive multimodal data transmission and the decoupling of logical reasoning from physical constraints. To address these challenges, we envision the Semantic-based IoEI (SIoEI), which leverages semantic information as a unified metric throughout the agent lifecycle. We systematically define four key dimensions of EI: perception, intelligence, control, and communication. We further elaborate how semantic empowerment revolutionizes environmental perception, cognition and task planning, action generation and robust control, and communication and networking. We also present a case study to verify that, the semantic-empowered end-to-end process significantly improves channel robustness and reduces end-to-end latency for EI. Finally, we outline critical open research directions for the SIoEI paradigm.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能（AI）和具身智能（EI）的最新进展使自主智能体能够与物理世界进行交互。然而，将这些系统扩展为多智能体网络，即具身智能物联网（IoEI），面临关键瓶颈，包括海量多模态数据传输的开销以及逻辑推理与物理约束的解耦问题。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了基于语义的具身智能物联网（SIoEI）范式，该范式将语义信息作为贯穿智能体全生命周期的统一度量。我们系统性地定义了具身智能的四个关键维度：感知、智能、控制与通信。进一步阐述了语义赋能如何在环境感知、认知与任务规划、动作生成与鲁棒控制、以及通信与组网方面带来革命性改进。我们还通过案例研究验证，语义赋能的端到端过程显著提升了信道鲁棒性并降低了具身智能的端到端延迟。最后，我们概述了SIoEI范式中关键的开放研究方向。

</details>


### [233] [B2X Networks: Joint Design of Communication and Control for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00537)
*Yuanwei Liu,Xu Gan,Zhaolin Wang,Chongjun Ouyang,Hao Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Kaibin Huang,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出脑-体到一切（B2X）网络概念，旨在融合无线网络与具身智能，设计了分布式和集中式两种架构，分别讨论了上行链路（面向事件紧迫性、感知体量和多体接入的状态获取）和下行链路（协调指令传输与常规业务）的重设计，并引入通信-控制帕累托边界刻画环路级传输性能与控制质量的权衡，最后指出若干开放研究问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前无线网络与具身智能的融合缺乏系统性框架，需要一种新的网络范式来有效整合智能推理、物理感知与周围生态系统之间的交互，以支撑脑-体协同的闭环通信与控制。

Method: 提出B2X网络概念，定义分布式和集中式两种脑架构；以基站侧大脑为典型场景，重新设计上行链路（事件紧迫性驱动的状态获取、感知体量管理、多体同时接入）和下行链路（指令传输与常规业务的资源协调），并利用通信-控制帕累托边界分析环路级性能权衡。

Result: 给出了B2X网络的上行和下行通信重设计方法，并通过通信-控制帕累托边界揭示了无线传输性能与控制质量之间的环路级权衡关系。

Conclusion: B2X网络为实现无线网络与具身智能的深度融合提供了系统化框架，明确了分布式与集中式大脑架构的设计选择，并指出上行/下行重设计中的关键权衡，最后提出了若干未来研究方向引导B2X网络的发展。

Original Abstract: This article proposes the concept of \emph{brain-body-to-everything (B2X)} networks to facilitate the integration of wireless networks and embodied intelligence. In this framework, the \emph{brain} refers to the intelligence functions for reasoning, planning, and decision-making, the \emph{body} denotes the physical embodied agent that senses and acts in the real world, and \emph{X} represents the surrounding ecosystem involved in the brain-body interaction loop. Two B2X architectures with \emph{distributed} and \emph{centralized} brains are introduced to characterize different placements of intelligence across the body, base station, and core network. The uplink and downlink designs of B2X networks are then discussed under a representative base-station-side brain setting. For the uplink, communication is redesigned for B2X state acquisition under event urgency, sensing volume, and simultaneous multi-body access. For the downlink, communication is redesigned to coordinate command delivery and conventional service under shared radio resources. Based on these uplink and downlink considerations, a communication-control Pareto boundary is further used to characterize the loop-level trade-off between wireless transmission performance and control quality in B2X networks. Finally, several open research problems are discussed to guide future B2X network design.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了“脑-体到一切（B2X）”网络概念，以促进无线网络与具身智能的融合。在该框架中，“脑”指代用于推理、规划和决策的智能功能，“体”指代在现实世界中感知和行动的物理具身智能体，而“X”代表参与脑-体交互回路的周围生态系统。本文引入了分布式脑和集中式脑两种B2X架构，以表征智能在身体、基站和核心网络之间的不同部署方式。随后，在典型基站侧大脑场景下讨论了B2X网络的上行链路和下行链路设计。对于上行链路，通信被重新设计以支持事件紧迫性、感知体量和多体同时接入条件下的B2X状态获取。对于下行链路，通信被重新设计以协调共享无线资源下的指令传输与常规业务。基于以上上下行链路的考量，本文进一步利用通信-控制帕累托边界来刻画B2X网络中无线传输性能与控制质量之间的环路级权衡。最后，讨论了若干开放研究问题，以指导未来B2X网络的设计。

</details>


### [234] [Measurement-Based Characterization and Statistical Modeling of 6G Urban Low-Altitude A2G Channels across FR1 and FR3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00541)
*Bin Ao,Jianhua Zhang,Pan Tang,Peijie Liu,Boyang He,Hao Zheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文在城市环境开展了2.85/4.6/7.25 GHz三个频段的宽带空地信道测量，提出了一种融合几何先验、信道特征与空间一致性约束的弱监督视距/非视距识别方法，分析了不同频段下路径损耗、阴影衰落、时延扩展和莱斯K因子等信道特性，发现高频信道稀疏性更强，且接收机移动会加剧信道的空间非平稳性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空地信道精确建模对低空通信系统设计与优化至关重要，但现有研究缺乏多频段、宽带宽的实际测量数据，且LoS/NLoS状态识别依赖人工标注或强监督方法，难以充分利用几何与空间信息。

Method: 在城市环境下使用250 MHz带宽在2.85、4.6、7.25 GHz三个频段进行宽带A2G信道测量；提出一种融合几何先验、信道特征与空间一致性约束的弱监督传播状态识别方法；基于实测数据提取并分析LoS/NLoS条件下的PL、SF、PDP、RMS-DS和Rician K-factor等信道特性。

Result: Close-in模型比3GPP参考模型更准确拟合测量PL；NLoS导致更大的路径损耗指数和阴影衰落；高频信道有效多径分量更少、时延色散更弱，LoS下平均RMS-DS从93.11 ns降至46.84 ns，平均Rician K-factor从9.16 dB升至12.88 dB；RMS-DS和Rician K-factor分别符合对数正态分布和正态分布；接收机移动加剧信道空间非平稳性。

Conclusion: 多频段实测数据验证了高频信道稀疏性增强的规律，弱监督识别方法有效利用了多源信息，所得统计模型可为6G空地一体化网络设计提供参考。

Original Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communications have been recognized as a key component of future sixth-generation (6G) space-air-ground-sea integrated networks. Accurate characterization and modeling of air-to-ground (A2G) channels are essential for the design and optimization of low-altitude communication systems. This paper presents a wideband A2G channel measurement campaign in an urban environment at 2.85 and 4.6~GHz in FR1 and 7.25~GHz in the FR3 frequency band, each with a bandwidth of 250~MHz. To enable reliable line-of-sight (LoS) and non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation state identification, a weakly supervised method is developed by fusing geometric priors, channel features, and spatial consistency constraints. Furthermore, based on the measured data, A2G channel characteristics are extracted and analyzed under LoS/NLoS conditions across different frequency bands, including path loss (PL), shadow fading (SF), power delay profile, root-mean-square delay spread (RMS-DS), and Rician $K$-factor. The results show that the close-in model fits the measured PL more accurately than the 3GPP reference model, and that NLoS propagation leads to larger path loss exponents and stronger SF than LoS propagation. For channel delay characteristics, higher-frequency channels exhibit fewer effective MPCs and weaker delay dispersion, indicating increased channel sparsity. Specifically, the mean RMS-DS under LoS conditions decreases from 93.11 to 46.84~ns, while the mean Rician $K$-factor increases from 9.16 to 12.88~dB. The statistical results further show that the RMS-DS and the Rician $K$-factor can be well characterized by lognormal and normal distributions, respectively. Moreover, the movement of the receiver in a complex scattering environment intensifies the spatial non-stationarity of the A2G channel.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机通信已被公认为未来第六代（6G）空天地海一体化网络的关键组成部分。空地信道的精确表征与建模对于低空通信系统的设计与优化至关重要。本文在城市环境下开展了2.85 GHz、4.6 GHz（FR1频段）和7.25 GHz（FR3频段）三个频段的宽带空地信道测量，每个频段带宽为250 MHz。为了实现可靠的视距（LoS）和非视距（NLoS）传播状态识别，本文提出了一种融合几何先验、信道特征和空间一致性约束的弱监督方法。进一步地，基于实测数据，提取并分析了不同频段下LoS/NLoS条件的空地信道特性，包括路径损耗（PL）、阴影衰落（SF）、功率时延谱、均方根时延扩展（RMS-DS）和莱斯K因子。结果表明，Close-in模型相比3GPP参考模型更准确地拟合了测量PL，且NLoS传播导致比LoS传播更大的路径损耗指数和更强的阴影衰落。在信道时延特性方面，高频信道的有效多径分量更少、时延色散更弱，表明信道稀疏性增强。具体而言，LoS条件下平均RMS-DS从93.11 ns降至46.84 ns，而平均莱斯K因子从9.16 dB升至12.88 dB。统计结果进一步表明，RMS-DS和莱斯K因子可分别由对数正态分布和正态分布很好地表征。此外，接收机在复杂散射环境中的运动会加剧空地信道的空间非平稳性。

</details>


### [235] [Frame-Based AFDM-ISAC Waveform Design With Chirp-Enabled Pulse Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00632)
*Qu Luo,Zilong Liu,Musavian Leila,Thomos Nikolaos,Qihao Peng,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿射频分复用（AFDM）的通感一体化（ISAC）设计，称为AFDM-ISAC，通过新型帧结构、模拟域感知接收、数字域感知融合算法以及低复杂度信道估计方案，实现了高效的通感融合。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通感一体化设计在利用仿射频分复用（AFDM）波形实现感知与通信融合方面研究不足，缺乏高效的帧结构设计和低复杂度的感知与信道估计方案，特别是在高移动性场景下。

Method: 1) 设计新型AFDM-ISAC帧结构，包含ISAC符号和纯数据符号，每个ISAC符号使用单个chirp子载波进行感知和信道估计；2) 提出模拟域感知接收机，利用本地chirp下混频实现chirp压缩增益，避免全双工硬件需求；3) 提出基于AFDM调制参数引导的感知融合算法；4) 提出基于广义复指数基扩展模型（GCE-BEM）的低复杂度信道估计方案及导频数据最优功率分配策略；5) 采用GCE-BEM-based卡尔曼滤波器实现帧内鲁棒信道估计。

Result: 所提出的AFDM-ISAC设计在感知和通信性能上均表现出优越性，实现了chirp压缩增益、低复杂度信道估计以及鲁棒的帧内信道跟踪。

Conclusion: AFDM-ISAC设计有效融合了感知与通信功能，通过创新的帧结构和信号处理算法，在高移动性场景下实现了高效、鲁棒的通感一体化性能。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes an Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM)-empowered integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) design, referred to as AFDM-ISAC. We first design a novel AFDM-ISAC frame structure that consists of both ISAC and pure data symbols. Each ISAC symbol consists of a single chirp subcarrier for both sensing and channel estimation, while the remaining subcarriers are allocated for communication. Building upon this structure, we present an analog-domain sensing receiver that down-mixes the received echo with a local chirp to fully exploit \textit{chirp compression} gains avoiding the need for full-duplex hardware. In addition, a sensing fusion algorithm, guided by AFDM modulation parameters, is further proposed in the digital domain. Leveraging the distinct features of the proposed AFDM-ISAC frame, we present a low-complexity channel estimation scheme for high mobility channels based on a generalized complex exponential basis expansion model (GCE-BEM), along with an optimal power allocation strategy between pilot and data symbols. Moreover, to support frame-based AFDM communications, a GCE-BEM-based Kalman filter is also employed for robust intra-frame channel estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于仿射频分复用（AFDM）的通感一体化（ISAC）设计，称为AFDM-ISAC。我们首先设计了一种新颖的AFDM-ISAC帧结构，该结构同时包含ISAC符号和纯数据符号。每个ISAC符号由单个chirp子载波构成，用于感知和信道估计，而其余子载波则分配给通信。基于该结构，我们提出了一种模拟域感知接收机，通过将接收到的回波与本地chirp进行下混频，充分利用chirp压缩增益，从而避免了全双工硬件的需求。此外，在数字域中进一步提出了一种由AFDM调制参数引导的感知融合算法。利用所提出的AFDM-ISAC帧的独特特性，我们提出了一种基于广义复指数基扩展模型（GCE-BEM）的高移动性信道低复杂度信道估计方案，以及导频符号与数据符号之间的最优功率分配策略。此外，为支持基于帧的AFDM通信，还采用了基于GCE-BEM的卡尔曼滤波器，用于实现鲁棒的帧内信道估计。

</details>


### [236] [Assessing Cardiac Dynamics through RF Sensing for Hemodynamic Monitoring in Pacemakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00791)
*A. Khaleghi,J. Bergsland,I. Balasingham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用心脏起搏器中的射频（RF）信道进行血流动力学监测，通过分析右心室与右心房之间以及皮下接收器的RF信号变化，发现RF信号与心脏节律紧密相关，可用于实时估计心腔容积、瓣膜行为和压力变化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索一种新型的、基于射频信号的实时心脏监测方法，以克服传统起搏器在血流动力学监测方面的局限性，为心脏起搏器系统提供更丰富的生理参数反馈。

Method: 分析心内收发器（右心室与右心房）以及皮下接收器之间的射频信号变化，研究其时序变化与心脏动力学的相关性，并建立RF信号与心腔容积、瓣膜行为和压力变化等参数的对应关系。

Result: 时序RF信号变化与心脏节律高度一致，能够用于估计心腔容积、瓣膜行为和压力变化等心功能参数。

Conclusion: 基于射频的传感技术有望成为起搏器系统中一种新颖的实时心脏监测方法。

Original Abstract: This paper examines the use of radiofrequency (RF) channels for hemodynamic monitoring in cardiac pacemakers. It analyzes RF signal variations between intracardiac transceivers in the right ventricle (RV) and right atrium (RA), as well as subcutaneous receivers, to determine their correlation with cardiac dynamics. The study shows that temporal RF signal variations closely align with cardiac rhythm, allowing for the estimation of parameters such as chamber volume, valve behavior, and pressure changes. These results underscore the potential of RF-based sensing as a novel method for real-time cardiac monitoring in pacemaker systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在心脏起搏器中利用射频（RF）信道进行血流动力学监测的方法。通过分析右心室（RV）与右心房（RA）内的心内收发器以及皮下接收器之间的RF信号变化，探讨了其与心脏动力学的相关性。研究表明，时序RF信号变化与心脏节律高度吻合，可用于估计心腔容积、瓣膜行为及压力变化等参数。这些结果凸显了基于射频的传感技术作为起搏器系统中实时心脏监测新方法的潜力。

</details>


### [237] [Meta-Transfer Learning for mmWave Beam Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00860)
*Ahmet Nuri Cevik,Sinem Coleri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出MTL-BA元迁移学习框架，通过冻结预训练卷积骨干网络并仅元学习轻量级Scale-and-Shift适配器和分类头，实现毫米波波束对齐，在参数量减少约17倍且元训练轮次减少60%的情况下，匹配全微调的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有元学习方法从头训练整个网络，导致更新参数多、元训练成本高；迁移学习虽限制网络部分更新，但未利用显式优化适应过程的元学习范式。需一种兼顾两者优势的低成本高效波束对齐方法。

Method: 提出MTL-BA框架：冻结预训练的卷积骨干网络，仅对轻量级Scale-and-Shift适配器和分类头进行元学习；从预训练模型热启动，限制适应范围至SS适配器和分类头，降低适应成本和元训练预算。

Result: 在DeepMIMO数据集上，MTL-BA在各种SNR水平下匹配全微调的精度和频谱效率，参数量减少约17倍（优于全微调和MAML），性能优于仅微调最后一层的方法，接近MAML但元训练轮次减少60%。

Conclusion: MTL-BA通过元迁移学习有效结合了元学习和迁移学习的优势，以极低参数量和训练成本实现了高性能的毫米波波束对齐，为下一代无线系统的波束预测提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) beam alignment plays a critical role in next-generation wireless systems, yet its efficient implementation remains challenging. Meta-learning and transfer learning have been explored to enable deep learning-based beam prediction models to rapidly adapt to unseen environments; however, existing meta-learning approaches adapt the entire network and are trained from random initialization, leading to a large number of updated parameters and a high meta-training cost, while transfer learning approaches restrict adaptation to part of the network but do not exploit episodic meta-learning, which explicitly trains the model over multiple tasks, to optimize the adaptation process itself. To overcome these limitations, we propose MTL-BA, a meta-transfer learning framework for beam alignment in millimeter-wave multiple-input single-output (MISO) systems that freezes a pre-trained convolutional backbone and meta-learns only lightweight Scale-and-Shift (SS) adapters together with a classifier head. Warm-starting from the pre-trained model and restricting adaptation to the SS adapters and classifier head reduce both the adaptation cost and the meta-training budget without sacrificing prediction performance. Simulation results on the DeepMIMO ray-tracing dataset show that MTL-BA matches the accuracy and spectral efficiency of full fine-tuning across various SNR levels despite updating approximately $17\times$ fewer parameters than both full fine-tuning and Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML), outperforms last-layer fine-tuning while updating a comparable number of parameters, and approaches MAML's performance while requiring $60\%$ fewer meta-training epochs.

中文摘要翻译: 毫米波波束对齐在下—代无线系统中扮演着关键角色，但其高效实现仍具挑战。元学习和迁移学习已被探索用于使基于深度学习的波束预测模型能快速适应未见过的环境；然而，现有的元学习方法对整个网络进行适应且从随机初始化开始训练，导致大量参数更新和高昂的元训练成本，而迁移学习方法虽将适应限制在部分网络，但未利用显式在多任务上训练模型以优化适应过程本身的片段式元学习。为克服这些局限，我们提出MTL-BA——一种用于毫米波多输入单输出系统的波束对齐元迁移学习框架，该框架冻结预训练的卷积骨干网络，仅对轻量级Scale-and-Shift适配器和分类头进行元学习。从预训练模型热启动并将适应限制在SS适配器和分类头，在降低适应成本和元训练预算的同时不牺牲预测性能。在DeepMIMO光线追踪数据集上的仿真结果表明，MTL-BA在各种信噪比水平下匹配全微调的精度和频谱效率，尽管更新参数比全微调和模型无关元学习少约17倍，性能优于更新参数数量相当的仅最后一层微调方法，且接近MAML的性能同时需要减少60%的元训练轮次。

</details>


### [238] [Lightweight Vision-Aided Beam Tracking for Cross-Environment mmWave Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00936)
*Mengyuan Ma,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Nhan Thanh Nguyen,A. Lee Swindlehurst,Markku Juntti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级视觉辅助波束跟踪模型，利用深度可分离卷积、层次化数据增强和波束功率标签平滑，在两个DeepSense 6G场景中实现了跨环境84%的波束预测准确率，同时参数量和计算复杂度分别降低约52倍和79倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 毫米波波束跟踪中，现有感知辅助方法面临信道快速变化和不同部署场景间环境差异大的挑战，难以在真实世界中推广；开发低复杂度、能泛化到多样环境的方案可以缓解这一问题。

Method: 将波束跟踪任务建模为序列到序列分类问题，从历史视觉观测联合预测当前和未来最优波束；采用深度可分离卷积构建轻量级模型，并提出层次化数据增强和波束功率标签平滑策略以提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在两个几何结构不同的DeepSense 6G真实场景中，所提策略在4个时隙（当前+未来3个）上跨环境波束预测准确率达到84%，优于现有最优方法；参数量和计算复杂度分别比高容量ResNet基线降低约52倍和79倍。

Conclusion: 轻量视觉辅助模型结合层次化数据增强与波束功率标签平滑，可在大幅降低计算开销的同时实现高精度跨环境波束跟踪，具备良好的实际部署潜力。

Original Abstract: Sensing-aided beam tracking is a promising approach to reduce the overhead for millimeter-wave beam management. However, real-world application remains challenging due to rapid channel variations and substantial environmental differences across deployment scenarios. Developing low-complexity sensing assisted approaches that generalize to diverse environments can alleviate the problem. With this motivation, this paper proposes a lightweight vision-aided model for cross-environment beam tracking. The task is formulated as a sequence-to-sequence classification problem, where the model jointly predicts the current and future optimal beams from past visual observations. We develop a low-complexity model based on depthwise separable convolutions and introduce hierarchical data augmentation and beam power-based label smoothing to improve robustness and generalization. Experimental results on real-world images from two geometrically distinct DeepSense 6G scenarios show that the proposed strategies consistently improve cross-environment beam prediction accuracy up to 84% across the current and three future time slots, outperforming the state-of-the-art solution. Notably, this performance is achieved while reducing the number of model parameters and computational complexity by factors of approximately 52 and 79, respectively, compared with the high-capacity ResNet baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 感知辅助的波束跟踪是减少毫米波波束管理开销的一种有前景的方法。然而，由于信道快速变化以及不同部署场景间的巨大环境差异，实际应用仍然具有挑战性。开发能够泛化到多样化环境的低复杂度感知辅助方法可以缓解这一问题。基于此动机，本文提出了一种用于跨环境波束跟踪的轻量级视觉辅助模型。该任务被建模为一个序列到序列分类问题，模型从过去视觉观测中联合预测当前和未来的最优波束。我们基于深度可分离卷积开发了一种低复杂度模型，并引入层次化数据增强和基于波束功率的标签平滑来提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。在两个几何结构不同的DeepSense 6G真实场景图像上的实验结果表明，所提策略在4个时隙（当前时隙及未来三个时隙）上持续地将跨环境波束预测准确率提升至84%，优于现有最优方案。值得注意的是，与高容量ResNet基线相比，该性能是在模型参数量和计算复杂度分别降低约52倍和79倍的情况下实现的。

</details>


### [239] [Channel Estimation and Beamforming for Microwave Linear Analog Computers (MiLACs)-Aided Multiuser MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00954)
*Qiaosen Zhang,Matteo Nerini,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对MiLAC辅助的多用户MISO系统，提出了一种低复杂度的信道估计与波束赋形方案，利用信道相关矩阵的秩亏特性在模拟域压缩信号，在数字域进行低维估计，并通过级联MiLAC实现R-ZFBF，大幅降低计算复杂度（信道估计达1540倍，波束赋形达16108倍），同时保持与数字基线相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MiLAC技术能大幅减少射频链数量和计算开销，但现有信道估计方法需要大量射频链和数字处理，损害了MiLAC系统的优势。因此需要为MiLAC辅助的多用户系统设计高效的、适配有限射频链的信道估计与波束赋形方案。

Method: 利用不同用户组信道相关矩阵的秩亏特性，通过MiLAC在模拟域对全维接收信号进行压缩，使其适配有限的射频链数量并保留关键信道信息；然后在数字域基于压缩观测进行低维信道估计；最后通过级联两个MiLAC实现正则化迫零波束成形（R-ZFBF），相较于单个MiLAC提供更高的计算灵活性。

Result: 所提方案在信道估计和波束赋形上分别实现了高达1540倍和16108倍的计算复杂度降低，同时性能与全数字基线方案相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于MiLAC的低维信道估计与级联波束赋形方案，能在大幅降低计算复杂度的前提下，实现与数字基线相近的性能，有效解决了MiLAC辅助系统中信道估计的开放问题。

Original Abstract: Microwave linear analog computers (MiLACs) have recently gained attention for future gigantic multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems by enabling beamforming with greatly reduced hardware and computational cost. However, channel estimation for MiLAC-aided multiuser systems remains an open problem. Conventional channel estimation requires many radio-frequency (RF) chains to access full-dimensional received signals, followed by massive digital processing, which undermines the advantages of MiLAC-aided systems in reducing the number of RF chains and computational complexity. In this paper, we propose computationally efficient channel estimation and beamforming schemes for MiLAC-aided multiuser multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) systems with a limited number of RF chains. We consider the general case where different user groups experience different channel correlation matrices. By exploiting the rank deficiency of these matrices, the proposed schemes use MiLAC to compress the full-dimensional received signals in the analog domain, making them compatible with the available RF chains while preserving the essential channel information. Then, in the digital domain, only low-dimensional channel estimation is performed based on these compressed observations, substantially reducing computational cost. We further show how regularized zero-forcing beamforming (R-ZFBF) can be efficiently realized from the low-dimensional channel estimates through a cascade of two MiLACs, which offers greater computational flexibility than a single MiLAC. Numerical results show that the proposed schemes reduce computational complexity up to $1540\times$ and $16108\times$, for channel estimation and beamforming, respectively, while achieving performance comparable to digital baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）因其能够以极低的硬件和计算开销实现波束赋形，在面向未来超大规模多输入多输出（MIMO）系统中受到了广泛关注。然而，MiLAC辅助的多用户系统中的信道估计仍是一个开放问题。传统的信道估计需要大量射频链来获取全维接收信号，随后进行大规模数字处理，这削弱了MiLAC辅助系统在减少射频链数量和计算复杂度方面的优势。本文针对射频链数量受限的MiLAC辅助多用户多输入单输出（MU-MISO）系统，提出了计算高效的信道估计与波束赋形方案。我们考虑了不同用户组经历不同信道相关矩阵的一般情形。通过利用这些矩阵的秩亏特性，所提方案使用MiLAC在模拟域压缩全维接收信号，使其适配可用射频链的同时保留关键信道信息。随后在数字域，仅基于这些压缩观测进行低维信道估计，大幅降低了计算开销。我们进一步展示了如何从低维信道估计出发，通过级联两个MiLAC高效实现正则化迫零波束赋形（R-ZFBF），相较于单个MiLAC提供了更大的计算灵活性。数值结果表明，所提方案在信道估计和波束赋形上分别实现了高达1540倍和16108倍的计算复杂度降低，同时取得了与数字基线相当的性能。

</details>


### [240] [Opportunistic Positioning with LEO Satellites based on SSB from NR NTN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00967)
*Rainer Bachl,Muhammad Nabeel,Tingting Lei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用NR NTN标准中的同步信号块（SSB）进行机会性接收机定位，无需修改标准，在Starlink星座仿真中实现平均定位误差小于10米。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 即将部署的低轨卫星网络（如Starlink的MSS）将采用NR NTN标准，该标准定义了周期性广播的SSB用于小区搜索和初始接入。作者希望利用这些现有信号进行机会性定位，而无需额外修改标准或增加专用定位信号。

Method: 基于SSB信号建模多普勒频移测量并推导伪距，同时考虑接收机时钟偏差和漂移。通过仅利用几何约束解决每颗卫星的伪距整数模糊度，无需星间差分或先验位置。从多颗卫星的SSB以及每颗卫星的多个时机进行测量，利用不同的传输定时和变化的传播延迟。基于实际Starlink星座和NR NTN标准构建仿真模型进行定位评估。

Result: 所提方法在不修改NR NTN标准的条件下，平均定位误差小于10米。

Conclusion: 利用NR NTN标准中已有的SSB信号，通过适当的多普勒测量和伪距建模，结合几何约束解决整数模糊度，可以在不改变标准的前提下实现高精度（<10m）的低轨卫星机会性定位。

Original Abstract: Forthcoming Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks such as Starlink's Mobile Satellite Service (MSS) will incorporate the New Radio (NR) Non-Terrestrial Network (NTN) standard. The Synchronization Signal Block (SSB) specified as part of NR is periodically broadcast for cell search and initial access. We propose to exploit the SSB for opportunistic receiver positioning. Doppler shift measurements are modeled and pseudoranges are derived from SSB while also taking into account the receiver's clock bias and drift. The resulting per satellite integer ambiguity in the pseudorange is resolved by geometry alone, without inter-satellite differencing or an a-priori position. Measurements are taken from SSBs of multiple satellites and at multiple occasions per satellite, whereby the SSBs are subject to different transmission timings and varying propagation delays. Finally, a simulation model is developed for positioning based on the actual Starlink constellation and the NR NTN standard to evaluate the positioning accuracy to be expected. The proposed approach achieves a mean positioning error of less than 10m without requiring any modification of the NR NTN standard.

中文摘要翻译: 即将部署的低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络（如Starlink的移动卫星服务MSS）将采用新无线电（NR）非地面网络（NTN）标准。作为NR标准的一部分，同步信号块（SSB）被周期性地广播，用于小区搜索和初始接入。本文提出利用SSB进行机会性接收机定位。我们对多普勒频移测量进行建模，并从SSB中推导出伪距，同时考虑了接收机的时钟偏差和漂移。由此产生的每颗卫星伪距中的整数模糊度仅通过几何约束即可解算，无需星间差分或先验位置。测量数据来自多颗卫星的SSB以及每颗卫星的多个时机，这些SSB受到不同的传输定时和变化的传播延迟的影响。最后，我们基于实际的Starlink星座和NR NTN标准开发了一个定位仿真模型，以评估预期可达到的定位精度。所提出的方法在不修改NR NTN标准的情况下，实现了平均定位误差小于10米。

</details>


### [241] [Low-Complexity Sensing-Aware PAPR Reduction for AFDM-based ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01064)
*Eya Gourar,Abdul Karim Gizzini,Yahia Medjahdi,Patrick Sondi,Laurent Clavier*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对AFDM波形在ISAC系统中高PAPR和距离旁瓣问题，提出了一种感知感知的chirp子载波预留（CSR）框架，在降低PAPR的同时提升了测距性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AFDM波形虽在高移动性环境中具有鲁棒性，但存在高峰均功率比（PAPR）问题，影响系统效率与弱目标检测性能。

Method: 提出感知感知的chirp子载波预留（CSR）框架，结合低复杂度梯度基PAPR最小化方法与随机局部搜索，利用AFDM自相关函数的相位敏感性抑制时延低模糊区（LAZ）旁瓣。

Result: 所提方案在实现显著PAPR降低的同时有效抑制了旁瓣，从而提升了弱目标检测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的CSR框架能够同时实现PAPR降低和距离旁瓣抑制，有效改善AFDM在ISAC系统中的弱目标检测能力。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a key technology for future wireless networks by enabling communication and environmental sensing through a common waveform and hardware platform. Among the candidate waveforms for ISAC, Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing (AFDM) had attracted significant attention due to its robustness in high-mobility environments, but it suffers from a high peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR). In this paper, we propose a sensing-aware chirp-subcarrier reservation (CSR) framework that reduces PAPR while improving ranging performance.
