The proposed method combines low-complexity gradient-based PAPR minimization with a randomized local search that exploits the phase sensitivity of the AFDM autocorrelation function to suppress delay low-ambiguity-zone (LAZ) sidelobes. Numerical results show that the proposed scheme achieves significant PAPR reduction together with significant sidelobe suppression, resulting in improved weak-target detection performance.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）已成为未来无线网络的关键技术，它通过共用波形和硬件平台实现通信与环境感知。在ISAC的候选波形中，仿射频率分复用（AFDM）因其在高移动性环境中的鲁棒性而备受关注，但其存在高峰均功率比（PAPR）问题。本文提出了一种感知感知的chirp子载波预留（CSR）框架，在降低PAPR的同时提升测距性能。所提方法将低复杂度的梯度基PAPR最小化与随机局部搜索相结合，利用AFDM自相关函数的相位敏感性来抑制时延低模糊区（LAZ）旁瓣。数值结果表明，所提方案在实现显著PAPR降低的同时有效抑制了旁瓣，从而提升了弱目标检测性能。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Polarimetric SAR Model Fitting for Soil Moisture Retrieval: Study of PALSAR-2 data over a Heterogeneous Mine Environment in Finland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00294)
*Oleg Antropov,Alireza Hamedianfar,Matthieu Molinier,Ulla Salmela,Hanna Kukkula,Lauri Seitsonen,Pauliina Liwata-Kenttälä,Maarit Middleton*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于ALOS-2 PALSAR-2四极化影像的多种模型驱动方法，用于提取芬兰东南部石灰岩采石场的表层土壤水分。通过提出一种基于极化相干矩阵[T3]的广义SAR时间序列TU Wien土壤水分指数（SMI）方法，结合半经验模型与机器学习基准，在复杂多沉积物矿区取得了最佳R²=0.67、RMSE=5.65 vol.%的检索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在复杂多沉积物矿区环境下，利用相对稀缺的参考数据，验证基于物理机制的半经验模型相比纯机器学习方法在表层土壤水分反演中的有效性和优势。

Method: 提出一种广义化的SAR时间序列TU Wien土壤水分指数（SMI）方法，在极化相干矩阵[T3]的多种表示空间（dB投影、线性、迹归一化）中拓展SMI检索；结合时间序列上下文信息（SMI）与当前观测的极化SAR参数（PolSAR），构建半经验模型；以通用机器学习模型为基准进行对比；并引入沉积物特异性校准。

Result: 最优半经验配置（时间上下文SMI+当前PolSAR参数）取得R²=0.67、RMSE=5.65 vol.%；最强的SMI_[T3]方法（沉积物特异性校准）取得R²=0.66、RMSE=5.67 vol.%，显著优于SMI_HH或SMI_VV；dB表示优于线性或迹归一化[T3]表示；引入沉积物信息大幅提升性能；机器学习结果接近但未超越半经验模型。

Conclusion: 在复杂多沉积物矿区且参考数据稀缺的条件下，基于物理机制的半经验模型在表层土壤水分反演中具有实用价值，且优于纯机器学习方法；沉积物特异性建模和时间序列信息对反演性能至关重要。

Original Abstract: This paper examines several model based approaches for retrieving surface soil moisture from ALOS-2 PALSAR-2 quad-pol imagery, over a lime stone quarry in southeastern Finland. The study primarily targets physically interpretable semi-empirical modeling approaches, with generic ML modeling used as a benchmark. Along with common polarimetric observables, we propose a generalization of the SAR time series based TU Wien soil moisture index (SMI) retrievals examined across several representational spaces derived from polarimetric coherency matrix $[T3]$. This study was conducted over a closed tailing storage facility and a landfill, with a set of 9 repeat pass PALSAR-2 images. The best semi-empirical configuration combining temporal context SMI and current observation PolSAR parameters achieved $R^2=0.67$ and RMSE $=5.65$ volumetric \% units. The strongest $SMI_{[T3]}$ approach with sediment-specific calibration, achieved $R^2=0.66$ and RMSE $=5.67$ vol. \%, which was considerably better than using $SMI_{HH}$ or $SMI_{VV}$. The proposed approach was sensitive to representations: dB-based projection outperformed linear or trace-normalized $[T3]$ representation. Factoring in sediment information dramatically improved retrieval performance compared to using global model fitting. Machine learning results closely approached but not outperformed semi-empirical model based methodologies. Similarly, they highlighted the need for sediment-specific modeling as well as the importance of including time-series/temporal backscatter dynamics during SSM retrieval. Our study demonstrated the utility of physics based SSM retrieval approaches in the complex multi-sediment mine environment under relatively scarce reference data conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了基于模型的多种方法，用于从ALOS-2 PALSAR-2四极化影像中检索芬兰东南部一处石灰岩采石场的表层土壤水分。研究主要针对具有物理可解释性的半经验建模方法，并以通用机器学习建模作为基准。除了常见的极化观测变量外，我们提出了一种广义化的SAR时间序列TU Wien土壤水分指数（SMI）检索方法，该方法在由极化相干矩阵[T3]导出的多种表示空间中进行评估。本研究在封闭尾矿储存设施和垃圾填埋场上进行，使用了9景重复轨道的PALSAR-2影像。最优的半经验配置结合了时间上下文SMI与当前观测的极化SAR参数（PolSAR），取得了R²=0.67和RMSE=5.65体积百分比单位的性能。经过沉积物特异性校准的最强SMI_[T3]方法取得了R²=0.66和RMSE=5.67 vol.%的性能，显著优于使用SMI_HH或SMI_VV。所提出的方法对表示空间敏感：基于dB的投影优于线性或迹归一化的[T3]表示。引入沉积物信息相比使用全局模型拟合显著提升了检索性能。机器学习结果接近但未超越基于半经验模型的方法。同样，机器学习结果也强调了沉积物特异性建模以及将时间序列/后向散射时间动态纳入表层土壤水分检索的重要性。我们的研究证明了在复杂多沉积物矿区且参考数据相对稀缺的条件下，基于物理机制的表层土壤水分反演方法的实用性。

</details>


### [243] [Enhancing Prostate Cancer Segmentation for Multi-Domain Generalization using a novel Parallel-Route Coherent Mixup Regularization Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00370)
*Josiah Simeth,Sudharsan Madhavan,Victoria Brennan,Sean McBride,James Janopaul-Naylor,Justin Haseltine,Daniel Gorovets,Himanshu Nagar,Neelam Tyagi,Harini Veeraraghavan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的并行路线混合训练方法（PaRC-mix），用于单源到多域泛化的前列腺癌病变分割，在两个深度网络上实现了显著的性能提升和泛化能力增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习分割方法在训练集之外的域上泛化能力有限，阻碍了MRgART的通用性和推广，而手动分割耗时且成本高昂。

Method: 提出并行路线混合训练（PaRC-mix），通过在多个网络层对不同训练样本的特征进行线性组合来创建特征增强，在两个深度残差网络（MRRN和UNet++）上实现。

Result: 在5个不同厂商和磁场强度的数据集上测试，PaRC-mix相比无混合和输入混合训练显著提升了肿瘤检测和分割准确性；侵袭性与非侵袭性病变的精度差距从21.1/19.5降至5.2/7.9。

Conclusion: 提出了一种易实现、网络无关的特征增强方法，可增强多流网络的泛化能力，解决前列腺癌病变分割的难题。

Original Abstract: MRI guided adaptive radiotherapy (MRgART) for prostate cancer (PCa) targets tumors while sparing organs from unnecessary radiation. Daily treatment adaptation requires accurate segmentation of tumors and organs. Manual delineation can be time and cost prohibitive. Deep learning segmentation methods have limited success applied to datasets distinct from training, hampering generalizability and adoption of MRgART. We develop a novel parallel route coherent mixup (PaRC-mix) training approach for single source to multi-domain generalization. PaRC-mix creates feature augmentations at multiple network layers through linear combination of features from different training samples in a batch. PaRC-mix training was implemented on two deep and residually connected networks, a multiple resolution residual network (MRRN) and UNet++ to segment PCa dominant intraprostatic lesions from apparent diffusion coefficient images. Models were trained on 2,029 samples from 3.0T GE MRI and tested on 1,547 PCa samples from 5 datasets acquired using 3T Siemens, 3T Philips, and 1.5T Elekta Unity MR-Linac scanners. PaRC-mix training led to significantly more accurate tumor detection and segmentation for both networks compared to training without mixup as well as input-mix training. PaRC-mix also achieved better recall to precision tradeoff than mixup applied only on the network backbone or input-mixup. Using a normalized composite DSC, HD95, and MSD score the accuracy gap between aggressive and non-aggressive lesions decreased from 21.1 and 19.5 for MRRN and UNet++ models trained without mixup to 5.2 and 7.9 with same models trained with PaRC-mix. This paper presents an easy to implement network agnostic approach to feature augmentation in multi-stream networks that enhances generalizability for the difficult problem of prostate cancer lesion segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 针对前列腺癌的MRI引导自适应放疗（MRgART）能够在靶向肿瘤的同时保护器官免受不必要的辐射。每日治疗适应需要精确分割肿瘤和器官。手动勾画耗时且成本高昂。深度学习分割方法在应用于与训练集不同的数据集时效果有限，阻碍了MRgART的泛化性和推广。我们提出了一种新颖的并行路线混合（PaRC-mix）训练方法，用于单源到多域泛化。PaRC-mix通过在多个网络层对批次中不同训练样本的特征进行线性组合，创建特征增强。PaRC-mix训练在两个深度残差连接网络——多分辨率残差网络（MRRN）和UNet++上实现，用于从表观扩散系数图像中分割前列腺癌主要前列腺内病变。模型在来自3.0T GE MRI的2,029个样本上训练，并在来自5个数据集、使用3T Siemens、3T Philips和1.5T Elekta Unity MR-Linac扫描仪采集的1,547个前列腺癌样本上测试。与无混合训练及输入混合训练相比，PaRC-mix训练使两个网络在肿瘤检测和分割上均获得了显著更高的准确性。与仅在网络主干应用混合或输入混合相比，PaRC-mix还实现了更好的精确率与召回率权衡。使用归一化复合DSC、HD95和MSD评分，侵袭性与非侵袭性病变之间的精度差距从无混合训练的MRRN和UNet++模型的21.1和19.5分别降至使用PaRC-mix训练的相同模型的5.2和7.9。本文提出了一种易于实现、网络无关的多流网络特征增强方法，增强了针对前列腺癌病变分割这一难题的泛化能力。

</details>


### [244] [MalariAI: A Label-Resilient Decoupled Framework for Universal Cell Segmentation and Explainable Stage Classification in Dense Malaria Blood Smears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00385)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md Hasibul Hasan,Mohammed Ali,Tanzilur Rahman*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MalariAI提出了一个两阶段解耦框架，通过距离变换引导的分水岭算法和EfficientNet-B0分类器，解决了疟疾自动诊断中标注不完整、密集细胞区域检测抑制和缺乏单细胞空间证据三大问题，实现了75.95%的细胞召回率和98.36%的分类准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习系统在疟疾自动诊断中存在三个关键缺陷：1）端到端检测器将未标注细胞视为背景，导致召回率受标注完整性影响；2）非极大值抑制在密集涂片区域会抑制有效检测；3）现有全切片检测管线缺乏每个细胞的空间证据供临床审核。这些缺陷阻碍了系统在资源有限环境中的可靠部署。

Method: 提出MalariAI两阶段解耦框架：阶段一采用标注无关的距离变换引导分水岭算法，在全尺寸1600×1200血涂片图像中隔离每个细胞，无需任何真实标注输入；阶段二在64×64裁剪图上使用带焦点损失（gamma=2.0，按类逆频率加权）的EfficientNet-B0进行微调，同时对每个检测到的细胞生成Grad-CAM++热力图提供实例级空间证据。

Result: 在NIH BBBC041数据集（120张图像）上：阶段一通过质心定位恢复了75.95%的真实细胞；阶段二整体分类准确率达98.36%，对罕见的分裂体和配子体阶段的每类准确率分别达87.5%和75.0%，远超Faster R-CNN基线（仅24.57%和25.95% AP）。

Conclusion: MalariAI通过解耦检测与分类、引入标注无关的细胞分割算法以及实例级可解释性热力图，有效克服了现有深度学习系统在疟疾诊断中的三大失败模式，在不牺牲分类性能的前提下为临床审核提供了每细胞空间证据。

Original Abstract: Automated malaria diagnosis from blood smear microscopy is a critical challenge in global health AI; in resource-limited settings, the scarcity of expert microscopists remains the primary bottleneck to timely and accurate diagnosis. Three compounding failure modes prevent reliable clinical deployment of existing deep learning systems. First, end-to-end detectors treat unannotated cells as background during training, producing recall figures that are strongly influenced by annotation completeness rather than reflecting true cell recovery. Second, Non-Maximum Suppression tends to suppress valid detections in dense smear regions where infection counts matter most. Third, existing whole-slide detection pipelines lack per-cell spatial evidence for clinical audit, despite image-level explainability methods such as Grad-CAM having been applied to malaria image classification tasks. We present MalariAI, a two-stage decoupled framework that addresses all three failure modes in a unified pipeline. Stage 1 applies an annotation-agnostic distance-transform guided watershed algorithm to isolate every cell in a full 1600x1200 blood smear image, recovering 75.95% of ground-truth cells by centroid localisation across the 120-image NIH BBBC041 test set without any ground-truth input. Stage 2 fine-tunes EfficientNet-B0 with Focal Loss (gamma = 2.0, per-class inverse-frequency weights) on 64x64 crops, achieving 98.36% overall classification accuracy with 87.5% and 75.0% per-class accuracy on the rare schizont and gametocyte stages, compared to only 24.57% and 25.95% AP for a Faster R-CNN baseline on the same classes. Grad-CAM++ heatmaps generated per detected cell provide instance-level spatial evidence for clinical audit, enabling microscopists to verify model predictions at the individual parasite level without sacrificing classification performance.

中文摘要翻译: 基于血涂片显微镜检查的疟疾自动诊断是全球健康人工智能领域的一项关键挑战；在资源有限的环境中，专家显微镜检查人员的稀缺仍然是实现及时准确诊断的主要瓶颈。三个复合失效模式阻碍了现有深度学习系统在临床中的可靠部署。首先，端到端检测器在训练过程中将未标注细胞视为背景，产生的召回率数值受标注完整性影响很大，而非反映真实的细胞恢复情况。其次，非极大值抑制倾向于在感染计数最为重要的密集涂片区域抑制有效检测。第三，尽管Grad-CAM等图像级可解释性方法已应用于疟疾图像分类任务，但现有全切片检测管线缺乏用于临床审核的每细胞空间证据。我们提出了MalariAI，一个两阶段解耦框架，在统一管线中解决了所有三种失效模式。阶段一应用与标注无关的距离变换引导分水岭算法，在全尺寸1600×1200血涂片图像中隔离每个细胞，在120张图像的NIH BBBC041测试集上通过质心定位恢复了75.95%的真实细胞，无需任何真实标注输入。阶段二在64×64裁剪图上使用带焦点损失（gamma=2.0，按类逆频率权重）的EfficientNet-B0进行微调，实现了98.36%的整体分类准确率，对罕见的分裂体期和配子体期的每类准确率分别达到87.5%和75.0%，而Faster R-CNN基线在同一类别上的AP仅为24.57%和25.95%。对每个检测到的细胞生成的Grad-CAM++热力图提供了实例级空间证据供临床审核，使显微镜检查人员能够在个体寄生虫层面验证模型预测，且不牺牲分类性能。

</details>


### [245] [Predicting Lethal Outcome (Cause) And Understanding Key Biomarkers Linked With Acute Myocardial Infarction Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00472)
*Sagnik Ghosh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合多种机器学习模型和人工神经网络的自动脉检测方法，用于预测急性心肌梗死致命结局并识别关键生物标志物，旨在实现更快、更准、更廉价的临床诊断。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 急性心肌梗死是全球主要死因之一，现有诊断依赖医生经验和病史回顾，耗时且不一致；早期溶栓治疗可改善预后，因此亟需更快速、准确的自动化诊断模型。

Method: 数据预处理（缺失值填充、类别不平衡处理采用SVMSMOTE/ADASYN/类权重法），包装法与嵌入法特征选择，特征缩放，集成模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、Light-GBM、Bagging SVM），并通过人工神经网络进一步提升精度。

Result: 使用精确率、召回率等关键指标评估所有模型，以确定最适合临床应用的方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化模型有望使MI诊断更清晰、快速且经济，帮助患者自我管理并预防致命事件。

Original Abstract: Cardiovascular disease is still one of the main causes of death around the world. Acute myocardial infarction (MI), or heart attack, claims millions of lives each year. MI happens when blood flow to the coronary arteries is blocked or reduced, which causes permanent damage to the heart muscle. Without treatment, this can lead to cardiac arrest, where the heart stops pumping blood to the organs, resulting in organ failure and death. Even survivors often face serious problems like heart failure, pulmonary edema, and asystole. Research shows that 5 to 10 percent of survivors die within the first year after an MI, and nearly half need to be hospitalized again. Early thrombolytic treatment leads to better outcomes, so there is a clear need for faster and more accurate ways to diagnose MI. Right now, doctors usually review patient history and use their own experience to find the causes of MI. This process takes a lot of time and can be inconsistent. Detecting MI accurately and quickly can help patients take better care of themselves and prevent fatal events. In this study, we introduce an automated model to predict deadly outcomes of MI and help doctors understand important biomarkers linked to its complications. This approach aims to make diagnosis clearer, faster, and more affordable. The process includes preparing the data, filling in missing values, and handling imbalanced data using SVMSMOTE, ADASYN, and class-weighted methods. We use wrapper and embedded feature selection to find the most important variables, then scale the features for consistency. The model combines Logistic Regression, Random Forest, Light-GBM, and Bagging SVM, and is further improved with an artificial neural network to increase accuracy. We evaluate all models using precision, recall, and other key measures to find the best option for clinical use.

中文摘要翻译: 心血管疾病仍然是全球主要死因之一。急性心肌梗死（MI），也称心脏病发作，每年夺走数百万人的生命。当冠状动脉血流受阻或减少时，就会发生MI，对心肌造成永久性损伤。若不及时治疗，可导致心脏骤停，心脏停止向器官泵血，引发器官衰竭和死亡。即使幸存下来，患者也常面临心力衰竭、肺水肿和无收缩等严重问题。研究表明，5%至10%的幸存者在MI后第一年内死亡，近半数需要再次住院。早期溶栓治疗可改善预后，因此迫切需要更快、更准确的MI诊断方法。目前，医生通常通过回顾病史并结合自身经验来查找MI原因，这一过程耗时且不稳定。准确快速地检测MI有助于患者更好地进行自我管理并预防致命事件。在本研究中，我们引入了一种自动化模型来预测MI的致命结局，并帮助医生理解与其并发症相关的重要生物标志物。该方法旨在使诊断更清晰、更快速且更经济。流程包括数据准备、缺失值填充以及使用SVMSMOTE、ADASYN和类权重方法处理不平衡数据。我们采用包装法和嵌入法特征选择来筛选最重要的变量，随后对特征进行归一化处理。该模型结合了逻辑回归、随机森林、Light-GBM和Bagging SVM，并通过人工神经网络进一步提升精度。我们使用精确率、召回率等关键指标评估所有模型，以确定最适合临床应用的方案。

</details>


### [246] [Image-Domain Tilt Constrained Distributed Fusion for Maneuvering UAV Tracking with Multi-Camera Electro-Optical Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01008)
*Minxing Sun,Yao Mao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种图像域倾斜约束的分布式融合方法，利用旋翼目标在图像中的表观横滚和俯仰作为机动线索，通过YOLO-OBB检测器提供在线目标位置和倾斜测量，在分布式融合中引入横滚/俯仰伪观测约束加速度，显著提升短时预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光电无人机跟踪中，短时预测对目标机动敏感，现有的图像中心、视线和距离测量对加速度约束较弱，导致激进机动时预测滞后，需要利用图像域中更直接的机动线索来增强加速度估计。

Method: 提出图像域倾斜约束的分布式融合方法：1) 利用弱先验自动标注流程从同步视频、云台IMU和无人机IMU生成定向边界框和图像域倾斜标签；2) 训练YOLO-OBB检测器提供在线目标位置和倾斜测量；3) 将图像域横滚和俯仰作为加速度相关的伪观测引入状态估计；4) 在分布式跟踪中异步融合一个移动云台相机和两个固定地面相机，增广相机姿态误差状态以吸收外参漂移和系统不一致性；5) 采用马氏距离门控结合协方差扩宽处理漏检和虚假检测。

Result: 仿真中，加入横滚/俯仰观测使预测RMSE从1.991 m降至0.821 m，累积预测误差降低60.75%；实测试验中，自一致性评估显示累积预测误差降低18.10%。

Conclusion: 图像域倾斜能够为目标加速度提供有效的约束，显著提升机动无人机短时预测的鲁棒性和精度。

Original Abstract: Short-horizon prediction is essential for electro-optical UAV tracking, especially when the target is small, maneuvering, or intermittently observed. Image center, line-of-sight, and range measurements provide direct constraints on target position, but their constraints on acceleration are weak. As a result, prediction can lag during aggressive maneuvers.
  This paper proposes an image-domain tilt constrained distributed fusion method for maneuvering UAV tracking. The method uses the apparent roll and pitch of a rotorcraft target in the image as low-level maneuver cues. A weak-prior auto-labeling pipeline first generates oriented bounding box and image-domain tilt labels from synchronized video, gimbal IMU, and UAV IMU data. A YOLO-OBB detector is then trained to provide online target position and tilt measurements. The front-end Python implementation is publicly available at github.com/ShineMinxing/PythonYOLO.
  In the fusion stage, the UAV state is modeled by position, velocity, and acceleration. Image-domain roll and pitch are introduced as acceleration-related pseudo-observations. For distributed tracking, one mobile gimbal camera and two fixed ground cameras are fused asynchronously. Camera attitude error states are augmented into the filter to absorb extrinsic drift and cross-camera systematic inconsistency. A Mahalanobis gate with time-since-last-valid covariance widening is used to reject false detections and handle dropouts.
  In simulation, adding roll/pitch observations reduces the prediction RMSE from 1.991 m to 0.821 m and decreases the cumulative prediction error by 60.75\%. In real distributed experiments, a self-consistency evaluation shows an 18.10\% reduction in cumulative prediction error. The results show that image-domain tilt can provide useful acceleration constraints for robust short-horizon UAV prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 短时预测对于光电无人机跟踪至关重要，尤其当目标体积小、机动性强或间歇性观测时。图像中心、视线和距离测量为目标位置提供了直接约束，但对加速度的约束较弱，导致在激进机动过程中预测可能滞后。本文提出一种面向机动无人机跟踪的图像域倾斜约束分布式融合方法。该方法利用旋翼目标在图像中的表观横滚和俯仰作为低层机动线索。首先通过弱先验自动标注流程，从同步视频、云台IMU和无人机IMU数据生成定向边界框和图像域倾斜标签；随后训练YOLO-OBB检测器以提供在线目标位置和倾斜测量。前端Python实现已在github.com/ShineMinxing/PythonYOLO公开。在融合阶段，无人机状态由位置、速度和加速度建模，将图像域横滚和俯仰作为加速度相关的伪观测引入。对于分布式跟踪，融合一个移动云台相机和两个固定地面相机，采用异步融合方式。将相机姿态误差状态增广到滤波器中，以吸收外参漂移和跨相机系统不一致性。采用马氏距离门控结合自上次有效观测以来的协方差扩宽机制，以拒绝虚假检测并处理观测丢失。仿真结果表明，加入横滚/俯仰观测后，预测RMSE从1.991 m降至0.821 m，累积预测误差降低60.75%。在实际分布式实验中，自一致性评估显示累积预测误差降低18.10%。结果表明，图像域倾斜能够为目标加速度提供有效的约束，从而实现鲁棒的无人机短时预测。

</details>


### [247] [Group-invariant Coresets for Data-efficient Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01089)
*L. C. Ayres,J. C. M. Bermudez,S. J. M. de Almeida,R. A. Borsoi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: GRINCO是一种群不变核集框架，通过在变换群诱导的商空间中进行样本选择，避免对同一实例的不同变换版本重复标注，从而提升标签效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准的核集方法忽略了已知的数据对称性，可能对同一实例的不同变换版本重复查询，浪费标注预算。

Method: 提出GRINCO框架，在变换群诱导的商空间中执行核集选择（即在轨道上选择而非原始样本），使用规范代表元或学习的轨道分离不变嵌入定义商空间度量，结合商空间k中心选择与轨道平均损失的群不变训练。

Result: 在合成尺度不变数据和带有旋转冗余的图像基准测试上，GRINCO相比传统核集基线方法提升了轨道覆盖率，并在群诱导冗余显著时表现出更强的标签效率。

Conclusion: GRINCO通过在商空间中操作，有效利用数据对称性，显著降低了标注成本，特别是在存在群诱导冗余的场景下优于传统方法。

Original Abstract: Active learning reduces labeling cost by querying the most informative unlabeled samples, but standard coreset methods ignore known data symmetries and can waste budget on transformed versions of the same instance. We propose GRINCO, a group-invariant coreset framework that performs acquisition in the quotient space induced by a transformation group, so that selection operates on orbits rather than raw samples. The method uses either canonical representatives or learned orbit-separating invariant embeddings to define practical quotient metrics, and combines quotient-space k-center selection with invariant training through an orbit-averaged loss. We further derive a generalization bound that relates excess orbit-averaged risk to quotient-space coverage, label uncertainty, and intra-orbit variability. Experiments on synthetic scale-invariant data and image benchmarks with rotation-induced redundancy show that GRINCO improves orbit coverage and achieves stronger label efficiency than conventional coreset baselines, especially when group-induced redundancy is substantial.

中文摘要翻译: 主动学习通过查询信息量最大的未标记样本来降低标注成本，但标准的核集方法忽略了已知的数据对称性，可能会将预算浪费在同一实例的不同变换版本上。我们提出GRINCO，一种群不变核集框架，该框架在变换群诱导的商空间中进行样本获取，使得选择操作在轨道上而非原始样本上进行。该方法使用规范代表元或学习到的轨道分离不变嵌入来定义实用的商空间度量，并将商空间k中心选择与通过轨道平均损失实现的群不变训练相结合。我们进一步推导了一个泛化界，将超额轨道平均风险与商空间覆盖、标签不确定性和轨道内变异性联系起来。在合成尺度不变数据和带有旋转冗余的图像基准测试上的实验表明，GRINCO改善了轨道覆盖，并实现了比传统核集基线更强的标签效率，特别是在群诱导冗余显著的情况下。

</details>

### [248] [NeuroCogMap Reveals Cognitive Organization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00397)
*Zhongxiang Sun,Haolang Lu,Qiang Ma,Qi Li,Qipeng Wang,Liang Pang,Chenyu Liu,Qiankun Li,Hao Sun,Kun Wang,Yi Zeng,Jun Xu,Guoqi Li,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroCogMap——一个受认知神经科学启发的框架，将LLM内部特征组织为功能分区，并链接到可解释功能、认知能力和认知层级。研究发现这些分区稳定、语义连贯且跨模型部分保守，与模型输出功能相关；LLM主要失败模式（幻觉、偏见、拒绝失败、谄媚）对应不同的表征和行为控制系统扰动。NeuroCogMap还能改进对人类自然语言理解中皮层反应的预测，并揭示指导人类决策模型优化的潜在策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管LLM展现出广泛的类认知行为，但其内部表征是否形成可复现的功能系统来解释行为、失败以及与人脑认知的联系仍不清楚。现有研究缺乏一个系统级的认知神经科学框架来组织LLM的内部特征并将其与可解释功能、认知能力和人类认知相关联。

Method: 提出NeuroCogMap框架，受认知神经科学启发，将LLM的内部特征组织成功能分区（functional parcels），并将这些分区链接到可解释功能、认知能力和认知层级结构。通过分析这些分区的稳定性、语义连贯性、跨模型保守性以及与模型输出的功能关联来验证框架有效性。

Result: 1) 分区形成稳定且语义连贯的组织，部分跨模型保守且与模型输出功能相关；2) 幻觉、偏见、拒绝失败和谄媚等LLM失败对应不同表征和行为控制系统的扰动，提供内部特征用于检测和干预；3) NeuroCogMap改进了对人类自然语言理解中皮层反应的预测，最强对应在高阶联合皮层；4) 内部特征揭示了指导经典人类决策模型优化的潜在策略。

Conclusion: NeuroCogMap作为一个系统级框架，能够有效映射人工系统的功能组织，并将这种组织与人类皮层功能和认知行为联系起来，为理解LLM的认知基础及其与生物认知的关系提供了新的视角。

Original Abstract: Understanding how complex cognitive functions are organized within artificial systems is central to interpreting large language models (LLMs) and relating them to biological cognition. Yet although LLMs exhibit broad cognitive-like behaviours, it remains unclear whether their internal representations form reproducible functional systems that explain behaviour, failure and links to human cognition. Here we present NeuroCogMap, a cognitive neuroscience-inspired framework that organizes internal features of LLMs into functional parcels and links them to interpretable functions, cognitive capabilities and a cognitive hierarchy. These parcels form a stable and semantically coherent organization that is partly conserved across models and functionally linked to model outputs. Within this organization, major LLM failures, including hallucination, bias, refusal failure and sycophancy, correspond to distinct disruptions in representational and behavioural-control systems, yielding internal signatures for mechanism-guided detection and targeted intervention. Beyond model behaviour, NeuroCogMap improves prediction of human cortical responses during naturalistic language comprehension, with the strongest correspondence in higher-order association cortex. At the cognitive level, its internal signatures expose latent strategies that guide refinements of classical models of human decision-making. Together, these findings establish NeuroCogMap as a system-level framework for mapping functional organization in artificial systems and for relating this organization to human cortical function and cognitive behaviour.

中文摘要翻译: 理解复杂认知功能如何在人工系统中组织，对于解释大型语言模型（LLMs）并将其与生物认知联系起来至关重要。然而，尽管LLMs表现出广泛的类认知行为，其内部表征是否形成可复现的功能系统来解释行为、失败以及与人类认知的联系仍不清楚。本文提出了NeuroCogMap——一个受认知神经科学启发的框架，它将LLM的内部特征组织成功能分区，并将其与可解释功能、认知能力和认知层级联系起来。这些分区形成稳定且语义连贯的组织，部分跨模型保守，并与模型输出在功能上相关。在这一组织内，主要的LLM失败模式——包括幻觉、偏见、拒绝失败和谄媚——对应着表征和行为控制系统中不同的扰动，从而为机制引导的检测和针对性干预提供了内部特征。超越模型行为层面，NeuroCogMap改进了对自然语言理解过程中人类皮层反应的预测，最强对应出现在高阶联合皮层。在认知层面，其内部特征揭示了潜在的策略，指导了对经典人类决策模型的改进。综上，这些发现确立了NeuroCogMap作为一个系统级框架，用于映射人工系统的功能组织，并将这种组织与人类皮层功能和认知行为联系起来。

</details>


### [249] [DRIADA: A Python Toolkit for Cross-Scale Analysis of Single-Neuron Selectivity and Population Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00851)
*Nikita Pospelov,Viktor Plusnin,Olga Rogozhnikova,Anna Ivanova,Vladimir Sotskov,Margarita Orobets,Ksenia Toropova,Olga Ivashkina,Vladik Avetisov,Konstantin Anokhin*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: DRIADA 是一个开源 Python 框架，统一了不同尺度的神经信号和行为数据，支持跨层次（单神经元、群体、网络）分析，并在合成数据、海马钙成像和环形吸引子网络上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前神经科学工具各自针对单一范式和互不兼容的数据格式，难以处理跨层次（单神经元、群体、网络）的神经编码问题。研究者需要统一的框架来整合不同尺度的神经信号和行为数据。

Method: DRIADA 是一个开源 Python 框架，采用共享数据模型统一神经信号和时间对齐的行为数据，将选择性检验、降维和网络分析集成在同一工作流中。

Result: 1) 在海马钙成像数据中，基于选择性的滤波从全神经元嵌入中恢复了二维空间嵌入；2) 反向分析显示约57%对主导流形维度有信息的神经元对11个行为特征均无选择性；3) 在环形吸引子基准上，四个独立模块恢复了预期的拓扑结构。

Conclusion: DRIADA 使得跨尺度分析在钙成像、尖峰序列和模拟网络中成为常规操作，有效连接了不同层次的神经信号分析。

Original Abstract: Brain activity spans single-neuron, population, and network levels, and core questions in neural coding require moving between them. Yet current tools target a single paradigm and incompatible data formats, leaving cross-level questions hard to address. We present DRIADA, an open-source Python framework that unifies neural signals and time-aligned behavior in a shared data model, so selectivity testing, dimensionality reduction, and network analysis operate within a unified workflow. We evaluate it on synthetic data with known ground truth, hippocampal calcium imaging from 13~mice in an open field, and a simulated toroidal attractor network. In the hippocampal data, selectivity-based filtering restored a two-dimensional spatial embedding from a collapsed all-neuron embedding, while reverse analysis showed that ${\sim}57\%$ of neurons informative about leading manifold dimensions were not selective to any of the 11 measured behavioral features. On the toroidal benchmark, four independent modules recovered the expected topology. DRIADA makes cross-scale analysis routine across calcium imaging, spike trains, and simulated networks.

中文摘要翻译: 脑活动跨越单神经元、群体和网络三个层次，神经编码的核心问题需要在这几个层次之间进行切换。然而，当前的工具各自针对单一范式且数据格式互不兼容，使得跨层次问题难以解决。我们提出了 DRIADA，这是一个开源 Python 框架，它通过共享数据模型统一了神经信号和时间对齐的行为数据，从而使选择性检验、降维和网络分析能够在统一的工作流中运行。我们在具有已知真实标签的合成数据、13只小鼠在开放场中的海马钙成像数据以及模拟环形吸引子网络上对其进行了评估。在海马数据中，基于选择性的滤波从崩溃的全神经元嵌入中恢复了二维空间嵌入，而反向分析表明，约57%对主导流形维度有信息的神经元对11个测量的行为特征均无选择性。在环形吸引子基准测试中，四个独立模块恢复了预期的拓扑结构。DRIADA 使得跨尺度分析在钙成像、尖峰序列和模拟网络中成为常规操作。

</details>
